# TOTAL MOBILIZATION — Living Roadmap & Model Handoff

**This file is the single source of truth for the ongoing work. Update it EVERY turn and commit it, so nothing is ever forgotten.** It also serves as the send-off / onboarding doc for the next model taking over (the designer is moving the expensive framework builds to **Fable 5**).

**Bank DESIGN DISCUSSIONS and concepts here, not just task lists.** Conversation memory gets compacted; ideas that were discussed but not written here have been lost before. If the designer talks through a concept — even a rejected or someday one — it gets a line in this file.

---

## 📦 SHIPPED STATUS (2026-10-24)

**Current version: `1.3.6`** (diagnostic) on the integration branch `worktree-levy-stardust`. **Levy ships as a `.tmod` from this branch — it does NOT merge to `main`.** `main` belongs to the OTHER project in this repo: `title.html`, the TOTAL MOBILIZATION HTML game (another session, now v4.6). Levy coexists in the same repo on its own branch; the integration branch is its source of truth and the `.tmod` is the deliverable. (Earlier notes here said "holding for a main merge" — that was a misread of the HTML-game's deploy directive; there is no Levy→main step. If the designer ever wants Levy folded into the repo main, that's a deliberate, coordinated merge — ROADMAP.md/CLAUDE.md/title.html would conflict hard — not an auto-deploy.)

**✅ The old HARD-HANG is fixed** (designer: Frost Moon wave 20, ~6-8k points, big army, "mowing through every boss" — the exact Ice-Queen+Pumpking-mid-moon scenario that used to lock up). The 1.3.3 perf/minion-doorway fix WORKS for that class.

**⚠️ BUT A DIFFERENT FREEZE IS OPEN (designer, 2026-10-24): Pumpkin Moon froze.** Env: **Master Mode** (GameMode 2), world "The Distressing Brook", heavy modpack (LuiAFK, MercenaryContracts, DeliveryGirl, Census, Mechanica, BossChecklist…). Client.log (`c0c9339c`) analysis: autosaves fire on schedule (19:40/19:50/20:00) right up to the log's end at 20:00:40 — so the game ran HEALTHY for 30 min, then a SUDDEN stop. **The `Levy watchdog:` line NEVER fired**, which is the key tell: the 1.3.3 watchdog only instruments UPDATE phases 1-7; a stall in **DRAW/render runs with Phase 0 and was invisible to it.** Set-bonus log lines are `First()`-deduped (once per world), NOT a per-hit flood — ruled out. 1.3.5 added map-marker DRAW code (runs every frame, minimap always on) — a prime draw-path suspect the old watchdog couldn't see.
- **1.3.6 (diagnostic, shipped 2026-10-24):** `HangWatchdog.cs` now has a **per-frame heartbeat** (`Beat()` in `PostDrawInterface`) + a watchdog check that catches a stall ANYWHERE in the main loop — update OR draw — at a 3s threshold (vs the old update-only 5s). Crucially, a frame-hang report with `Phase 0` tells us "the stall is OUTSIDE Levy's update — draw/render, another mod, or vanilla," so the next repro's log pinpoints update-vs-draw-vs-other. Boots clean v1.3.6. **NEXT: designer reproduces the Pumpkin Moon freeze → read the new `Levy watchdog:` line.** Open questions to her: hard-lock vs slideshow? big map (M) open? what was happening right at the freeze? army size? using the new sky-drop weapons?
- `BossesTargetSoldiers` default-ON is DEFERRED again until this new freeze is understood (don't add enemy load mid-investigation).

**🐛 BANKED BUGS (found while the freeze took priority — return to these):**
- **Soldiers exceed player HP/mana caps** (designer screenshot: Sgt. Luda 940 HP / 300 mana). TWO things: (1) **By design** — the progression guides raise caps: Firepower doubles base HP 100→200 & mana 20→100; Healthcare raises HP cap 500→600; **The Arcane Arts raises mana cap 200→300** (so 300 mana = working as intended IF Arcane studied). **DESIGN QUESTION for designer:** should the guides exceed the player caps (600/300) at all, or hard-cap soldiers at 500/200? (2) **REAL BUG** — 940 HP exceeds even the mod's own `MaxSoldierHp=600` ceiling. `record.MaxHp` is hard-capped ≤600 and every soldier `lifeMax` write = `record.MaxHp`; the officer path (`town.lifeMax + AvatarBonusHp=150`) is the only >600 path but Luda is a REGULAR (Promote/Demote/Reserve buttons; officers are a separate panel). ROOT CAUSE NOT YET FOUND — env is Master Mode (GameMode 2); difficulty scaling leaking onto the friendly soldier `lifeMax` is the leading hypothesis but Bind overrides lifeMax post-SetDefaults so it's unconfirmed. Needs a focused trace (and/or asking the designer: which guides studied? is it ALL soldiers or some?).
- **Empress of Light won't target levies** (designer). The Empress (NPC 636, aiStyle special — "cannot hurt while ai[0] is 0 or 10" per `SoldierNPC.Damage.cs:24`) isn't engaging soldiers. Likely a boss-targeting allowlist / `BossesTargetSoldiers`-gate gap or her unusual AI not picking soldier targets. Group with the Empress + True Eyes + boss-targeting pass.

## ✅ 1.3.5 (shipped 2026-10-24) — sky-drop weapons + demote/demobilize + map markers (3 parallel tracks, merged clean)
- **Sky-drop weapons** (`levy-skydrop`, table-driven refactor): Star Wrath now **calls down stars** (was flinging them sideways from the soldier); same for Starfury, Blizzard Staff, Meteor Staff, Lunar Flare, Daedalus Stormbow, Blood Rain Bow. Found 6/7 already existed ad-hoc + 3 bugs (open-sky tested above the soldier not the target → a roof broke it; roofed Meteor fired invisible scale-0 meteors; no target leading). Unified into `SkyDrop.cs` + `LevyConstants.SkyDrop.cs` table. Verified no-regression for the other ~100 weapons (`TryFire`/`HeldAim` no-op off-table). Rain works under a roof (starts above the TARGET) and over walls (thin-ray path beats `CanHitLine`); stars/flares fly through ground. Caveats: bows still need LOS to *decide* to fire; Star Wrath can be high-volume at army scale (no cap, tenet #1).
- **Demote + demobilize** (`levy-roster`): **Demote** lowers a grade (keeps name/veterancy/gear; spare accessories slide or return to armory). **Demobilize → reserve** benches a soldier whole (keeps everything, frees the command-point slot, no wage, rested to full by dawn); **Recall** brings him back beside you. Reserve cap 100 (packet-size safety, not balance). Discharge moved to the card's "More" page. Save-compatible, MP-safe. Fixes folded in at merge: dye rides with a slid accessory on demote; `FollowSlotIndex` skips reserve.
- **Map markers** (`levy-minimap`): army (grouped azure pips + count on minimap/overlay, individual on fullscreen), officers (gold rings on their town-NPC head icon), bosses (pulsing red rings/discs). Proper boss de-dupe (Moon Lord→1 on core, Eater of Worlds→1 centroid, Destroyer→segment mean). Replaces the old one-dot-per-soldier layer (deleted at merge). Uses the supported `ModMapLayer` hook; agent render-verified off-game. Calibration constants (`PlayerHeadHalfPx`) are code-guesses → eyeball in game.

**1.3.2 (CRASH HOTFIX, shipped 2026-10-23):** fixed a hard FREEZE when a soldier's held-weapon beam (Last Prism etc.) lands the killing blow on a **boss**. Root cause: `HeldWeaponSwap.RunDamage` put the soldier's dummy into `Main.player[255]` + `Main.myPlayer=255` around `Projectile.Damage`, so the whole boss-death cascade (checkDead→NPCLoot, every mod's OnKill, event/boss-log bookkeeping) ran on a **fake player** and hung. Fix: resolve the beam's damage/kill as the REAL player (`p.owner = SoldierMinions.TrueMyPlayer`, what `WeaponUse.OwnerFor` gives every normal shot) for the one Damage call, restored to 255 in `finally`; + a thread-static re-entrancy guard on the AI/Damage detours. Damage numbers unchanged (chain projectiles are `npcProj`, so owner-derived terms are gated out). New in 1.3.1 because soldiers only started landing boss-kills with held beams then. Boots clean; **awaiting designer confirmation the freeze is gone** (can't be reproduced headless).
- RESIDUAL, banked: **(a) minion/summon soldiers have the SAME latent hazard** — `SoldierMinions.RunUpdate` runs the whole Projectile.Update (incl. Damage) under the dummy-in-255 swap; a minion landing a boss-kill could freeze the same way — NOT yet fixed → FOLLOW-UP (1.3.3): give driven minions a Damage-phase detour mirroring the held-weapon fix. **(b)** the fix moves the Last Prism beam's owner-wide hit-immunity slot (type 632 uses `npc.immune[owner]`, not local) from 255 to the real player, so a mass-Prism army now shares a per-target hit-throttle with other PIERCING soldier shots (single-shot guns/arrows unaffected; minions no longer throttled) — optional ~35-line immunity-isolation patch exists at `/tmp/claude-0/-home-user-total-mobilization/9fbf7c25-c34e-53c7-98cd-36a181fc49d3/scratchpad/levy-optB/optional-immunity-isolation.patch` if the designer notices DPS loss. **(c)** bonus: Solar Eruption now actually works for soldiers in single-player (was silently a no-op).

(3) `TESTING.md:105` stale.

## ✅ 1.3.3 (shipped 2026-10-23 PM) — crash build + wave-1 fixes [AWAITING designer's freeze verdict]

Test build shipped. **Corrected crash diagnosis (supersedes the 1.3.2 AND the first perf theory):** the measured freeze cost was a **~85ms/tick pathfinding thrash** — soldiers flip-flopping between two unreachable flying targets (a boss + its escort), re-running the full firing-spot search every think (`FindEngageSpot`, ~5ms each). Pumpking only spawns **2 untargetable** scythes (no NPC swarm — that theory was wrong); Ice Queen's frost storm is a real projectile flood. Fixes in 1.3.3:
- **Perf:** 3-slot unreachable-memory ring (stops the A/B thrash) + per-tick search-time budget (85ms→10ms worst tick, measured) + per-tick cached never-target + friendly early-outs. `SoldierTargeting.cs`, `SoldierNPC.Brain.cs`, `SoldierMinions.cs`, `SoldierProjectileTag.cs`, `LevyConstants.Targeting.cs`.
- **Minion freeze doorway (the other candidate):** a driven minion's kill cascade now resolves as the REAL player via an `On_NPC.StrikeNPC` interposer that lifts the swap ONLY for the strike (`SoldierMinions.cs` + `HeldWeaponSwap.cs`) — closes the hang class 1.3.2 closed for beams, WITHOUT the beam-style re-own that would've nerfed minion owner-dependent damage (Desert Tiger/Abigail gem scaling, Stardust Dragon segment cooldowns, hit-immunity slot).
- **Hang watchdog** (`Common/Util/HangWatchdog.cs`): logs `Levy watchdog:` with the stuck phase/NPC/projectile/soldier if a tick exceeds 5s — **the diagnostic if it STILL freezes.**
- **Flat economy:** `TierLevyWage` → {1000,1200,1400,1700,2000,2400} (was 10× spread).
- **Lifecycle:** militia stays mustered while a boss lives (no demobilize on death/teleport); regulars keep orders on death; unpaid officers go off-duty/suspend (commission + slot kept) instead of auto-unhiring.

Crash cause still UNCONFIRMED in-game (can't repro headless). `levy-crashdiag` branch kept (unmerged stack-trace probe) as a fallback if the watchdog isn't enough. **If it still freezes: get the designer's client.log, grep `Levy watchdog:` / `Levy CRASH-DIAG`.**

**WAVE 2 IN FLIGHT:** set bonuses (`levy-setbonus`: Nebula visible, Spectre verify, Stardust Guardian) + Moon Lord targeting (`levy-aggrofix`: True Eyes allowlist via BossParts, enemy-spread dead-code fix). **WAVE 3 QUEUED:** soldier positioning (seek vantage for kitless, Follow override — held until perf is designer-verified, since it also touches Brain.cs), demote/demobilize, pylon recruiting, minimap marker, Star Wrath sky-drop class. Officer-kit rebuilds still DEFERRED.

## 🐛 BIG BUG BATCH (2026-10-23 PM) — designer braindump, mandate "knock it all out, don't ask every time"

**⚠ CRASH — REAL diagnosis (SUPERSEDES the 1.3.2 theory; 1.3.2 fixed the WRONG thing):** NOT the targeting swap, NOT boss death. Verified vs decompiled source: **none of the 5 moon minibosses are boss-classified** by Levy (no `boss`/`ShouldBeCountedAsBoss`/MainBosses), so the slot-255 swap is **inert** for Pumpking/Ice Queen and there's no boss-death handler special to them. The freeze is a **quadratic per-tick PERF collapse**: every soldier runs an O(Main.maxNPCs) target scan + `Collision.CanHitLine` LOS **every tick** (`SoldierTargeting.PickTarget` ~:51-63, LOS :61; from `SoldierNPC.Brain.TryEngage` :682 + a wasted 2nd pass :691). Pumpking floods `Main.npc` with scythe-NPCs (PumpkingBlade 328, dontCountMe, uncapped); Ice Queen floods `Main.projectile` with frost (inflating per-minion scans `SoldierMinions.cs` :168/276/430/550/574, `WeaponUse.cs:464`, `SoldierProjectileTag.cs:264`); both FLY (unreachable → degenerate rescans) + highest HP (longest fight → biggest army). Cost peaks at the finale = "froze the moment it died." **Fix = PERF (tenet #1, keep the army): throttle+stagger per-soldier target & per-minion projectile scans, skip scythe NPCs as targets, stagger mass-retarget-on-death.** [WAVE 1 IN FLIGHT: `levy-crashperf`]. Config caveat: if designer added aiStyle 58/60 to `ExtraTargetingAiStyles`, that would flip those two into the swap path — confirm live config.

**ECONOMY balloon — root:** `TierLevyWage` (LevyConstants.cs:61) = 10× boss-progress step table {1000,2500,4000,6000,8000,10000}; EVERY cost is a multiple of it → whole economy inflates 10× on boss kills (wages, hazard pay, muster L/5, recruit-drive 2L, officer wage 2L / fee 10L, enlist ×5). **Fix = flatten the table to ~2× total** [WAVE 1: `levy-econlife`].

**LIFECYCLE** [WAVE 1: `levy-econlife`]: (a) militia auto-demobilize on death/teleport — `SettlementSystem.PostUpdateInvasions` stands down on falling edge of `IsBossActive`, which needs a LIVING player within 250 tiles of the boss (`LevyUtil.IsBossActive` :89) → death/teleport flips it off while boss lives; `OnCommanderDied`→ReturnToBarracks also retreats regulars every death. Fix = key off boss-existing not player-proximity (+grace); don't retreat while boss/event active. (b) officers self-unhire — `PayOfficers` Releases("Lapsed") after 2 unpaid dawns (tier-inflated wages drain treasury). Fix = non-destructive suspend-until-paid, never auto-dismiss.

**SET BONUSES** [WAVE 2]: soldier dummy (255) never runs Player.Update; vanilla reads Main.player[owner]=commander (no soldier armor) → mod re-implements per-dummy via `SoldierProjectileTag.OnHitNPC`→`SoldierNPC.OnProjectileHit` (ArmorSets.cs:252). Nebula = IMPLEMENTED but **invisible** (absorb-only, no pickup) + magic-gated; Spectre Mask/Hood = IMPLEMENTED, magic-gated + skip boss-class enemies (canGhostHeal); **Stardust Guardian = genuinely NOT implemented** (vanilla spawn gated to real player; dummy deliberately excluded via whoAmI=253). Fix = make Nebula visibly proc, verify/force Spectre, build a soldier Stardust Guardian.

**MOON LORD / TARGETING AI** [WAVE 2/3]: True Eyes (MoonLordFreeEye 400, aiStyle 81) NEVER allowlisted (no boss flag/realLife, absent from BossParts) → always chase player; fix = add MoonLordFreeEye→MoonLordCore to `LevyConstants.BossParts` (read eye ai[3]). Core/head/hands ARE swap-eligible but gated behind config `BossesTargetSoldiers` **default FALSE** + first-kill-solo → consider defaulting ON. Enemies clump: `EnemyTargeting.Compete` spread is ±120px jitter only; `ArmyPull*` spread is DEAD CODE (`vanillaIsPlayer` declared false :200, never set) → fix the flag + add an already-targeted load term. Positioning: high-ground/LOS logic exists but gated on FLIGHT KITS (`SoldierNPC.Brain` UpdateAltitudeIntent :760 / TryAerialPursuit :820 / AirPerchFeasible :1252); kitless soldiers never climb to GAIN a firing line → add seek-vantage search (hook FindEngageSpot/TryPerch). Trickle-to-floor: unreachable airborne target dropped (TryEngage :734-737) + order→Idle/ReturnHome (OrderState :379-384) → keep engaging while boss active. Follow ignored: TryEngage runs before orders (Brain :233-236), Follow is a leash (WithinChaseBounds :632) not an override → fresh Follow suppresses combat.

**FEATURES** [WAVE 3]: demote + demobilize (keep soldier, free slot); pylon network recruiting (owed); minimap marker for army/boss; Star Wrath + sky-drop weapon class (Blizzard Staff/Daedalus Stormbow/Meteor) spawn from above target (`WeaponUse.cs:793`). **Noted:** Solar Eruption is fine per designer (scratch the "broken" claim). Officer-kit rebuilds (earlier plan) = DEFERRED behind this batch.

- **Mechanical guides (post-1.0):** Mechanization (mounts), the Chlorophyte expedition — still PAUSED. **Engineering = NOW ACTIVE (designer pulled it forward 2026-10-24) → see COMBAT DRONES below.**

### 🛩 GUIDE TO ENGINEERING = COMBAT DRONES (active design, 2026-10-24 — discussing, NOT yet building)
The designer's turret idea, converged this session. **Core = armory-mounted weapon platforms with healthbars** (the "original idea," designer's words):
- **They MOUNT your Armory weapons** (the Last-Prism-turret dream), NOT a fixed cannon. Reuse the soldier WeaponUse/Armory pipeline, not `SummonedUnitNPC`'s fixed-projectile `Fire()`. ← the real new work.
- **Only cost = the weapon each one carries** (ties up one armory weapon). NO IC / manpower / command-points / upkeep / hard cap. "i don't want them to cost the player anything beside a weapon." The weapon IS the limiter (as many drones as you have spare weapons).
- **Healthbars** (reuse `SummonedUnitNPC`'s healthbar/ITargetBody + enemies-target + Nurse-heals — that part is DONE). Destructible, rebuildable, medic-sustainable.
- **DRONES, not static emplacements** (solves the designer's aesthetic worry: "not sure you could design nice looking turrets if fixed"). Hovering machines — reuse a clean vanilla drone/probe/UFO sprite; reads as automated warfare, fits the theme. Static ground-turret variant = optional later.
- **Weapon types = RANGED + MAGIC only.** Melee / summon / whips stay on SOLDIERS (mobile wielders use them properly). Don't force every type onto a drone — the drone's identity is a ranged/magic platform. (Designer landed here too: "it'd only really make sense to use ranged or magic weapons.")
- **PROGRESSION — the key insight:** because a drone mounts YOUR weapon, it AUTO-SCALES (early weapon = weak drone, Last Prism = devastating drone). So **unlock EARLY** (lean: hitting hardmode, or even pre-hardmode) and it stays relevant the whole game. This dissolves the designer's "post-Martian = not enough time to enjoy them" worry. Engineering = an early, easy-to-get unlock, NOT a late tier gate. The weapon tiering does the balancing.
- **Placement = low/no micro:** auto-garrison around any barracks (zero micro, fortress fantasy) + a **banner item** anchor for forward positions (semi-auto — her idea; drop a banner, drones build up there). The drone auto-pulls the best ranged/magic weapon from the linked Armory (zero weapon-micro too).
- **OPEN forks for the designer:** (1) drones-led aesthetic OK, or does she also want ground turrets? (2) exact unlock gate (early-hardmode vs pre-hardmode). (3) weapon on a destroyed drone — returns to armory (safe, lean) vs drops (riskier).
- **Infra reality:** `SummonedUnitNPC` gives healthbar+targeting+Nurse-heal for free, but fires a FIXED projectile — the new work is giving the drone a weapon-from-armory path (port/adapt soldier `WeaponUse`) + a settlement/banner anchor+lifecycle (vs the current officer-owner binding) + the Engineering guide unlock.

## 🎖 OFFICER RECONCILIATION (2026-10-23) — rebuild the kits to the designer's ACTUAL specs

**The problem (designer, this session):** the 12 shipped officer kits DRIFTED from the specific ideas the designer gave (voice-dump + brainstorm, chat ~2026-10-18). They are NOT "bad" — the designer never said so — they were *genericized into weapon/aura/summon archetypes*, losing the flavor. Standing self-correction: STOP capitulating / stop calling the work bad; it's a fidelity gap, fix it.

**No visual-clutter problem.** "Takes up the screen with three of them" was a mix-up over a buff-above-head display that was never proposed — nothing to thin (the mod draws no buff icons on units). The ONE real visual bug: the officer avatar draw is broken for ALL town NPCs (hand-rolled sprite) → fixed in wave 1.

**Designer's ACTUAL officer specs (SOURCE OF TRUTH):**
- **Guide** — accuracy; same-weapon soldiers *fire in sync (a volley at once)*; +morale. Flourish idea: he's the WoF, if he dies everyone opens fire. [BUILT as enemy-marker → REBUILD]
- **Arms Dealer** — *accuracy* (ammo guy → bottomless/upgraded ammo). "Accuracy" isn't native → reduced spread + truer shots. [BUILT as rapid bullets + an **Archery** aura — Archery buffs BOWS, backwards → REBUILD]
- **Merchant** — mobile fence: sell to him on the march at a better-than-max-happiness price; +coins/+loot; hands out ammo/basics + potions; morale lift; throws knives; own dialogue; optional item-storage while traveling. [BUILT as coin-gun + ⅛-wage treasury trickle → REBUILD]
- **Goblin Tinkerer** — reforge weapons better *while you move* (reuse bulk reforge-to-floor). [BUILT as +dmg/crit/def aura + spiky balls → RE-HOOK]
- **Princess** — a *morale boost* (expensive). [BUILT as 4-buff aura → COLLAPSE to one Morale buff]
- **Dryad** — *Dryad's Blessing* on the army (follow-to-buff). [BUILT as Bane+thorns → ADD Blessing]
- **Painter** — use his *paint gun* on enemies. [BUILT ~right + bolted-on Wrath aura → drop the aura]
- **Nurse** — pay to follow + heal from treasury. [BUILT ~right; still owes bug fixes: stops self-healing, follows player not troops, odd syringes, dawn-unhire]
- **Angler** — Well Fed buff. [BUILT right — leave]
- **Demolitionist** — fire support (grenades, scale mid-game); nearby regulars occasionally lob grenades. [BUILT ~right; add the regulars touch]
- **Witch Doctor** — venom debuff on a share of army hits. [NOT BUILT]
- **Summoners (archetype MATCHES spec; all summons carry health):** Pirate = pirate soldiers + cannon turrets; Clothier = skeleton squad on the fly; Zoologist = summon monsters; Wizard = enchanted swords + magical creatures; Cyborg = Spelunker-effect drones (+ he sells nanites, not the Steampunker). [only Pirate's crew built]
- **Parked/optional:** Tavernkeep (fear-resist but tipsy/worse-aim), town pets (small bonus, outside slots), Steampunker (unresolved).
- **INVENTED by the old plan, NOT designer specs → CUT unless re-justified:** Tax Collector (built; coin-bounty folds into Merchant), Mechanic, Truffle.

**Reuse hooks (confirmed by code read):** accuracy → extend `WeaponUse.AimErrorMultiplier` (WeaponUse.cs:568); bottomless ammo → officer flag in `WeaponUse.ConsumesAmmo` (:579); reforge → extract per-item loop from `ArmoryTE.ReforgeToFloor` (:1238) via public `PrefixQuality`/`IsReforgeable`/`ReforgePrice`; coin/loot → `GlobalNPC.OnKill` like `TaxBountyGlobalNPC`; morale → one `MoraleBuff : ModBuff` (copy `MarkedBuff`) + a case in `ApplyBuffEffect` (SoldierNPC.Damage.cs:202); aura-collapse → `AuraKit.BuffTypes={MoraleBuff}`; network recruiting → relax `OfficerSystem.CanCommission` (:182-183) + reuse `SettlementSystem.NearPylon`/`NetworkedArmories`. **Volley-sync** and **Merchant sell-on-the-march** are the only NET-NEW systems.

**Avatar-draw fix (wave 1):** stop suppressing the parked town NPC's vanilla draw (`OfficerGlobalNPC.PreDraw:128` → return true), make the avatar body invisible, delete hand-rolled `Officer_Draw`/`Officer_AdvanceWalkFrame`; vanilla renders the co-located town NPC (correct frames/hats/packs free), avatar keeps its nameplate.

**Weapon-picker fix (wave 1, GREENLIT "fix the whole class"):** soldiers bench the Last Prism for a Bubble Gun because `Score` (WeaponClassifier.cs:519) rates ramping channel-beams on listed damage. Last Prism = HeldRealCadence 12, ramp uncredited → ~500; Bubble Gun = 3 bubbles → ~900. Fix via `ScoreShotDamageMult` (LevyConstants.Weapons.cs:139) / `HeldRealCadence` so Moon-Lord weapons out-rank novelties; sanity-check Vortex Beater/Solar/Phantasm.

**Build waves:** (1) avatar-draw + weapon-score [SHIPPED 1.3.1 test build 2026-10-23, awaiting in-game confirm]; (2) rebuild drifted kits (Arms Dealer, Guide, Merchant t1, Goblin) + MoraleBuff/aura-collapse; (3) pylon recruiting; (4) Nurse bug-fix; (5) Merchant t2 + Witch Doctor + invented-officer cuts; (6) summoners to spec. Full plan: `/root/.claude/plans/wiggly-orbiting-pond.md`.

**Milestone (2026-10-23):** the designer's levy army fought **Moon Lord** — the whole fantasy (conscripted townsfolk taking the final boss unmicroed) working in a real playtest. 🔥

## ⭐ LEVY MOD NORTH STAR (designer, v0.9.7): "these emulate real players as much as possible"

When a soldier's stats / gear / consumables diverge from how a REAL Terraria player works, the default answer is **make it match vanilla** unless there's a strong reason not to. This settled the HP question (grade no longer caps HP: 15 Life Crystals → 400, Life Fruit → 500, same for every grade, exactly like a player). Use it to settle future "why does X behave weirdly" reports. (This is the Levy-mod guiding principle; the tenet below governs the separate title.html game.)

**⭐ WEAPON-COMPATIBILITY RULE (designer, standing): ALWAYS try to make an incompatible weapon WORK — marking it Unsupported is the LAST resort, not the default.** When a weapon breaks on soldiers (owner-anchored projectile, control-scheme mismatch, channel, etc.), the first move is a compatible path: route it through a Levy-owned projectile (SoldierBoomerang / SoldierThrust / a drive-it-ourselves PreAI like the Flying Knife) so it behaves right without colliding with the commander's ownership. Benching it is only acceptable when there is genuinely no sane way to make it fight. (This corrected the v0.9.12 instinct of marking Mechanica's Serpent's Spear Unsupported — v0.9.15 routes it through the boomerang path instead.) Applies to every agent dispatched for weapon work.

---

## 🌙 1.0 OVERNIGHT PUSH (designer 2026-10-19: "go ham, use up my weekly limit, finish everything, this is 1.0")

**KEY REFRAME (told the designer):** compatibility is GENERIC — the classifier routes by weapon CLASS/BEHAVIOR, not per item. So we do NOT catalogue every Calamity/Thorium item; we make each weapon FAMILY + system bulletproof, and every mod's weapons of that family work for free. (Also: can't reach the designer's PC Calamity files from the cloud; deep custom-class work needs the .tmod uploaded like Mechanica was.)

**NEW STANDING DESIGN RULES (designer, this session):**
- **LINE FIGHTING IS VALID AND GOOD — do NOT force soldiers off a working line.** Verticality/high-ground/flight is OPPORTUNISTIC (take it when it helps, reachable within mobility), never a mandate. A bias toward holding the line.
- Designer wants units to FLY, DASH, double-jump, take high ground (use mobility gear like a player) — the brave version (occasional over-commit/death is fine), but line-safe.
- Melee-primary soldiers should NOT carry a redundant melee blade sidearm; take a SUMMON-staff utility sidearm instead (frontline fields a minion).
- **WEAPON-COMPATIBILITY RULE (already standing): make it WORK, Unsupported is last resort.**
- **ART is a known GAP:** cannot reliably produce Terraria-quality pixel art via agents; the Command Radio / added items look "funky." Needs a human hand or reference sprites to trace. Not brute-forceable. (Designer flagged for 1.0; set expectations.)

**SCOPE for 1.0 = the 4 BASE CLASSES (melee/ranged/magic/summon) all work.** Secondary classes (Calamity ROGUE custom resource; Thorium BARD/HEALER custom resources) = "a different beast" (designer's words) — deferred; if trivial, add later; would be separate submods (Thorium-compat, Calamity-compat) and need the uploaded mod files.

## 🎯 1.0 FEATURE SCOPE — THE BIG BRIEF (designer 2026-10-20: "i think this should be 1.0 update ngl" + a UI mockup)

A dense design brain-dump. Captured in full; sequencing + opinions are Opus's, NOT yet all greenlit. GREENLIT = fire-and-let-live weapons (building now). Everything else needs the design pass below to settle before building.

**T1 — DEPLOY-AND-PERSIST WEAPONS (✅ SHIPPED v0.9.35, boots clean — needs playtest):** fire-and-let-live hold-fire gate. ItemShots.HoldsFire(s, itemType, target) holds a soldier's fire while its OWN tagged field (SoldierProjectileTag) is alive (timeLeft > 30) and still reaches the target; re-fires only on expiry/target-left (PreShoot then clears the spent rainbow). Gate in SoldierNPC.Combat.cs:173 after range+LOS, before the attack path, guarded by AttackCooldown<=0 so the pool scan only runs on fire-eligible ticks; non-category weapons bail on a HashSet miss. SET = LevyConstants.DeployPersistItems {Rainbow Gun, Magnet Sphere, Nimbus Rod, Crimson Rod}; per-weapon coverage: rainbow head always covers + pieces within 24px, Magnet ball within 210px w/ clear line, rain clouds = moving always / raining-not-fading with target in the drop column. Clinger Staff SKIPPED (16px column, instant recast = no uptime lost by mashing; one switch-case away if ever wanted). KNOWN (pre-existing, not introduced): multiple soldiers with the SAME one of these weapons share the commander's owner, so vanilla's same-owner field culling (Magnet 30-tick cut, Nimbus 3-cap, Crimson 1-cap) can truncate each other's fields → the gate treats truncated as lapsed and re-fires, rate-limited by cooldown (most visible with several Crimson Rod soldiers); real fix needs per-soldier owner handling (future). DeployPersistItems is now a ready-made sidearm-preferred list for T2. NOTE: "keep those weapons as SIDEARMS" still deferred to T2 loadout work.

**T2 — AUTO-UPGRADE / LOADOUT FLOW (KEYSTONE). ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.36 (boots clean — needs playtest).** ArmoryTE.UpgradeWeapons: every 5s (staggered by id) a soldier checks his home barracks' LinkedArmory for a weapon scoring ≥1.15× his current one OF THE SAME KIND (same damage class for primary; sentry/whip/summon/blade pool for sidearm), via the SAME TakeVaried/TakeBest pick as initial issue — so it draws a REAL finite instance (old weapon deposited back, protected-spill if storage full → no loss) and N soldiers SPREAD across the best N farmed weapons (the variety the designer missed, for free). 30s swap cooldown + the 15% margin = no thrash (score only climbs). Server/SP authority (mirrors Rearm; client-safe). Reforge counts as an upgrade too. GATES: per-soldier SoldierRecord.LoadoutLocked (saved `loadoutLock` + synced; default off = auto-upgrade on; UI toggle is future), per-slot hand-locks honored, LevyConfig.AutoUpgradeWeapons (default on), skipped in Engage/Shaken/Routed/mid-swing. LEFT OUT (follow-ups): armor/accessory auto-upgrade; forced class-change; thrown-stack swap. WATCH in playtest: (1) a swap that changes ammo type (old gun/new gun different ammo — IssueGear re-draws ammo, untested live); (2) Magic Storage perf — TakeVaried falls through to a MS network snapshot, called per-soldier every 5s; if it hitches, lengthen AutoUpgradeCheckIntervalTicks or add a no-MS flag. Original design notes below:
OLD KEYSTONE NOTE: The loudest pain: "i'm making tons of weapons, i've been farming, but they still use post-1-mech sentries" / "too many items i'm making and they refuse to use."** Root cause: `IssueGear`/`Rearm` (SettlementSystem/RosterSystem) picks a weapon ONCE on arm and never re-evaluates when the player farms better gear. The core fantasy (farm → army gets stronger) is broken because loot doesn't flow to the army. FIX: soldiers AUTO-UPGRADE — periodically re-evaluate and swap to the best available armory weapon for their role (reuse WeaponClassifier.Score), default ON with a per-soldier LOADOUT LOCK to protect hand-picked kits. This also recreates the weapon VARIETY/churn the designer misses from the old item-loss-on-death ("it forced units to use more weapon varieties" — NOT advocating item loss, but wants its effect). PLUS manual control: "force X units to use a specific class or loadout" + per-unit rearm. Sidearm-flag for T1 weapons lands here.

**T3 — ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.43 (boots clean; UI needs in-game look). ARMORY SEARCH + FILTER (Magic Storage-style).** ArmoryPanel: vanilla UISearchBar (multi-term AffixName match) + a RARITY-SWATCH strip (16 tiers + "+" for modded; colors via ItemRarity.GetColor with rarity-0/Expert-disco/Master special-cased; multi-select uint mask; Expert swatch animates) + a category cycle button (All/Melee/Ranged/Magic/Summon/Armor/Accessory/Ammo/Potion/Other via WeaponClassifier.ItemDamageClass + item kind). Filtered grid is COMPACTED (Magic-Storage style, chosen call — flip to dim-in-place if designer prefers) with REAL-index mapping verified (MakeSlot gets the true Storage index via `_shown[start+c]`, so drags act on the correct item; filtering is VIEW-ONLY, never edits/sends packets). Clear button, "Showing N of M", no-match message, scroll clamps to filtered set, reopen resets filters. JUDGMENT CALL to confirm: Loot All follows the active filter (takes only shown items when filtered) — flip if designer wants it to always empty everything. No empty slots in a filtered view (can't drop into empty cell while filtering; a trailing drop-slot is an easy follow-up if wanted). Panel grew 424→452px. Original ask below:
OLD: **T3 — ARMORY SORT/FILTER (Magic Storage-style). Designer: "the armory is difficult to sort — i'd just steal magic storage in terms of how to sort it and filtering out items."** Search bar + category filter (melee/ranged/magic/summon/sentry/accessory) + sort (tier/DPS/name). Supports T2 and T4.

**T4 — BARRACKS / ROSTER UI OVERHAUL. ✅ CORE SHIPPED v0.9.37 (loads clean — UI UNVERIFIED headless; NEEDS IN-GAME LOOK).** BarracksPanel rewritten: bottom-right RESIZE GRIP (UIWidgets.ResizeGrip) → drag to resize, roster REFLOWS into a grid of cards (CardGrid, cols from width, scroll on overflow, only in-view cards attached so 100-soldier rosters stay cheap). Size persisted per-character (UIPlayer.BarracksW/H, SaveData/LoadData). CARDS: name + GRADE COLOR tint/border (Levy grey-blue / Man-at-Arms blue / Champion gold; dead dimmed), HP BAR + MANA BAR, 5-slot gear strip (weapon/sidearm/head/body/legs with real tooltips), state + kills/Unpaid/Stood-down, actions on 2 pages (Follow/Hold/Return/Kit + Promote/Discharge/More → patrol/rebase/feed). Global buttons stay in the top header. GradeColor in UIActions. ⚠️ HEADLESS BOOT CANNOT RENDER UI — only proves content loads; resize/reflow/draw/tooltips are unverified by Opus, need the designer's eyes. DEFERRED/TODO: (1) LIVE SPRITE on card — deferred (rendering the in-world SoldierNPC dummy into the UI layer fights the world's Deferred batch + Main.Camera coords; needs a dedicated portrait-dummy or render-target driven from the record's gear; hook in place: CardPortraitWidth=0 reserves the column, RosterCard.DrawPortrait is an empty method; armor icons show for now). (2) MP mana bar shows default — SoldierRecord.WriteSnapshot doesn't carry Mana (SP correct; add Mana to the snapshot for MP). (3) button widths at the 220px min card untested vs real font metrics; (4) UI scale ≠ 100% untested. PLAYTEST CHECK: drag the corner reflows cards; small panel = ~1 row + scrollbar; scroll wheel + "More" page; loadout panel docks beside (not under) a wide panel; tooltips don't fight. ORIGINAL SPEC: Replace the vertical list with unit CARDS. KEY CLARIFICATION: the "picture of them" is NOT art — it's the soldier's LIVE SPRITE with their equipped armor set ("just what they look like, their armor sets. not like an art, just throw their sprite onto the menu"). The mod ALREADY renders each soldier via Main.PlayerRenderer.DrawPlayer on their Dummy (SoldierNPC.Draw.cs) — draw that same Dummy into the card. No pixel art needed; this was the only UI blocker and it's gone. ⚠️ CORRECTED 2026-10-20 (designer, frustrated — Opus over-engineered this): NOT draggable floating cards / multi-window. It's ONE RESIZABLE PANEL. Reference clip: a Magic-Storage-style storage bag with a CORNER DRAG-HANDLE — grab the bottom-right corner, the panel grows, slots REFLOW to fill it. "just do this. with the menu. let us adjust the size of the menu." So: the barracks menu is a single window with a resize handle; unit cards reflow in a grid to fill whatever size you drag it to → bigger window = more units visible, smaller = fewer ("see as many or as few as you want at once" = the resize, not floating windows). Keep it simple. Each card shows: the sprite (armor visible), name, loadout/equipment (the current nameplates are "descriptionless... can't see the items or equipment" — show the actual kit), HP BAR + MANA BAR ("show their health bars, mana bars, etc"), grade — "there should just be more information in general", color-coded by QUALITY/grade ("the better the type of unit it is a different color"). Global buttons (Mobilize, orders, enlist) at the TOP. Per-card: DIRECTLY grab weapons from the searchable armory (T3). Natural home for: DEMOTE ("it's impossible to downgrade a unit without removing them :(") alongside promote; FILTER the roster (by class/grade/home/status); FORCE-CLASS/loadout (T2); easier loadout editing. This also supersedes the bare floating nameplates with real info.

**T5 — ECONOMY / PROGRESSION PASS (needs design decisions):**
- **Wage-failure consequence** ("i don't know what the punishment is for failing to pay them considering all they do is complain"). Today: dialogue complaints only (payday/unpaid/hold-fire lines). Opus rec: escalate — grace period → morale/performance penalty → DESERTION if it persists (with warning). DECISION: how harsh?
- **Manpower** (cap = ManpowerPerNpc × townNPCcount, +3/day; designer: "cap ~90, 3/day... i'd change it so it could provide increased buff slots / new things"). Opus PUSHBACK: don't overload manpower (it's cleanly "army size"). INSTEAD route capability-unlocks through the BOOKS layer (below). DECISION: books vs manpower as the unlock lever?
- **BOOKS underpowered / "the guide to discipline sucks."** 5 lootable books (Conscription/Drill/Plunder/Fortification/Discipline); Discipline's effect is just +regen (DisciplineExtraRegen). Opus rec: make the books the PROGRESSION-UNLOCK layer — buff slots, new unit abilities, new orders/capabilities — so they're worth farming. This absorbs the designer's "increased buff slots / allowing units to do new things" wish.

**2026-10-21 FEEDBACK BATCH (resize confirmed working):** 🔧 2 agents in flight.
- UI POLISH agent (BarracksPanel): (1) softer scroll (scale CardGrid.ScrollWheel delta down); (2) compact header / kill dead space before cards; (3) MANPOWER→row of HEARTS, COMMAND POINTS→row of STARS (Terraria HUD style, "like the game"); (4) ROSTER SORT/FILTER by class (melee/ranged/magic/summon) + weapon tier + grade.
- WAGE MUTINY agent (T5 wage consequence, designer LOVES it): unpaid-by-end-of-day → soldiers REFUSE ORDERS until paid, then OPEN WAR — turn hostile and ATTACK the commander ("i think that's funny"). Behind a LevyConfig toggle (default OFF, opt-in chaos); paying clears it. Full attack-the-player may be iterative (big AI change: make the player a valid target for mutinous soldiers).
- CORRECTED (designer, twice): the DISCIPLINE book is NOT mana recovery — Opus misread "regen". It is "Each settlement regenerates 1 extra manpower at dawn" (DisciplineExtraRegen=1, FlavorSystem.cs:129 / Flavor.hjson). It's weak because +1/day does nothing at the 90 manpower cap. FIX = make manpower regen scale with total NPC count (see batch-2 MANPOWER REGEN). The other 4 books: Conscription (+manpower cap per NPC), Drill, Plunder (+coin), Fortification (+radius defense) — only Discipline flagged weak so far.
- UNDECIDED (designer floated, then "i dunno"): (6) toggle "weapon disabled until next day if its wielder dies" (variety mechanic, echoes item-loss-on-death) — Opus LEANS SKIP (auto-upgrade + finite instances already gives variety; this adds confusion/bookkeeping; low harm as a toggle though). (7) CLASS QUOTA — set % per class (e.g. 25% each melee/ranged/magic/summon), army auto-composes to hit it — Opus LIKES THIS, cleaner than (6) and subsumes the earlier "force X units to a class"; recommend building (7), skipping (6). Both await designer's call.

**2026-10-22 BATCH 11 — 🌙 1.0 PUSH (designer: "you can ship this as 1.0"; big progression + fixes brain-dump):**
- ✅ TWO-WHIPS + SHROOMITE STEERING — SHIPPED v0.9.52 (boots clean — needs playtest). (1) Summoners took a whip primary + whip sidearm (two whips): root = IssuePrimary→TakeBestWeaponInClasses accepted any Summon-class item and Score ranks a whip over a staff (TakeBestWeaponOfClass already required Family==Summon, so it wasn't the culprit). FIX: new PrimaryRule helper (in TakeBestWeaponWhere/TakeBestWeaponOfClass/UpgradePrimary) excludes whips while a summon staff is stocked-or-in-hand (StaffAvailableFor scans shelves); UpgradePrimary lets a held whip yield to any staff ignoring the margin; ReconcileWithinClass swaps a whip→best staff for Summon armor; ShelveSidearmForPrimary shelves a duplicate whip. Only-whips-in-stock → whip still issued (never empty). (2) SHROOMITE helmets steer ranged subtype (read from Player.cs ~11534): Headgear→arrows(bows), Mask→bullets(guns), Helmet→specialist(rocket/dart/gel/flare launchers + IsRangedSpecialistWeapon). New ArmorDoctrine.RangedSubtype enum + FavoredRangedSubtype(head) + FitsRangedSubtype(weapon,sub) (uses AmmoID.Sets.IsArrow/IsBullet/IsSpecialist). RangedSteer (soft 0.6 SubtypeSteerMinScoreFraction margin, not strict): a Shroomite soldier prefers the favored subtype only if the best favored weapon scores ≥0.6× the best ranged weapon of any subtype — else a far-stronger off-subtype weapon still wins, and none-in-stock keeps the best ranged. Non-summon/non-Shroomite soldiers: both helpers early-return → unchanged (verified). PERF watch: StaffAvailableFor + RangedSteer scan shelves+MS snapshot per primary pick (unmeasured; cheap for button-press/throttled).
- 🔧 BRANCH CONSOLIDATION (in progress): the real-minion rework (SoldierMinions.cs) was NEVER merged to main — it lived only on the long-lived `worktree-levy-real-minions` test branch (= main + minions, re-derived every deploy → the version churn). The Stardust Dragon task DEPENDS on it, so it's time: Stardust agent redirected to build on the minion branch; the Stardust branch (= minions + Stardust) then merges to main, folding the minions in as part of 1.0. The user has played the minion-superset build since v0.9.47 with no minion-stability complaints (behavioral verify — walkers/tiger/drops — still owed, but it's shippable + reversible). After this, no more separate test branch; everything on main.
- 💬 GUIDES PROGRESSION SYSTEM + LOGISTICS — DESIGN LOCK PENDING (laid roster out to designer, asked 3 Qs: what Guide to Compartmentalization is, single mana-max path, HP ladder 100→200→400). Captured so far: Guide to Expertise (Lunar Pillar drop → +1 accessory slot, all soldiers), Guide to Firepower (kill WoF → +2 buff slots + 2× armory), Guide to Healthcare (craft w/ Life Fruits → militia max HP 400), Guide to the Arcane Arts (craft: Hallowed Bars + 1 of each boss soul → max militia mana), Guide to Compartmentalization (undefined), an unnamed +100HP(→200)+maxmana guide; Logistical Centers (building, chests → +250 slots to nearest armory). ENGINEERING FLAGS raised: "buff slots" is a NEW concept for soldiers (not players w/ a buff bar — must define); armory resizing (2× / +250) needs the armory storage to be growable (real plumbing — verify). TWO-WHIPS note "prioritize strongest weapon to each class before sidearms" already satisfied by IssueGear order. NOT BUILDING until the 3 Qs are answered.

**2026-10-22 BATCH 10 (designer weapon batch — bullets/scoring/held weapons/Spectre; "please do the scoring fixes!"):**
- ✅ SCORING + BULLET-AIM FIXES — SHIPPED v0.9.51 (boots clean — needs live-fire playtest). Three fixes in WeaponClassifier.Score + WeaponUse.AimShot:
  (1) BURST-CAP (Tome of Infinite Wisdom): Score ignored useLimitPerAnimation → rated a 4-shots-then-idle weapon as if it sustained the burst forever. FIX: shots = min(useLimitPerAnimation, naturalShots=ceil(anim/useTime)); cadence = (anim+reuseDelay)/shots when shots<natural or reuseDelay>0, else useTime. Plain auto-guns unchanged.
  (2) BULLET OVER-LEAD (high-velocity bullets miss): root cause = extraUpdates (a projectile advances 1+extraUpdates times/tick: High Velocity Bullet proj 242 = 7 → 8×/tick; plain Bullet proj 14 = 1 → 2×/tick). AimShot solved lead with the spawn speed, not the per-tick travel, so soldiers assumed a longer flight and over-led. FIX: steps=1+extraUpdates; leadSpeed=speed×steps; SolveIntercept at leadSpeed then /steps (spawn magnitude stays `speed`); gravity×steps², delay/steps. ⚠️ BROAD: this corrects EVERY bullet gun (plain bullets were over-led ~2× too), not just high-velocity — correct physics, better accuracy on movers, but a feel change across all guns. WATCH in playtest. steps=1 (no extraUpdates: most bows, bolts) = identical to before. Homing ammo already skips lead.
  (3) VORTEX BEATER ignored: cause was SCORING (classification + armor gate both correct). Its held AI (AI_075) fires every 5 ticks but Score used useTime 20 ×0.4 HeldScoreFactor → ~130, below a Uzi. FIX: new LevyConstants.HeldRealCadence dict {VortexBeater:5}; Score uses the real cadence + skips the 0.4 damp for listed held weapons → ~1300 (Chain Gun < Beater < SDMG). HeldScoreFactor stays 0.4 for all OTHER held weapons (Phantasm/Last Prism unshifted). Extensible dict if more held weapons report low. NOTE: a soldier in melee/magic armor still correctly refuses the ranged Beater (reconciliation) — if some still skip it, check armor + bullet stock.
- 💬 OPEN (awaiting designer answers, not built): SPECTRE — the MASK bonus (homing Spectre Wrath bolts on magic hit) IS implemented (SoldierNPC.SpectreWrathOnHit, reads Dummy.ghostHurt); the HOOD bonus (heal the wearer, Dummy.ghostHeal) is NOT — genuine gap, buildable (route heal to the soldier not the commander). Asked designer: Mask or Hood? PHANTASM "no secondary firing on hit" — held AI runs REAL vanilla behavior (HeldWeapons spawns a holder projectile, dummy set up holding fire, vanilla spawns the arrows), so unclear what's missing; guess = the holder is culled on idle (HeldIdleKillTicks) + respawned, resetting the Phantasm's fire-rate RAMP so it never hits overdrive. Asked designer to clarify (no arrows at all / never speeds up / a specific missing projectile / modded Phantasm?). STARDUST DRAGON — known-deferred (real-minion rework punts the linked-segment chain to the fake stand-in); designer agrees "expected"; do a proper multi-segment driver later.

**2026-10-22 BATCH 9 (designer boss clips — "we did really good until you look at the healthbar" [the PLAYER's], + "LOVE the tome of infinite wisdom"):**
- ✅ ARMY DRAWS A DOWNED BOSS'S AGGRO — SHIPPED v0.9.50 (boots clean; OFF by default; NEEDS heavy in-game test, esp. modded bosses — headless can't verify boss AI follows). Designer's problem: army trivializes the fight but the boss hard-targets the PLAYER and melts them. ROOT: EnemyTargeting deliberately walls bosses out (4 guards in IsAllowlisted) — "the boss rule owns them." FIX (discussed+gated with designer; chose downed-boss unlock over a drop item to avoid art): new config `BossesTargetSoldiers` (default FALSE, needs EnemiesTargetSoldiers on); unlock PER BOSS TYPE only after it's been DOWNED IN THIS WORLD (first kill always solo — no trivializing). New Common/Boss/BossDowned.cs: BossDownedSystem ModSystem, HashSet<string> keyed `ModNPC.FullName` or `"Terraria:"+type` (covers modded bosses a vanilla downed-flag can't), saved under world tag `downedBosses`, synced via WorldData (NetSend/NetReceive union, server re-sends on each new kill); records on OnKill (server/SP), parts never record, EoW on last segment, Twins only when BOTH dead. IsAllowlisted: the 4 boss guards now defer to BossAllowlisted = BossAggroUnlocked && (native route for UsesNewTargetting, else swap for any aiStyle except swap-unsafe). Added BossSwapIncompatibleAiStyles={EmpressOfLight 120} (reads target only via GetTargetData → slot 255 = no target → she'd idle). TWO-SWAPPER SLOT-255 COORDINATION (the real risk — BossRule already swaps 255 for dashes, runs even with this toggle OFF): mutual exclusion via BossRuleGlobalNPC.IsDashSwapping/DashSwapDue + EnemyTargeting.IsSwapped — the dash wins on a dash tick whichever hook runs first; each system unswaps only its own; ET.EnsureSlotRestored is the catch-all net. Opus VERIFIED: with the new toggle OFF, BossAllowlisted→false for every boss, new checks are cheap no-ops, zero behavior change to existing play; dash refactor (TryGetDashBody) preserves dash behavior. BossRule HP-scaling + fear read life-fraction/kills not target, so unaffected (agent read, not run). KNOWN/PLAYTEST (agent-flagged, honest): (1) existing worlds — bosses killed BEFORE this build count as not-downed; re-kill to unlock (did NOT seed from vanilla downedBoss* flags; modded bosses have none anyway). Test flow: kill a boss once → re-summon → army tanks it. (2) despawn/flee: a boss that despawns on player-death/out-of-range now reads the soldier dummy → may keep fighting instead of despawning. (3) multi-part mismatch (head on soldier, a hand on player). (4) modded bosses = blanket allow, no per-AI audit — some will keep fixating via direct Main.player[0] reads. (5) Betsy native route probably ineffective (TargetClosestBetsy not detoured; harmless).
- 💬 TOME OF INFINITE WISDOM over-picked — DIAGNOSED (scoring bug), fix not yet built. NOT a hardcoded favorite. ROOT: the Tome has useLimitPerAnimation (fires 4 shots then idles out its animation; WeaponUse.FinishUse:906 handles the FIRING), but WeaponClassifier.Score ignores useLimitPerAnimation (only accounts for reuseDelay) → scores it as if it sustains the 4-shot burst forever at its tiny useTime → inflated DPS out-ranks genuinely-better weapons. GENERIC (any burst-cap weapon). FIX (queued, pending designer confirm): fold useLimitPerAnimation into Score's cadence = (useAnimation + reuseDelay) / useLimitPerAnimation. CAVEAT raised to designer: if the "lunar weapon" they expected is ranged/melee and the soldier wears MAGIC armor, the v0.9.49 reconciliation CORRECTLY keeps them magic (they'd only consider Nebula Blaze/Arcanum) — that's intended, not the scoring bug. Awaiting designer: soldier's armor + which lunar weapon, to know if the scoring fix fully solves it.

**2026-10-21 BATCH 8 (designer — "turtle armor (melee) / spooky armor (summoner) with MAGE weapons. This IS a bug"):**
- ✅ WEAPON↔ARMOR CLASS MISMATCH FIXED — SHIPPED v0.9.49 (boots clean — NEEDS the designer's 29-soldier reset test; a headless boot can't catch this, it only shows at scale). Opus FIRST mis-called this "working" (saw the coherent mages, missed the mismatched ones) — designer corrected, correctly. ROOT CAUSE: the weapon-picker and armor-picker are INDEPENDENT and disagree. IssuePrimary→ArmorableClasses scans the WHOLE pool (local+networked+Magic Storage) and calls a class armorable because a set exists *somewhere*, so the weapon goes magic (robes seen in MS); then TakeArmorSet places the best set from the LOCAL racks FIRST (own-armory-first) + by score (class-match is only a bonus, not a guarantee), so it bolts a local Turtle(melee)/Spooky(summon) set on. Loose TakeArmor fill is class-blind too. Nothing reconciled them → mage weapon on melee/summon armor, worst at a 29-soldier reset (sets vs weapons deplete at different rates). FIX (ArmoryTE.cs +49/-18, post-hoc reconciliation — chosen over an armor-first reorder because it's a guaranteed post-condition catching every divergence path): new ReconcileWeaponToArmor(soldier) — fluid only — after the armor is actually equipped, if the worn armor has a PreferredClass and the weapon's ItemDamageClass differs, return the weapon (Deposit + protected Spill) and draw TakeBestWeaponOfClass(armor's class); null→keep current (never empty-handed); locked-pouch guard (replacement must fire a hand-locked pouch's ammo, mirrors UpgradePrimary). Called in IssueGear (after the loose armor fills, before sidearm/ammo so they see the final weapon) and in UpgradeWeapons (after UpgradeArmor). Sidearm cleanup extracted to ShelveSidearmForPrimary (shared). ANTI-THRASH: UpgradePrimary fluid path now pins a classed soldier's upgrades to his ARMOR's class (PreferredClass non-null → candidates must match it; null → old ArmorableClasses rule) so he never chases an off-class weapon that reconciliation would revert every cooldown. NET MODEL: the armor a soldier wears IS his class; the weapon follows it and upgrades within it; UpgradeArmor can still move the whole class to a genuinely better set (weapon follows once). Turtle→melee weapon, Spooky→summon staff, robes→mage, no-class armor→any weapon. ClassMatchIssue path untouched.
- NOTE: "Reset loadouts" clears ALL gear + hand-locks and re-optimizes from scratch — spamming it on a magic-heavy armory re-homogenizes toward magic. With this fix a reset now yields COHERENT kits (weapon matches armor) instead of mismatches; class *composition* is still driven by what armor is stocked + TakeVaried spread. TRUE per-soldier/per-barracks CLASS CONTROL (the designer's real long-term want — "these are my melee troops") is still a separate backlog feature (class lock vs class quota — designer leaning per-soldier/barracks lock); pitched, not yet greenlit.

**2026-10-21 BATCH 7 (designer questions + a staff bug):**
- ❓ ANSWERED — do banners affect friendly levies? YES for OFFENSE: soldier shots are owned by the commander, and Projectile.cs:12465 applies `Main.player[owner].ApplyBannerOffenseBuff` (reads the GLOBAL Main.SceneMetrics.NPCBannerBuff) when a friendly projectile hits an NPC, so in SP a placed enemy banner boosts the damage levies deal to that enemy type (the new real minions get it too — HasNPCBannerBuff reads global scene state regardless of the owner object). The banner's DEFENSE half (take less from that enemy) only protects the player, not the soldiers (they're NPCs). Pure-contact melee that never spawns a projectile would miss the offense buff, but most soldier melee is projectile-driven (SoldierThrust/Boomerang/broadsword swing) and qualifies.
- ✅ METEOR / BLIZZARD / LUNAR FLARE STAVES RAIN FROM THE SKY — SHIPPED v0.9.48 (boots clean — needs playtest). Bug: on soldiers these fired straight out of the muzzle instead of raining from above; Meteor specifically fired at `ai[1]=scale=0` (invisible meteors). Vanilla hard-codes the spawn in Player.ItemCheck_Shoot else-if chains (≈44104/44135), NOT via aiStyle — spawn 600px above the caster at a point halfway between (target + spread toward the caster's back) and the caster, velocity forced downward (≥20px fall), scattered. FIX: ItemShots skyfall cases (BlizzardStaff/MeteorStaff/LunarFlare) + SkyfallOrigin/SkyfallVelocity/SkyIsOpen/Scatter helpers + LevyConstants.Skyfall.cs; per-staff ai[1] replicated (Blizzard frame 0-4, Meteor scale 0.5-0.8 + 1-of-3 variants ×0.75 speed, Lunar Flare target-Y collision height ×0.5 speed). Daedalus Stormbow + Blood Rain Bow already handled by WeaponUse.RainShot. Star Wrath (item 3065) flagged — different spawn shape, small follow-up. CAVEAT (agent judgment call, WATCH in playtest): SkyIsOpen gates on clear sky 600px above the soldier; under a roof/in a cave it falls back to the plain muzzle shot (so a roofed Meteor Staff soldier still fires the old invisible meteors) — the alternative (spawn inside rock, die on frame 1) is worse. Terrain collision reads the COMMANDER's Y (owner), so a shard can pass through terrain a bit differently than a player-fired one; NPC hit detection unaffected.
- 💬 DISCUSSING (not built) — BARRACKS HP/MANA UPGRADE. Designer wants militia to spawn with >100 HP / ~20 mana. CURRENT: militia spawn at the vanilla player baseline (BaseHp 100, BaseMana 20) and are raised by FEEDING Life/Mana Crystals to each soldier via the barracks panel (SoldierRecord.MaxHp = min(100 + 20·LifeCrystals + 5·LifeFruit, 500); MaxMana = min(20 + 20·ManaCrystals, 200); per-soldier counters, UIActions.FeedCrystal). Pain = per-soldier + tedious + fresh recruits reset to 100. OPUS REC (put to designer): a barracks "training standard" — deposit crystals into the BARRACKS once, every recruit it musters inherits that level (same 500/200 ceiling, reuses the existing per-soldier crystal fields), rather than a flat barracks-tier +HP number that would break the real-player ceiling + crystal metaphor. Awaiting designer's call on the shape before building.

ClassMatchIssue DEFAULT now FALSE=fluid (label "Fixed-class companies" for the old behavior; existing saves that never touched it flip to fluid, saved `true` keeps it). UpgradePrimary cross-class upgrade when fluid (any class scoring ≥1.15×; role separation kept: summon↔summon, sentry↔sentry); UpgradeArmor added on the same WeaponUpgradeTick heartbeat → re-arms the set toward the soldier's CURRENT WEAPON's class (set bonus lands), draw-all-first-or-rollback item safety + protected spill + same margin/cooldown/LoadoutLocked/hand-lock gating. Score now credits ammo: shotDamage = item.damage + AmmoScoreDamage (bullet 10, arrow 10, ROCKET 40 [GUESS — tunable constant], dart 6) so guns aren't undervalued vs melee. Two agent-added guards: need ≥100 of a new ammo stocked before switching onto it (no muster all grabbing bows + stranding each other); a ranged→melee cross returns the now-pointless fallback blade. PLAYTEST FLAGS: (1) rocket dmg 40 is a guess — tune AmmoScoreRocketDamage if launcher soldiers feel off; (2) 1400 class-match set bonus ≈17% of a mid set — a big tier jump still wins but can keep a slightly-lower same-class set; (3) perf: UpgradeArmor scans storage + networked + Magic Storage per soldier per 5s check (watch a 40-man muster w/ big MS); (4) verify thrown-restock + hand-locked re-arm still fine with weapon-before-armor order. The earlier agent fully respects the calmer build rhythm (this was discussed+agreed, not reflexively dispatched).
- (was: 🔧 FLUID LOADOUTS agent brief) ROOT CAUSE found + confirmed with designer: soldiers OSSIFY into a class — armor sets the class (ArmorDoctrine.PreferredClass), ClassMatchIssue makes the weapon pick prefer that class, auto-upgrade (v0.9.36) never crosses damage class (ArmoryTE:1166), and the armor-set score gives a class-match bonus — so farmed ranged/magic gear is ignored by melee soldiers and Orichalcum is kept over higher-tier Hallowed. PLUS a real melee SCORING bias: WeaponClassifier.Score credits ranged weapons on item.damage ALONE (ammo damage ignored) while melee gets full credit → melee over-rated. Designer wants FLUIDITY (enjoys both variety AND uniform legions; "go for stronger weapons/armor regardless of type; rearm armor as much as weapons"). MODEL being built = WEAPON DRIVES, ARMOR FOLLOWS: (1) weapon = strongest issuable regardless of class (relax the same-class auto-upgrade guard; ClassMatchIssue default flips to FALSE=fluid, kept as opt-in for fixed-class companies); (2) ARMOR auto-upgrades on the same heartbeat (was weapons-only) toward the best set of the soldier's CURRENT WEAPON's class (set bonus lands), same finite-instance + margin + cooldown + LoadoutLocked safety; (3) FIX Score to credit ammo damage for ranged so fluid doesn't collapse to all-melee (keep the HasAmmoFor availability filter — a gun with no stocked ammo genuinely can't fire). NOTE the designer's floated "mass reset button" — Opus pushed back it's a mop vs the real fix (fluidity makes it mostly unnecessary); not building it unless asked. BEHAVIORAL META (designer, important): STOP reflexively turning every musing into an immediate build; DISCUSS first, build on agreement. Don't call design choices "bugs". Don't condescend.
- (shelved) 🎯 ASSIGN SETS IN THE ARMORY (the big reframe of T2; supersedes score-based auto-spread as the primary control). Designer RETRACTS "monoloadouts are boring" — "a legion of hallowed soldiers is AWESOME." Do NOT force variety. Want CONTROL: define loadout SET TEMPLATES (armor+weapon+accessories) in the armory and assign them (uniform legion OR mixed, by choice). Maybe connect a MAGIC STORAGE crafting station so the armory auto-PRINTS a set of items into itself. This is how the player makes soldiers use the EXACT weapons they want (fixes the scoring complaint by explicit assignment). NEEDS DESIGN ALIGNMENT before building.
- MANPOWER REGEN: Discipline book's +1/day (3→4) is useless at the 90 cap. Designer: make manpower RECOVERY SCALE WITH TOTAL NPC COUNT so you don't keep buying recruiting drives. "The economy stuff is otherwise FINE, not a big issue" → DE-SCOPE the big T5 economy pass; just this regen tweak + the mutiny. (Earlier book-buff confusion resolved: the weak buff IS the manpower regen.)
- ARMORY RARITY FILTER (T3): "it's the BULK" — filter items you see/interact with by their Terraria RARITY COLOR (white/blue/.../rainbow). Concrete armory-panel filter.
- KIT BUTTON redundant now the card shows a gear strip — make the gear strip editable / drop the Kit button (small UI cleanup).

**1.0 MILESTONE STATUS (2026-10-21):** Done tonight (0.9.29→0.9.37): T1 deploy-persist weapons ✅, T2 auto-upgrade keystone ✅, T4 barracks resize+cards ✅ core (sprite + MP-mana deferred). STILL OPEN for a real 1.0: T3 armory sort/filter (not started), T5 economy pass (wage consequences, books/discipline actually unlocking things, manpower rework — none started), T4 live sprite. Opus pushed back: NOT everything — T3 + T5 (the progression/economy layer the designer was LEAST happy with) are unbuilt, so stamping 1.0 now ships with those complaints unaddressed. RECOMMENDATION: land T3 + T5, then cut 1.0. Awaiting designer's call (finish-then-1.0 vs ship-1.0-now-rest-as-1.1).

**OPUS'S PROPOSED 1.0 BUILD ORDER:** T1 (now) → T2 auto-upgrade (keystone, fixes loudest pain) → T3 armory sort → T4 barracks UI (drives the manual overrides) → T5 economy pass. ART remains a known gap (needs human/reference sprites) and will bound how polished T4's cards can look.

**WAVE 1 ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.16 (all four, integrated via 3-way merges, packs+boots clean):**
- ✅ Yoyos + Flails → Levy-owned SoldierYoyo/SoldierFlail (whole families work, vanilla+modded).
- ✅ Verticality + flight + dashes, LINE-SAFE — gear-assisted perches (winged soldiers take higher ground via AirPerchFeasible→aerial system), TryAerialPursuit for airborne targets (needs wings/rockets), TickDash (dummy.dashType). Gated: ~55% climb appetite, ≤25% mid-climb at once, kitless never flies.
- ✅ De-aggro fix — ROOT CAUSE was STICKY TARGETS (worm/etc. AIs only re-search on target loss, so a player-locked enemy never re-evaluated). Fix: periodic ~1/s re-poll of allowlisted non-soldier-locked enemies through the existing Compete path. Also added Unicorn/Snowman/Tortoise aiStyles, excluded Tumbleweed. Flying aiStyle 5 (Hornets/Meteor Head/etc.) STILL excluded (GetTargetData rejects slot 255) — documented; possible future: swap into slot 254.
- Melee sidearm — melee primaries take a summon sidearm, not a melee blade (ArmoryTE.IssueGear).
**WAVE 2a ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.17 (both integrated via disjoint 3-way merges, packs+boots clean):**
- ✅ Active whips — cadence now `clamp(round(useTime×0.75),14,40)` ticks (~2.7 lashes/s), lash lives the whole period, engages at the lash's *real* reach incl. `whipRangeMultiplier`, no more step-back after each hit. Damage ×1.35, tip 30% is a guaranteed crit, hitbox padded 8px. Tag: the summon tag was ALREADY applied (dead-summoner fear was unfounded) — PLUS now also feeds the commander's OWN vanilla minions the whip's tag buff (240t), so a whip-summoner soldier actively buffs the player's minions. Overhead-arc lash visual + crack SFX replaces the laser-pointer snap. Constants in LevyConstants.Summon.cs (WhipCadenceMult etc.).
- ✅ Bulk reforge-to-threshold — ArmoryPanel bottom bar: cycle Floor (Good top50% / Great top25% / Superb top10% / Best single-best), then "Reforge to floor". Treasury-funded (nearest linked barracks, vanilla reforge price, no discount), goes issue slots then storage. Quality = item.value ÷ unprefixed value (ranks ANY prefixable weapon vanilla/modded, scores cached per type via 600 throwaway Prefix(-2) rolls). Skips armor/accessory/ammo/favorited/stacked. Keep-best (never worse), 40-roll/weapon cap, stops when treasury dry. Full MP wiring (LevyPacket.ArmoryReforge → RosterNet.OnArmoryReforge, range-checked). Fixed a merge-caught hjson bug (ReforgeCapped value started with `{` → quoted it).
**WAVE 2b — SENTRIES ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.18 (boots clean; NEEDS LIVE PLAYTEST — see caveats):**
- New `WeaponFamily.Sentry=13`, detected GENERICALLY by `item.sentry==true` (checked BEFORE the Summon rule so a Summon-class sentry isn't misrouted). Equippable as primary OR as the utility sidearm a melee-type soldier takes (mirrors the summon-staff sidearm). Auto-issued via `IsIssuableDeployer` (staff|sentry).
- Deploy-and-forget: a sentry-armed soldier plants its tower where it stands when it enters combat & has no live tower; re-plants if the tower dies or it moves >50 tiles; one tower per soldier; the tower does its own vanilla targeting. Placement is free (no mana). Ground-search places DD2/Hydra/Spider/Houndius on the ground, Rainbow/Lunar hover.
- **SHARED-TURRET-CAP SOLUTION (the clever bit — review if ever touched):** soldier towers are owned by the commander (like all soldier projectiles) so they'd normally count against the commander's `maxTurrets` and get culled oldest-first by vanilla `Player.UpdateMaxTurrets` (non-virtual `WipableTurret`, can't hook). FIX: `LevyPlayer.PreUpdate` PARKS every soldier-tagged sentry (sets `sentry=false`) and `PostUpdate` restores it — the commander's item-check (25080) and maxTurrets-check (25102) both fall inside that window, so the cull only ever sees the commander's OWN towers. Backstop restore in `SoldierProjectileTag.PreAI`. Local player only. Damage: vanilla recomputes a sentry's damage from the OWNER's summon ratio each tick, so `SentryDamage()` divides the commander's ratio back out (clamp 0.1–10×) → the SOLDIER's gear sets tower damage. Sentry-fired shots inherit the soldier tag in OnSpawn for kill/loot credit.
- Files: Enums.cs, WeaponClassifier.cs, SoldierGear.cs, ArmoryTE.cs, SoldierNPC.Combat.cs, SoldierNPC.Summon.cs (all sentry logic), Globals/LevyPlayer.cs + Globals/SoldierProjectileTag.cs (park/restore + shot credit), LevyConstants.Summon.cs (RangeSentry 36, replace dist 50t, cooldowns, etc.).
- ⚠ **LIVE-PLAYTEST CAVEATS (reasoned from source, not exercised):** (1) the park window — verify 5 soldiers w/ Ballista Rods + commander placing own tower at maxTurrets=1, and swapping a maxTurrets accessory mid-fight; (2) modded sentries whose AI needs `ai[]` set in the item's Shoot hook get zeroed ai (vanilla ones are mirrored); (3) MP untested (added a 30000-tick MP re-plant since remote clients count down from spawn); (4) sentry-primary soldiers stand off at 36t and never melee unless they also carry a melee sidearm.
**DE-AGGRO BUG — ✅ FIXED v0.9.20 (designer repro: "flesh knuckles on soldiers, drink invis, they STILL target me first").** ROOT CAUSE: `SoldierNPC.RefreshStats()` success path read Defense/MoveSpeed/Abilities off the dummy after UpdateEquips but NEVER read `Dummy.aggro` into the soldier's `Aggro` field — so every soldier's runtime Aggro was permanently 0 (only ever set to 0 in the manual-fallback branch + network read). Flesh Knuckles / tank gear / taunt gear did LITERALLY NOTHING for targeting; the only soldier pull was raw proximity. The ROADMAP even CLAIMED "soldier Aggro is read off the dummy's equip pass" — documented intent, never wired. FIX = one line: `Aggro = Dummy.aggro;` after the Abilities read. Now EnemyTargeting weighs a Flesh-Knuckles soldier at the full vanilla −400px, same as a player (verified vanilla: FleshKnuckles `aggro+=400`, Putrid Scent `-=400`, NPC.cs target search does `dist -= player.aggro`). ⚠ TOLD DESIGNER: the **Invisibility Potion does NOT reduce aggro** in current vanilla (no aggro write anywhere near `invis` — it's purely visual); the real player de-aggro levers are **Putrid Scent (−400)** + stealth gear. So "drink invis → enemies prefer soldiers" was partly a vanilla misconception; but the unwired soldier Aggro was the real bug and is fixed. KNOWN REMAINING GAP (documented, not this bug): flying aiStyle 5 enemies (Hornets, Eater of Souls, Meteor Heads) are still excluded from the allowlist (GetTargetData reads Main.player[target] unguarded, neither swap nor native is safe) — they can't be redirected onto soldiers at all. Possible future: swap into slot 254 instead of 255, or an IL patch. Flag if the designer reports flyers specifically.

## 🌙🌙 WAVE 3 — WIDE PARALLEL PUSH (designer 2026-10-19: "go do EVERYTHING now while usage is 100%, be thorough, stop doing it piecemeal. KEEP signature weapon abilities — don't strip the phoenix.")

Four agents dispatched in parallel on disjoint file regions (serialize pack/boot as usual):
- **HELD-PROJECTILE WEAPONS (the big one).** Make Phantasm / Vortex Beater / Last Prism / Charged Blaster / Laser Machinegun / Celebration Mk2 / DD2PhoenixBow ("Phantom Phoenix") WORK on soldiers WITH their real signature AI (phoenix-every-3rd, Vortex rockets, Last Prism beam) — the designer explicitly refused stripping the gimmick. ROOT CAUSE of "Phantom Phoenix won't fit the weapon slot" (designer screenshot): item 3854 DD2PhoenixBow has `channel=true`, `shoot=705` (aiStyle 75 = HeldProjectile, `IsHeldOwnerProjectile` true), so WeaponClassifier.cs:125 benched it — the held "gun" projectile would anchor to & fire from the COMMANDER, not the soldier. Same family as Phantasm/VortexBeater/LastPrism. FIX APPROACH (faithful): spawn the REAL vanilla held projectile owned by slot 255 (dummy), tagged to the soldier, and a new GlobalProjectile swaps that soldier's Dummy into Main.player[255] for the held projectile's AI call (IDENTICAL to the proven EnemyTargeting slot-255 swap) so the real AI anchors/aims/fires from the soldier. New WeaponFamily.HeldWeapon. Agent fenced to WeaponClassifier/WeaponUse/Combat/SoldierGear/Enums/new files; must guarantee slot-255 always restored. NEEDS live playtest.
- **DD2 / MULTICLASS ARMOR → wrong class.** Designer: "multiclass Old One's Army armor is weird — they only take summoner weapons unless you mess with their stuff." DD2 sets (Squire/Monk/Huntress/Apprentice + upgrades) pile on sentry/minion bonuses → armory's class-sniffer reads SUMMON → issues summon weapons though Huntress=ranged, Apprentice=magic, Squire/Monk=melee. Fix in ArmorDoctrine.cs + ArmoryTE class-pick: weight real per-class damage so the dominant martial class wins; sentry/minion-capacity alone must not = summoner. Fenced to ArmorDoctrine + ArmoryTE class region.
- **MOVEMENT: stuck-underground + run/jump oscillation.** Two narrow pathologies, CONSERVATIVE guards only (designer likes the current feel — must NOT regress). Fenced to Movement.cs/Brain.cs/Common.Movement + LevyConstants.Movement. NEEDS live playtest.
- **READ-ONLY AUDIT** → RE-STRUCTURED per designer ("decentralize — dispatch several Haiku agents per progression STAGE instead of one going through all of Terraria"). Monolithic Sonnet audit STOPPED; replaced by 5 Haiku stage agents (early-prehm / late-prehm / early-hm / mid-hm / endgame), each writing scratchpad/audit-N-*.md. Consolidate into the next build wave when they land.

## 🧭 WAVE 4 BACKLOG — from the decentralized per-stage audits (reports in scratchpad/audit-N-*.md)

Audits done: **ALL 5 COMPLETE** — stage 1 (early-prehm) ✅, stage 2 (late-prehm) ✅, stage 3 (early-HM) ✅, stage 4 (mid-HM) ✅, stage 5 (endgame) ✅. Full gap map below; Wave 4 closes it. Findings (source-verified by the Haiku agents):

**⭐ GENERIC ROOT-CAUSE PATTERNS (fix these, not 30 one-offs — echoes the generic-compat reframe):**
1. **Zero-mana free-thrower exploit:** a melee-class `noMelee` thrown weapon (aiStyle 2 ThrownProjectile) with no `consumable`/`maxStack` falls through to the plain-shooter fallback (WeaponClassifier.cs:182) as Magic at 0 mana → infinite throws. Hits: **Vampire Knives, Shadowflame Knife** (and Daybreak is adjacent). GENERIC FIX: detect melee+noMelee+ThrownProjectile → a Levy-owned thrower with an ammo pouch (preferred, tenet #1) or bench.
2. **Channel/owner-gated shots the held-reject misses:** WeaponClassifier.cs:125 only rejects channel items whose shot is HeldProjectile(75)/ThickLaser(84). Other owner-reading channel/guided styles slip through and misbehave: **Medusa Head** (aiStyle 100 MedusaRay — self-kills unless commander channels, charges commander mana), **Rainbow Rod** (aiStyle 9 MagicMissile under channel — ignores soldier aim, homes nearest), **Chlorophyte Chainsaw** (aiStyle 20 Drill — owner-anchored). GENERIC FIX: expand owner-anchored detection; for the salvageable ones use the Flying-Knife driver (SoldierProjectileTag.PreAI feeds soldier center/target/mana — Rainbow Rod uses aiStyle 9, the SAME style the Flying Knife already drives).
3. **Owner-benefit leaks (heal/mana credited to the COMMANDER):** **Vampire Knives** lifesteal heals the commander; **Soul Drain** charges commander mana; **Medusa** charges commander mana. GENERIC FIX: Levy-owned AI that credits the SOLDIER (mana from Record.Mana, lifesteal to the soldier).
4. **Fidelity batch (family right, shot differs from vanilla) — all EASY, low-risk polish:** Multishot table corrections (Onyx Blaster 3→ currently 4; Marrow should be 1 not 2; Tsunami spread; Bubble Gun 3; Razorpine; Blizzard Staff) in LevyConstants; missing ResolveProjectile ammo-forcing cases (Ice Bow→FrostArrow, Marrow→BoneArrow, Pulse Bow→PulseBolt) in WeaponUse; flail on-hit status (Sunfury fire) via a new `ApplyFlailStatus` mirroring ApplyBoomerangStatus; Score() ignores `reuseDelay`/`useAnimation` → overstates burst guns ~2x (Clockwork); Ice Sickle vanilla ×0.67; Rainbow Gun per-soldier kill-old; spawn jitter (Crystal Storm, Sky Fracture, Gatligator, Shadowflame Hexdoll).



**ENDGAME (stage 5):**
- **Zenith** — PROBLEM: owner-anchored (shot 933, aiStyle 182 reads commander MountedCenter → swords orbit the COMMANDER) AND misclassified (noMelee=true → falls to Magic plain-shooter). FIX (medium): slot-255 dummy swap for proj 933 only, + route as Broadsword (relax the noMelee gate) so it fights at blade reach.
- **Daybreak** — PROBLEM: misclassified (melee, noMelee, aiStyle 113 StickProjectile → falls to Magic → brain kites at RangeMagic instead of closing). NOT owner-anchored. FIX (medium): thrown-melee route (Levy thrower/thrust) for melee-class noMelee non-spear non-beam swings; keep DamageType Melee.
- **Starlight (PiercingStarlight 4923) + Solar Eruption (3473)** — aiStyle 75 held + channel + MELEE class; benched today. Should be caught by the HeldWeapon wrap (generic aiStyle75+channel key) — I've told the held agent to give MELEE-class held weapons melee reach, not ranged standoff.
- **Anti-gravity Hook** — correctly Unsupported (damage=0 grapple tool). Would need a Levy grapple MOVEMENT ability, not a fired weapon. Leave benched.
- **LunarSentry facing** (Rainbow Crystal Staff 3571, Lunar Portal Staff 3569; aiStyle 123) — minor cosmetic: tower reads commander's `direction`. EASY: slot-255 swap + set Dummy.direction from the soldier.
- Confirmed OK (no action): Influx Waver, Meowmere, Star Wrath, Terra Blade (all Broadsword+beam, no owner re-anchor), Terrarian (yoyo), Kaleidoscope (whip), Stardust Dragon/Cells (SoldierMinion), Betsy's Wrath/Eventide (bow), Nightglow/Nebula Blaze/Nebula Arcanum/Lunar Flare (magic), Xenopopper/SDMG/Celebration (gun).

**MID-HARDMODE (stage 4):**
- **Life Drain / Soul Drain (3006)** — PROBLEM: owner-billed + owner-steered. Shot 476 aiStyle 185 LifeDrain charges `Main.player[owner]` (the COMMANDER's) mana on each hit and steers follow-ups to the commander. Issued as Magic, misbehaves. FIX (medium): Levy-owned AI via SoldierProjectileTag.PreAI (same pattern as the Flying Knife) — charge the SOLDIER's mana, aim at the soldier's target. Do NOT bench (tenet #1).
- **Chlorophyte Chainsaw (1232)** — latent PROBLEM, currently refused only by the axe>0 rule: it's channel + aiStyle 20 Drill (owner-anchored, reads owner.channel + MountedCenter), which `IsHeldOwnerProjectile` (only 75/84) MISSES. FIX (easy): add Drill (20) to IsHeldOwnerProjectile / the channel refusal — **do this BEFORE the axe-rule relaxation below** so a commander-anchored drill never ships.
- **Chlorophyte Greataxe (1233) + Warhammer (1234)** — coverage gap: refused by the pick/axe/hammer rule though they're plain melee swing blades (useStyle 1, shoot 0). FIX (easy): let Melee-class useStyle-Swing items with shoot==0 through the axe/hammer check → Broadsword (keep the PICK refusal; shoot==0 excludes the Chainsaw which has shoot=224).
- **Toxic Flask (3105)** — aiStyle 2 thrown, the aim-solve assumes no gravity-drop; source has no gravity term so likely fine. Verify in-game only if it visibly arcs.

Ranks ~#1 in tier (score ~184). FIX: per tenet #1 make it work (medium) — a Levy-owned thrown knife with its OWN projectile type (not 304) so the type==304 heal never fires; credit lifesteal to the SOLDIER.
