EASY generic fix: add shoot types 16 (Magic Missile) + 34 (Flamelash) + 79 (Rainbow Rod) to the EXISTING Flying-Knife driver `DriveSoldierFlyingKnife` in SoldierProjectileTag.cs — it already drives aiStyle 9 for the Flying Knife. NO classifier change needed (they're already classified Magic). Cleanest fix in the whole backlog.
- **THROWING CONSUMABLES (whole class benched)** — Throwing Knife, Shuriken, Poisoned Knife, Javelin, Spiky Ball, Grenade, Beenade, Bone Dagger/Javelin: benched by the `consumable` gate (WeaponClassifier.cs:117). **BOMBS** (Bomb, Dynamite, Sticky/Bouncy Bomb): benched by damage==0 (the projectile carries the damage). NEW SUBSYSTEM: a Levy-owned thrown projectile + soldier pouch draw (like SoldierBoomerang but consumable-stack). MEDIUM, no existing pattern — a whole early-game playstyle currently unusable. DESIGN CALL: build it? (lean yes — it's "make weapons work", and throwing is a real class.)
- **TETHERS (Harpoon 160, Chain Knife 1325, KO Cannon/Boxing Glove 1314; also Golem Fist, Chain Guillotine)** — aiStyle 13 Harpoon, benched. Retract to Main.player[owner], one-per-owner. MEDIUM, no pattern (Levy-owned tether anchored to soldier). Nicher; lower priority. DESIGN CALL.
- Flying Knife already handled; Light's Bane safe (aiStyle 188 no owner read). All ore/wood weapons, yoyos, flails, whips, boomerangs, summon staves, guns, bows, spears, plain casters verified SUPPORTED.

## 🔨 WAVE 4 PLAN (all audits in — closing the map). Partition is constrained because WeaponClassifier.cs + WeaponUse.cs are touched by many items (routing depends on behaviors existing), so classifier-coupled slices serialize / hand-merge; isolated slices parallelize.
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.23 (boots clean): (1) channel-guided driver — Magic Missile(16)/Flamelash(34)/Rainbow Rod(79) now home on the SOLDIER's target via new `DriveSoldierGuidedMissile` in SoldierProjectileTag.cs (pure pursuit lerp 0.14, no lead yet, own dust trails; Flying Knife untouched). (2) fidelity batch: Marrow→1 shot + BoneArrow force; Ice Bow→FrostArrow; Pulse Bow→PulseBolt; Onyx Blaster count 4→5 (vanilla is 5; center BlackBolt 2×/1.3× NOT replicated — approximation); Bubble Gun→(3,30°) approx; `ApplyFlailStatus` (Sunfury OnFire 50%, Flaming Mace OnFire) — only two vanilla flails have on-hit status; Score folds reuseDelay+useAnimation so burst guns (Clockwork ~-54%) rank realistically (MAY shift issue order); Ice Sickle beam ×0.67. PLAYTEST: burn appears on Sunfury/Flaming Mace, Ice Sickle beam weaker than swing, forced-ammo bows, Rainbow Rod visual, re-ranking.
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.24: (B) Soul Drain(476)/Medusa(535) now driven from the soldier — Soul Drain walks onto the soldier's target + heals the SOLDIER 1 HP/hit; Medusa floats at the soldier, pulses the nearest 3, never self-kills, credits the soldier; neither ever touches the commander's mana/HP.
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.25: (C) THROWING class — WeaponFamily.Thrown=15. Thrown consumables (knives/shuriken/javelin/spiky ball/grenade/beenade/bone dagger+javelin) + bombs (bomb/dynamite/sticky/bouncy) now throw from the soldier with real shot behavior. Economy: stack lives in the weapon slot (cap 60), depletes per throw, OUT_OF_AMMO bark + fall to sidearm at zero, armory restocks on re-arm + a mid-fight TopUp within 3 tiles (ThrownRestockTick on the sidearm-check line). Flight-sim aim solver (ThrownAim.cs) per aiStyle (delay/gravity/drag + lead), prefers flat arcs, RangeTiles 20. Bombs: ThrownBlastGuard.cs stops soldier bomb blasts from destroying tiles/walls (toggle ThrownBlastsHarmTiles) and friendly-fire is off (npcProj). Classify gate is strict (consumable + damaging friendly shot of aiStyle 1/2/14/16/113, excludes potions/placeables/liquid bombs/ammo). Score ×0.3 so guns outrank stacks. MINOR FOLLOW-UPS (deferred): (a) Brain resupply doesn't treat an empty thrower as "dry" so a soldier won't actively WALK to an armory to restock throwables (waits for the 3-tile TopUp) — small Brain change; (b) Beenade bees spawn untagged → loot credit to commander; (c) thrown stacks can't be placed in the armory issue-weapon ROW (only bulk storage + the loadout slot). PLAYTEST: throws land at 15-20 tiles, each projectile type's first spawn (grenade/dynamite timing, sticky/bone-javelin sticking, poison), stack depletion + restock loop, blast guard fully stops terrain damage incl. scarab bomb, thrower/gun mix in a stocked armory.
- (prior IN FLIGHT marker cleared — throwing class landed) — new WeaponFamily.Thrown; thrown consumables (knives/shuriken/javelin/grenade/beenade/bombs/dynamite) throw from the soldier with real shot behavior; ECONOMY = stack lives in the weapon slot, depletes per throw, armory restocks like ammo (designer's call; reversible to infinite). Touches Enums/WeaponClassifier/WeaponUse/SoldierGear/ArmoryTE + new files. NOTE: Vampire Knives + Shadowflame Knife (the noMelee free-thrower exploit) will be caught by C's consumable/thrown routing — BUT Vampire Knives (non-consumable, melee noMelee) may still need the lifesteal-to-commander fix separately; re-check after C lands.

Melee/thrown/held/summon skipped. Should fire the Staff of Infinite Wisdom's tornadoes. Daybreak + Shadowflame Knife fire their REAL shots at RangeThrownMelee=14 (close ~12t, no more kiting at 40); Vampire Knives (shot 304) fires a fan of NEW Levy SoldierVampireKnife that heals the SOLDIER (clamp(dmg×0.075,1,5)) via ai[0]=whoAmI, never touching the commander (the vanilla type-304 vampireHeal never runs). ThrownAim per-shot flight models.

The melee-thrower agent found it reading the code (headless boot can't catch dead-but-loads code) and fixed all three. So THROWING ACTUALLY WORKS as of v0.9.27, not v0.9.25. PLAYTEST throwing fresh.

## 🎮 PLAYTEST FEEDBACK (designer, during Wave 4b) — queued/assigned
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.24: **Flying Knife + guided missiles no longer orbit** — new shared `HomeIn` helper, turn rate ramps 0.16→0.55 rad/tick as it closes, aims at an intercept/lead point, speed ramps while lined up and bleeds on overshoot so it tightens the turn and strikes. Knife dives in, passes through, snaps back, strikes again.
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.24: **Mage sidearm-over-primary** — ROOT CAUSE was MANA ECONOMY (flat 3/s combat regen << staff drain → starved onto sidearm), NOT selection/range. Fix: pool-scaled regen (combat max(6, 20% pool)/s, idle max(12,40% pool)/s), and a mana-fed primary that's ≤1 cast short HOLDS FIRE up to 90 ticks for the primary instead of instantly swapping; sidearm stays out only for longer waits. Buff to mages = fits the tenet.
- ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.24: **True-melee relentless** — Broadsword/Shortsword/Spear only: removed the 12-tick post-hit step-back, press into contact (hitbox ≤0.5 tiles / centre ≤2 tiles vs old 3), wider swing trigger (reach×1.8), and swing at ANY hostile within reach+1 when the chosen target slips. Cadence (useTime/reuseDelay) UNCHANGED (no speed buff — just removed the gaps). Line-fighting/hold/anti-suicide untouched.
- **ALT-FIRES — "soldiers should use their alt fires more"** (example: Staff of Infinite Wisdom's tornadoes; also "fires one paper"). The mod only triggers LEFT-click attacks; weapons with a right-click alt (AltFunctionUse — most modded weapons) never use it. NEW FEATURE, QUEUED (touches WeaponUse/Combat — serialize behind throwing-class + combat agents). Design: when a weapon has an alt attack, soldiers should use it situationally/mix it in. ⚠ Staff of Infinite Wisdom is a MODDED item not loadable in the cloud container — can't verify its specifics (the "one paper" primary); generic alt-fire support should enable its tornado alt, but confirm in-game.

## 🔬 FULL CODE REVIEW (3 decentralized agents, all findings source-verified; reports in scratchpad/review-{weapons,soldier,economy}.md)
NO crashes / NO data corruption / NO second dead-dispatch. Every feature wired + running; slot-255 discipline airtight; SP item conservation correct on normal paths. Findings = a bug list. Opus spot-verified C1 + hauled-item loss against code.

**TIER 1 — SP, high impact. ✅ ITEM-LOSS + WEAPON BUGS SHIPPED v0.9.28 (boots clean); REMAINDER = ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.29 (boots clean). TIER 1 COMPLETE. Shipped: (G1) AccessoryClassFit now live (equips armor[3] + GetTotalDamage; weight-3000 term active 1st time → accessory ranking SHIFTS, playtest); per-soldier pipeline latch w/ 30s retry + per-item WeaponDamage blacklist (one bad modded item no longer wipes army-wide flight/dash); dummy statMana synced (modded alt-fire/Shoot see real mana); POTION BUFFS applied to dummy (Ironskin/Swiftness/Wrath/Rage/Endurance/Regen/Magic Power/Mana Regen/Ammo Reservation/Archery/Summoning) + NEW SoldierNPC.Endurance so Endurance potion + Worm Scarf/Brain of Confusion cut damage (playtest). (G2) target-fixation fixed (PickTarget exclude → picks next reachable enemy instead of idling on a hovering bat); thrown soldiers walk to armory to restock + IssueGear fallback + 45s give-up (StateTimer's first reader). (G3) boot wiring self-check (asserts every WeaponFamily is dispatched-or-not-fired + sample Build ranges; logs "N families OK" at Info, Warn on gap) — would've caught the dead Thrown wiring. Opus hand-cleaned the Combat.cs latch shim (now uses instance PipelineOk). PLAYTEST: accessory re-ranking, the 11 potion buffs, endurance accessories, the latch isolation, magic soldiers' modded mana.
**REMAINING TO 1.0 (not blind code): Tier 3 MP (only if MP is a goal — designer plays SP), ART (needs human/reference), + designer playtest confirmations.**
- ✅ v0.9.30: Tier 2 polish — swing-at-nearby now faces the enemy it hits (SwingFacingLocked); melee holds at reach instead of overlapping the enemy + taking contact damage; shaken/routed mages regen mana at combat rate. + Manual/text refresh (Field Manual lists all new weapon families; armory tooltip real 1000-slot/120-tile numbers; "Unarmed" localized).
- ✅ v0.9.31 (boots clean, zero exceptions — VISUAL/FEEL, needs in-game playtest): three melee-visual/formation fixes. (1) SWORD ARC — SoldierNPC.Draw.cs DrawSwordSlash redraws the big vanilla swing arc (proj aiStyle 190) for Excalibur / True Excalibur / Night's Edge / Terra Blade / TNE / Horseman's Blade, scale ramps with swing progress ×1.1 (Excalibur's gold swing is back, big-sword sweeps read full size). (2) GUNGNIR/SPEAR — SoldierThrust.cs TryDrawVanillaSpear draws the item's shoot-projectile sprite at proj×sample scale (reproduces Main.DrawProj_Spear) instead of the small item icon; Gungnir proj 105 scale 1.3. (3) SPREAD OUT — SoldierNPC.Brain.cs idle/Hold soldiers now stand on evenly-ranked slots (rank via SlotFrac salt 0.47, slot = (rank-(n-1)/2)*spacing) instead of random wander + soft-repulsion clumps; 4.5t gaps tight / 11.25t Spread Out, squeezed to fit line half-width (12t / 30t), bob ±1t, walks back when >1.5t off slot; soft repulsion kept as ±2t secondary correction. New constants in LevyConstants.Brain.cs. PLAYTEST: big swords swing big, Gungnir full-size, Spread Out forms an even line.
- ✅ v0.9.34 (boots clean, zero exceptions): RAINBOW GUN FLOOD FIXED. ItemShots.PreShoot(s, type) replicates vanilla's one-rainbow-per-owner kill (Player.cs:43753) scoped per-soldier (SoldierProjectileTag.SoldierIndex == s.NPC.whoAmI); called in WeaponUse.FireMagic BEFORE any spawn path (so the FireSpread fallback can't bypass it). No-ops for every non-RainbowFront shot. RainbowBack (251) inherits the tag via OnSpawn so it dies with its front. Result: each Rainbow Gun soldier keeps exactly 1 rainbow (refreshed per shot, vanilla behavior); the player's rainbows (SoldierIndex -1) and other soldiers' are untouched; global projectile pool no longer floods → everyone's deleted weapons return. SIBLING CAPS listed by agent, NOT fixed (judged safe/unverified): Proximity Mine Launcher (item 760, per-owner cap 20 — mines have finite timeLeft + detonate, don't accumulate unbounded; a naive port would wrongly cull the player's mines, needs per-soldier scoping if ever done); Mystic Coil (item 4262, kills prior snake — lifetime untraced, check in-game if soldiers wield it). PLAYTEST: 2-3 Rainbow Gun soldiers + fire your own Rainbow Gun — projectile count stays low/flat, your rainbow persists, army DPS recovers. UNBLOCKS the Mothron boss-range test.
- (archived) 🐞 RAINBOW GUN FLOOD — CRITICAL. A Rainbow Gun (ItemID 1260) soldier spawns a persistent rainbow (proj 250 RainbowFront + 251 RainbowBack) every magic shot and never removes its prior one → unbounded accumulation → EXHAUSTS Terraria's global ~1000-projectile cap → every OTHER projectile (player's + all soldiers') is silently deleted on spawn. Confirmed in Mothron clip: 3 Rainbow Gun soldiers (Hikari/Hazel/Scarlett) flooding, army DPS cratered to 35-152 mid-boss because real weapons can't spawn. ROOT CAUSE: vanilla Player.ItemCheck_Shoot (Player.cs:43753-43762) KILLS the owner's existing 250/251 before spawning a new rainbow ("one rainbow per owner") — the soldier spawn path (ItemShots/WeaponUse) skips this. FIX (agent): replicate the kill-prior rule PER-SOLDIER in ItemShots (scope by SoldierProjectileTag.SoldierIndex so each rainbow-gun soldier keeps exactly 1 rainbow; never touch the player's or other soldiers' rainbows). Also scan ItemCheck_Shoot for sibling capped weapons the soldier path bypasses. Fixing the flood fixes all 3 reported symptoms (multiple rainbows / AI spam / everyone's weapons deleted). Blocks the Mothron boss-range test (unobservable until the flood is gone).
- ✅ v0.9.33 (boots clean, zero exceptions — TARGETING + FEEL, needs playtest): two parallel-agent changes. (A) MELEE-BEAM → NEAREST. FiresProjectileMelee weapons (Terra Blade/Death Sickle/Ice Sickle/Starfury/Beam Sword — broadswords that launch the item's own projectile, position as gunners) now fire at the CLOSEST hostile instead of attacker-priority. SoldierTargeting.PickTarget gains `preferNearest` (ranks all candidates equal so distance decides); passed when Weapon.FiresProjectileMelee at the acquire + KeepRoutedTarget + boss-reach-fallback calls; retention re-picks a nearer target only when it's <0.85× the current distance (NearestSwitchRatio hysteresis, no flip-flop). Focus-fire still overrides (branch order). Non-beam families byte-identical. (B) WING ACCELERATION. Fly() now ramps from FlySpeed up to a wing-scaled peak over 75 ticks (1.25s) of continuous flight; peak = clamp(1.3 + (wingRun−6.25)×0.12, 1.0, 1.8) where wingRun = Dummy.GetWingStats(wingsLogic).AccRunSpeedOverride (real per-wing horizontal speed; Fledgling 3→no ramp, starter 6.25→1.3×, Solar/Nebula 9→~1.63×, ~9.8px/tick ≈ player's own wing speed). FlySpeed is the FLOOR (no-wings/weak-wings unchanged). Bonus fades to zero over the last 200px to goal so arrival/stop is exactly as before; ramp resets at every _flying=false site (grounded/no-goal/arrived/non-fly-else/teleport). flyUp climb gets 35% of the bonus. New constants in LevyConstants.Movement.cs. PLAYTEST: (A) do beam-swords cut the nearest thing now; (B) do winged soldiers visibly build speed across an open sky and still land cleanly — watch for end-of-traverse overshoot or start-of-ramp twitch, tune FlyRampPeakMaxMult/FlyRampArriveDampPx if needed.
- 📹 PLAYTEST LOG 2026-10-20 (post-mech solar eclipses, 2 clips): army HOLDS the line clean — "100th Frankenstein" + "100th Creature from the Deep" milestones, DPS peaks ~1800-2300, composition scales with player gear (Excaliburs, Megashark, Ice Sickle, Shadowflame, Tome of Infinite Wisdom). Designer: "they have so much fuckin aura." Out-of-ammo chatter ("I'm out! Does anyone have a bullet? A pebble? An encouraging word?" / "Empty! I'd say sorry, but mostly I'm just empty.") surfaces the finite-ammo loop AS COMEDY — working as intended, designer likes it. NOTE: eclipses have no boss until Mothron (post-Plantera), so v0.9.32 boss-range standoff is STILL UNVERIFIED in a real fight — needs a Mothron/mech/Plantera/Golem fight to confirm ranged units hold back and plink instead of crowding. CORRECTION: Reapers (and the Death Sickle) spawn POST-MECH, not post-Plantera — designer corrected my earlier wrong note. So progression is NOT confirmed past Plantera and Mothron is NOT necessarily live yet. Boss-range test still needs a real boss fight (a mech re-summon works too).
- ✅ GUARD STANCE RESOLVED — NOT NEEDED. Designer confirmed in a live solar-eclipse fight (v0.9.29): "the agro definitely works." Emergent tanking (Flesh Knuckles + the fixed aggro/de-aggro) delivers it; a dedicated Guard order would be a button for something that already happens. Dropped from 1.0 scope.
- Remaining Tier 2 (DEFERRED, low value, designer happy with feel): centre-Y-goal bunny-hop ROOT cause (patched by the goal-jump debounce — leave unless it resurfaces), dash-overshoot, dead-code cleanup (SoldierGear.Dyes sent-for-nothing, Memorial, dead constants/methods), perf footguns (bounded — held an eclipse fine), old-save-0-mana, GiveAmmo maxStack.
- ✅ FIXED v0.9.28 (item-loss): DropCarriedItems on both despawn paths; desertion returns the whole kit (deserter leaves unarmed, keeps chat line); militia kits persisted to world save + Mobilized cleared on load (survives crash/dedicated); protected spill (400-cap safe); audited every Gear.Clear/Remove/active=false in both system files. Hauled items still lost on a hard CRASH mid-haul (runtime-only, vanilla doesn't save ground items either) — documented, needs a save-format change to fix.
- ✅ FIXED v0.9.28 (weapons): base-safe blast guard (gun rockets/mininuke/cluster no longer destroy tiles, gated on soldier-shot+explosive); thrown-knife flight model corrected (reach at range); Medusa clamped to ~18t; secondary projectiles (Beenade/cluster/Molotov) tagged + npcProj so loot credits the soldier; Zenith anchors on dedicated servers; ThrownMelee gets the utility sidearm; Vampire Knives score + HeldWeapon score factor (0.4, tune); OwnerLockProbe covers Thrown.
- **ITEM LOSS (SP) [FIXED above]:** (a) hauled items deleted on despawn — RosterSystem.DespawnBody(:195-203) + SettlementSystem.DismissMilitiaBody(:395-411) flip active=false without DropCarriedItems [VERIFIED]. (b) desertion deletes kit (RosterSystem.Desert :919-932) — return it, keep flavor. (c) militia kits lost on crash/dedicated shutdown + Mobilized stuck true (PreSaveAndQuit only runs SP Save&Quit) — persist militia kits in SaveWorldData. (d) thrown-empty→weaponless dead-end (RestockThrown clears type w/o re-issuing). (e) DropAllGear lacks Spill(protect) (>400 ground items), Discharge empty-box clears w/o drop.
- **AccessoryClassFit DEAD** (all 3 reviews CONFIRMED + VERIFIED): ArmorDoctrine.Measure(:206-233) never equips the `accessory` param → the weight-3000 accessory class-fit term is always 0; accessories ranked by rarity/defense/value only. Fix: equip armor[3] + use GetTotalDamage/CritChance.
- **Pipeline latch kills army-wide movement** (soldier M4): static `_pipelineBroken` — ONE modded item throwing in the dummy pass disables flight/dash/aggro for EVERY soldier for the session (one logs nothing). Make per-soldier + retry/blacklist.
- **Unreachable target blanks engagement** (soldier M1): PickTarget has no exclude → a hovering bat makes the soldier re-pick it + idle ~4s while reachable enemies walk up. Add exclude param.
- **Base destruction** (weapons m4): ThrownBlastGuard only covers Thrown-family types → a Gun soldier firing Rockets/Grenade Launcher/Mini Nuke DESTROYS tiles/base. Gate on IsSoldierShot + explosive instead of registered set.
- **Thrown knives fall ~7 tiles short** (weapons M2): ThrownFlightKnife uses vanilla's type-162-only numbers for ALL aiStyle-2; generic is (19,0.4,0.97)+terminal clamp. Soldiers open fire at 20t and don't close → outer third misses.
- **Medusa stalls at 40t** (weapons M3): Magic family range 40 but Medusa reaches 20 → casts at nothing forever (mana refunded, so stall not drain). Clamp the profile range.
- **Potion BUFFS don't apply** (soldier M5, SUSPECTED): NPC.AddBuff doesn't affect the dummy's stats (no UpdateBuffs) → Ironskin/Swiftness/Wrath do nothing. Apply a buff→stat table like StationBuffSystem. VERIFY in-game.
- **Dummy mana not synced** (soldier M6): Dummy.statMana stuck ~20 → modded alt-fire/Shoot hooks that read statMana misfire. Sync around shoot.
- **Secondary projectiles untagged** (weapons M4): Beenade bees / cluster fragments / Molotov get SoldierIndex=-1 → no loot credit + commander on-hit effects run. OnSpawn inherit from any tagged parent + npcProj=true.
- **Thrown soldiers can't walk to armory to restock** (soldier M2/M3, economy #5/#6): dry thrown soldier never routes to an armory (only restocks if within 3t by luck). Add a dryThrown resupply reason + Resupply to UsesPath + a give-up timeout.

**WoF clip "le grand armé versus the wall" (2026-10-20) — RESOLVED, LEAVE AS-IS (designer's call).** Army BEAT the Wall of Flesh on the hellbridge (hardmode gate), magic-heavy, line held across the whole Underworld. The casualties were NOT melee charging in (my first guess was wrong) — designer corrected: soldiers were correctly FLEEING the wall, but the WoF outruns infantry (it accelerates as its HP drops) and runs the stragglers over from behind. Designer chose to leave it: an infantry army getting outrun by the WoF is thematically fine (the vanilla answer is a long bridge + mobility, which is the player's job, not the soldiers'). NO fix — dropped the "melee fights from arm's length" idea entirely.

**AMMO WASTE — NOT A BUG, designer finds it funny, LEFT AS-IS.** Pivoted away from the aim-lead/hold-fire fix entirely (never written). See ranged-range extension below instead.

**✅ v0.9.32 — RANGED SOLDIERS REACH FURTHER (boots clean, zero exceptions — FEEL, needs playtest).** Designer asked: open fire a touch past max range, and ranged units fight from longer range, especially vs bosses. All power-UP, no nerf (fire rate/damage untouched; melee/whip/yoyo/flail/spear/summon/sentry untouched). Constants in LevyConstants.Weapons.cs: RangedRangeMult 1.15 (gun/magic overall bump), RangedFireRangeBuffer 1.10 (open fire a touch past listed range — all ranged families incl. thrown/held-ranged, excl. boomerang/thrownmelee), RangedBossRangeMult 1.75 gun / 1.5 magic / 1.25 bow (bow lower: arrow drop caps ballistic range). Numbers: gun 72→83, fires ~91t; magic 40→46, ~51t; bow ~59t. Vs boss: gun reach ~145t standoff ~43t, magic ~69t/~21t, bow ~67t/~20t. Boss detection LevyUtil.IsBossNpc (boss || ShouldBeCountedAsBoss || worm-segment realLife root boss). CRITICAL coupling verified: BossRangeMult flows through RangedGoal range → StandoffTiles → KeepAway/approach, FindEngageSpot's HoldMaxTiles (KiteMax 64 cap scaled by mult so gunners don't walk up to a distant boss), TargetStillValid + FocusTargetIndex (boss held at long reach stays target), and TryEngage fallback (normal enemies in range still priority; distant boss is bossesOnly fallback pick). Flamethrower/short-lived-shot weapons get the buffer but NO boss reach (won't stand 80t back from a boss they can't hit). CAVEATS to watch in playtest: (1) possible kite-away from nearer adds up to ~43t for guns (bounded by ApplyChaseClamp); (2) a few magic weapons whose own projectile AI self-limits lifetime (Vilethorn-style) could slip the timeLeft filter and stand too far back — not audited exhaustively; (3) AimMaxLeadTicks 90 untouched, so at ~145t gun boss range it slightly under-leads a moving boss. PLAYTEST: do ranged units now hold back and plink a boss from safety instead of closing?

**(archived) 🎯 AMMO WASTE investigation (clip "the line vs overloaded slime rain", 2026-10-20) — SUPERSEDED by the above.** Watched the full clip: army on the Oakwall bridge vs a dense-but-sparse-at-any-moment slime rain (2-5 enemies "nearby" at a time, slimes falling from high). Soldiers pour continuous bullet/arrow/fire/magic streams that arc UP into empty sky and miss — DPS readout sits at 9-31 despite the volume on screen = many shots, few hits. Pvt. Xylia's flamethrower throws a continuous pillar at air where a slime WAS. This burns the armory's finite ammo (bullets/arrows/gel) for almost no kills. ROOT-CAUSE CANDIDATES (agent to confirm): (1) Aiming.SolveIntercept (Common/Weapons/Aiming.cs:21) leads with LINEAR targetVel*t — a slime rain target accelerates downward under gravity, so the predicted intercept undershoots and the shot sails above/into the sky (systematic miss on any falling target). (2) fire gate (SoldierNPC.Combat.cs:166-178) passes any target within Weapon.RangeTiles as-the-crow-flies even if it's high overhead / steep / tiny+fast = low P(hit); no "commit only when the shot will land" discipline. (3) flamethrower/short-cone (Extended) may be firing past its true short range — verify ShortShotRange actually clamps it. FIX PHILOSOPHY (tenet-aligned, NOT a nerf): make them aim better and commit smarter so they kill MORE per second while wasting far less — fix the falling-target lead (account for target gravity/accel, not linear vel), and have finite-ammo weapons hold fire on low-confidence shots (high/far/steep/fast) until the target descends into a clean band. Do NOT cap fire rate or damage. Touches Aiming.cs (weapons-agent-owned) + Combat.cs fire gate.

**TIER 2 — SP polish:** ThrownMelee not in IsMeleeTypeFamily (blade not summon sidearm); Vampire Knives scored ~4.5x low + HeldWeapon cadence over-trusted; mana regen treats Shaken/Routed as out-of-combat; swing-at-nearby wrong facing; centre-Y goals bunny-hop (real cause behind the debounce patch); dash overshoot + press-to-contact overlap (contact damage); old saves load 0 mana; dead code (SoldierGear.Dyes sent 8 items/soldier for nothing, Memorial, dead constants/methods); stale player-facing text (storage size, Field Manual missing new families); perf footguns (projectile scans, per-tick allocs — stagger _statsTimer). OwnerLockProbe not applied to Thrown (s2).

**TIER 3 — MP-ONLY (designer plays SP → deferred, real for 1.0-if-MP):** slot edit destroys real item (visual sends no stack); panels bind stale tile-entity (desync/dupe); full-TE broadcast per slot (Loot All = 300 broadcasts); per-soldier order buttons order whole roster; client records read "Not deployed"+stale HP; packet authority (free money, no range check); Zenith not anchored on DEDICATED server (SP/listen-host fine); held-weapon child dup-spawn on listen host.

**SUGGESTED GUARD (from review):** a ~20-line startup self-check asserting every WeaponFamily has a Dispatch case (or is in the explicit not-fired set) + a sample Build() returns non-default range — would have caught the dead Thrown wiring at boot.

**STILL OPEN — GUARD stance (design decision, NOT auto-built):** a new "front-line soldiers crank aggro so enemies peel onto them" ORDER. Held back because (a) it's a player-facing design addition, not a bug/compat fix, and (b) the Wave-1 de-aggro re-poll may already deliver this emergently (Flesh-Knuckles tank soldier + positioning). DESIGNER TO DECIDE after playtesting de-aggro: if enemies reliably peel onto an out-aggroing soldier, Guard is redundant for 1.0; if they still glue to the player, build it. (Asked the designer 2026-10-19.)

## 🛠 BUILD PLAN — ordered easy→hard (designer gave GO-AHEAD 2026-10-19; "get the easier stuff first")

**KEY CODE FINDINGS from investigation (so we don't rebuild what exists):**
- **Potion drinking ALREADY WORKS** (`SoldierNPC.Damage.Potions()`): soldiers auto-drink HEAL (<50% HP), MANA (magic/summon, <30% mana, `ManaPotionDrinkBelowPercent`), and BUFF potions from the armory's 2 potion issue slots (slot 0 = heal/mana shared, slot 1 = buff). So "mages should drink mana" is DONE — the friction is that slot 0 is SHARED between healing and mana, and a soldier only drinks a potion that serves its need, so a mage can't get mana if slot 0 holds Healing Potions. Likely designer just hadn't stocked mana potions. CANDIDATE small improvement: a dedicated mana slot (or smarter slot handling) so heal+mana coexist. NOT a from-scratch build.
- **Movement ALREADY HAS the bones for verticality+dodging:** `SoldierNPC.Movement.cs` has `TickDodge(grounded, air)`, `UpdateAerial`/`ApplyAltitude`/`Glide`, an `Abilities` object (WingTimeMax, JumpTiles, HasWings, extra jumps, rocket boots), and jump-to-reach-a-goal-above. So "they never double-jump / never take height / die to worms" is almost certainly: (a) Abilities not being detected from the dummy's accessories (extra-jump bottles/wings not translating), (b) TickDodge too timid (doesn't evade worms/wyverns/piercing), and (c) no tactical HIGH-GROUND preference (they only jump to reach a goal, never to gain advantageous height). → verticality/dodge is STRENGTHEN-WHAT-EXISTS, not build-new. Must read TickDodge/UpdateAerial/Abilities-detection/perch before briefing.
- **Militia default `Order = SoldierOrder.Patrol`** on a fixed-Y line between two settlement points (`CreateMilitiaRecord`). THAT is "walk in circles and die." Fix = default them to SearchDestroy leashed to the settlement.

**MOVEMENT DIAGNOSIS (read of SoldierNPC.Movement.cs — confirms "strengthen, don't rebuild"):**
- **Worms/wyverns/chargers massacre them because `TickDodge` only scans `Main.projectile` (hostile PROJECTILES).** Enemy NPC BODIES (worms = NPC segment chains, wyverns, chargers) are invisible to it → soldiers sidestep arrows but stand still in a worm. FIX = extend the dodge to scan hostile NPCs too, same closest-approach math, using the NPC's velocity + padded hitbox; key on CLOSING SPEED so slow melee trades aren't dodged, only fast/piercing/contact threats. [DISPATCHED as the dodge pass]
- **"Never double-jump": abilities ARE detected** (`MovementAbilities.FromDummy` reads extra jumps / wings / rocket boots off the dummy). Extra jumps only FIRE (a) in a dodge-hop (which ~never triggers, see above) or (b) to reach a goal ABOVE them (never happens in a ground fight). So the jump is unmotivated, not missing. The NPC-dodge fix will surface double-jumps naturally (kicking off the air to escape a body). Tactical HIGH-GROUND preference (Tier 2 #5) is the other motivator, still a separate Brain-side change.

**AGGRO / "enemies target soldiers" — ALREADY EXISTS (designer asked for it, didn't know it's built).** `Common/Targeting/EnemyTargeting.cs` detours vanilla `TargetClosest`/`TargetClosestUpgraded`/OldOnes/NonBees; after the vanilla player pick it re-decides, comparing `playerDist − player.aggro` vs each soldier's `dist − soldier.Aggro`, and redirects the enemy onto a soldier when it wins (swap slot-255 dummy for SwapAiStyles, or native 300+ index for UsesNewTargetting styles). Config `EnemiesTargetSoldiers` defaults TRUE. BOSSES / ShouldBeCountedAsBoss / boss parts / main-boss roots are never redirected (the boss rule owns them). Soldier `Aggro` is read off the dummy's equip pass (Flesh Knuckles/tank armor raise it, stealth lowers it). So it's a RELATIVE competition: a player standing point-blank with 0 aggro WINS and gets hit (the designer's wyvern case — he was tanking it himself). **Wyvern is Worm aiStyle → on the eligible list (NOT excluded).** DIAGNOSTIC TEST for the designer: put Flesh Knuckles on a soldier; if the wyvern peels onto it → system works, it was positioning/aggro; if it NEVER does → wyvern slips the allowlist and THAT's the bug to fix. Player de-aggro already works IF the accessory sets `player.aggro` negative (vanilla has few; a Mechanica one may use stealth the mod can't see — needs the item name). **OPEN DESIGN FORK (awaiting designer): tanking stays EMERGENT (build a Flesh-Knuckles tank corps) vs a new "GUARD" order/stance that cranks front-line soldier aggro so the army reliably draws fire ("hold them off me, I'll shoot") — my lean: the Guard stance is what he's actually reaching for, but run the Flesh Knuckles test first.**

**BUG — Mechanica Serpent's Spear locked the PLAYER out of attacking → ✅ FIXED v0.9.12.** Mechanica's `SerpentSpear` (Glacial Coil drop; converted Melee, classified as extended Magic) fires `SerpentSpearProjectile`, whose AI writes `owner.heldProj`/`itemTime`/`itemAnimation` EVERY TICK and never reads `controlUseItem`/`channel` — so it pins the commander (shots are owned by them) mid-use forever. Existing guards missed it (aiStyle 0, not channel). FIX: new `Common/Weapons/OwnerLockProbe.cs` reads the IL of a ModProjectile's PreAI/AI/PostAI (+ same-class helpers, depth 3) and flags any that STORE heldProj/itemAnimation/itemTime but never LOAD controlUseItem/channel; fail-open, cached per type. `WeaponClassifier.ClassifyCore` wraps the old logic (renamed ClassifyFamily) and returns Unsupported for Gun/Bow/Magic/Broadsword weapons whose own shot is flagged — mechanism-keyed, not name-keyed. Live-verified Levy+Mechanica: exactly 1/469 Mechanica items flagged (Serpent's Spear); all vanilla spears/boomerangs/Flying Knife unaffected; shadow sibling SerpentisOccisio (Broadsword) unaffected. SO: v0.9.12 benched it; ✅ v0.9.15 MAKES IT WORK — owner-locking MELEE weapons now route to WeaponFamily.Boomerang (thrown as a Levy SoldierBoomerang: own AI, borrows the item's shot sprite, returns to the SOLDIER, never spawns the locking projectile). Non-melee owner-lockers stay Unsupported. Live-verified: Serpent Spear→Boomerang; Serpentis Occisio→Broadsword, vanilla spears/boomerangs/Flying Knife unchanged. (Per the standing WEAPON-COMPATIBILITY RULE above: make it work, don't bench it.)

**TIER 1 — easy / contained (DOING FIRST):**
1. **Militia default doctrine** → ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.9. Militia default to SearchDestroy, roam-anchored to their settlement muster point, 80-tile leash (MilitiaLeashTiles = SettlementRadiusX*2/3). Brain was hard-coded to Patrol in several places (Roaming was IsRegular-only) — all gated on militia now; regulars unaffected.
2. **Potions** → HEAL/MANA ✅ FIXED v0.9.13 (draw from STORAGE too, dump-in-chest like ammo/crystals; issue slots still preferred). BUFF ✅ corrected v0.9.14: buffs stay ISSUE-SLOT-ONLY (NOT storage) — buffs are a DELIBERATELY LIMITED resource (the buff-slot economy, to be made upgradeable with progression). v0.9.13 wrongly let buffs pull from unlimited storage (deleted the limit); v0.9.14 reverts buff-from-storage via a `allowStorage:false` on TakeServingPotion. So: heal/mana unlimited-from-chest (survival), buff limited-by-slot (intentional). New ArmoryTE.TakeServingPotion(preferSlot, serves, allowStorage) + SplitOne; Potions() takes heal then mana (storage ok) then buff (issue-slot only).
2b. **Ammo-on-death** → ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.11. Removed the softcore half-ammo-pouch ground-drop in OnBodyDied; softcore soldiers keep all ammo (it persists/returns like the rest of the kit). Hardcore/mediumcore still drop the whole kit.
3. **Boot/wing particles + SFX** → ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.19. New `SoldierNPC.MovementFx.cs` (`TickMovementFx` + `DrawDashShadows`), called from AI() and DrawDummy — COSMETIC ONLY, zero movement-logic changes (reads private movement flags, writes only its own _fx fields). Calls vanilla's OWN `Player.RocketBootVisuals`/`WingAirVisuals`/`DashMovement`/`SpawnFastRunParticles` on the dummy via bound delegates, so every boot/wing/dash type + dye matches vanilla exactly: rocket-boot flame+sound (Item13/Item24), wing-flap dust+Item32, extra-jump OnStarted hooks (Cloud/Fart/Blizzard/Sandstorm/Tsunami trails, DoubleJump sound else), dash dust+afterimages (ported DashMovement; SoC type2 = afterimage only, no dash sound in vanilla so none added), fast-run dust+Hermes step. Perf: dedServ + off-screen skip, half-rate emits staggered by whoAmI, shared 24-emit/tick budget, all try/catch (fails safe → visuals off for session, never breaks drawing). Constants = `Fx*` in LevyConstants.Movement.cs. ⚠ MP CAVEAT: flight state is server-only, so clients INFER thrust/dash/jumps from synced velocity + visible gear (client hover not detected; client double-jump always plain Cloud). Exact MP would need a 1-byte flag in SendExtraAI (netcode, deferred). Single-player is exact.

**TIER 2 — medium, HIGHEST feel impact (the stick-figure drawing: cyan=mobile player, green=static clump):**
4. **DODGING pass** — ✅ SHIPPED v0.9.10. `TickDodge` now also flinches out of fast, hard-hitting hostile NPC BODIES (worms/wyverns/chargers) on a collision course — keyed on closing speed (DodgeNpcMinSpeed 4.5 px/tick) + contact damage (DodgeNpcMinDamage 20), predicts padded-hitbox overlap, sidesteps the lane or hops (surfaces double-jumps), brief flinch + cooldown, not a retreat. Projectile dodge unchanged. TUNE-IN-PLAY: 4.5/20 gate may catch fast bats/harpies; adjust DodgeNpcMinSpeed/MinDamage if they twitch at non-threats.
5. **VERTICALITY / mobility** — make high ground a PREFERENCE (perch on platforms/ledges for angles over the frontline, not stand at the enemy's Y), and actually USE double-jump / wings / dash to get there and to dodge. The double-jump is the visual TELL. Likely root cause: Abilities not detected + perch too shy. [investigate aerial/Abilities/perch first] — (4 & 5 are one coupled movement pass: mobility enables both.)

**TIER 3 — the summoner / melee cluster:**
6. **Whips** — more active damage + aggressive use + fix the visual jank (summoner's active tool).
7. **Melee summon/sentry sidearm** — carve the `!primaryMelee` no-sidearm rule so melee may take a summon-staff or sentry sidearm; summoner close positioning (beside melee) + anti-air/reach-behind-cover role.
8. **Sentries** — support the family (equip + simple drop-where-you-stand + periodic re-placement, same spot 2–3× sometimes; mass forgives).

**TIER 4 — bigger systems:**
9. **Bulk reforge-to-threshold** — expensive station/UI; reroll each item only until it clears a chosen modifier tier.
10. **Yoyos & flails** — new projectile-drive families (use the Flying-Knife PreAI pattern).

**TIER 5 — hard / lower priority:**
11. **Pathfinding** — the "run-then-jump" twitch (real bug) and underground-stuck (tight-tunnel nav; designer says low-pri, mass masks it).
