**✅ SHIPPED v4.1.0 (2026-09-07) — THE FLEET OVERHAUL.** Every item of the designer's fleet feedback, built by three parallel Sonnet agents (D: fleet ×3 + projectile pass; E: task forces + carrier; F: fleet stance + raids + readiness) plus two follow-ups (G: PD/grid fix). **Fleet ×3:** `ESC.POP_CEIL 92→280`, `G.fleetMax = 270·diff·fleetMul` (164→491 at General), every enemy-count gate ×3 (D's off-limits list applied by hand: `spawnSuper` scap 100/340→300/1020, `doWave` size `×2→×3` and `<78→<234`, `updEnemies` 70/74/130→210/222/390), readiness `noSpaceReach 40→100`, `noPdPierce 3→8`, `tutIntentBlurb` fleet estimate fixed. **Fewer, harder, faster (H):** deterministic script pass `scratchpad/task_h_pass1.py` — rate ×2.2 & dmg ×2.2 (rotary/flak ×1.6) across `UNIT_DEFS`/`UPGRADES`/`BUILD_DEFS`/enemy defs, DPS-neutral within ±2% on 10 sampled units, enemy line units +15% dmg; projectile speed ×1.6 (tracer/rotary/shell/rail/plasball/krod) ×1.4 (rocket/missile/microrkt) with `leadTime` PS table and missile thrust/turn scaled; `mlrs`/`hydra` 4-round thin fast ripples (`burstN/burstGap`, `fire(...thin)`, rocket ×1.8) — D's literal formula compounded to ×4.5 DPS, corrected to old cadence 4×9 / 4×8 (DPS 12 / 17.8, near-neutral); pelter left at ×5 by the pass, corrected to 76 dmg / 7s. Controlled bench: live projectiles 50.9→15.0 (29%), shots −36%, missile hit-rate 6/6→6/6. **Task forces (E):** `escortfighter` (rail 31t/3.52 after the pass, orbit ≤260px of `escortOf`, engage within 700px of parent, PD every .35s), `ESCORT_TABLE`/`attachEscorts(e)` called from the tail of `spawnEnemy` (bombarder 4 esc · hive 6 int + shieldgen · leviathan 2 gtank + shieldgen + 2 gunship · annihilator 4 esc + 2 frigate · carrier 6 esc; gate 300→900 so a full theatre never strips a boss), `spawnEnemy(kind,x,noEscort)` + `spawnSuper` index-capture fix (it used to grab the last-pushed escort instead of the boss). Bombarders: `openingBombarders(it, n)` now fired by `G._bombarderT=20` from `phase3Skyfall` (2 invasion / 3 glass+extinction / 0 decap+recon), `phase2Suppression` no longer spawns them; main gun `plasmaLanceFire` → `k:'eplasma'` (1400 px/s, 120t, r70) at `densestGroundClusterNear`, twin secondary batteries 9t/.5s/700, producer `['escortfighter','drone']` 8s, cluster pods on `BOMBARDER_POD_CD = 22` (was a 50% roll every 5-9s PLUS fabricator pods — seven bombarders put ~10 wide-area bomblets/s on the ground and deleted a 12,000-body drone army in 30s). `carrier` "FLEET FLAGSHIP SOVEREIGN" (4200t, armor 24, SPACE_Y+40, producer 9s cycle + pod 30s, 3-lance ripple, 4 batteries, PD, 6+6+1 grid, 6 escorts) via `tickCarrierArrival` 25s into phase 4 on invasion/glass/extinction; death: fleet −25% of max, stab +15 (replaces the +10), debris, `⚠⚠ FLEET FLAGSHIP` banners. **The fleet reacts (F):** `scanEnemyIntel` rolling `groundLoss` (`G._gSpawnLog` + `killsByType` deltas), `G.fleetStance` landing/orbital, two scans >.7 → `⚠ AUTOFLEET WITHDRAWING TO ORBIT` (beachhead troops `liftOff` climb 120 px/s and refund `G.fleet` at MESO, landings paused 90s via `G._landingPauseT`, wave `groundFrac .15`, super pool `{bombarder:3,hive:1}`, unfired `supersPlan` leviathans → bombarder), back to landing on `dominant==='spaceReach'` or two scans <.35 (`⚠ AUTOFLEET COMMITTING GROUND FORCES`); `tickFleetStance` (one line at the end of `updateInvPhases`) also keeps `2+round(diff)` picket `frigate`s in the space band from phase 3 and decays `G.front={x:lz,t:40}` (dashed FRONT marker + ground band in `renderGame`). `raider` (spd 340, twin rail 22t/2.64, double evasion, ≈26% of air rolls), `shieldcarrier` (flying dome projector, one per ≥8-air wave, dome follows via optional `dm.y`), `noFlagshipReach` readiness, manual ⑧ entries (escorts rewritten by me to match E's build), +4 tips. **G (after the bench):** `PD_UNINTERCEPTABLE = {rail, shell}` on all FOUR PD scans (annihilator, bombarder, carrier, escort fighter — the escort's own scan was missed by the builder and patched by hand); full 8+8+mender grid only for `e.wallGrid` bombarders (set in `openingBombarders`) and the carrier, every other bombarder gets escorts + the small pair; dead `ESC.OPEN_BOMBARDERS_GRID` deleted. Verified: tutquick, v41 (escorts 4, no grid on a plain spawn, eplasma fires, carrier 6 esc + 12 sats, kill → fleet 491→368 & stab +15, first bombarder at the skyfall, carrier at 75 = phase 4 + 25, 31 escorts for 5 bombarders, FRONT seen, stance flips, raider share .26), v403, v401 (test patched: `G.counterBias=counterBias(null)` before the null roll — the bias is cached per scan, not per roll), v3102, v3103b (errs 0), 0 pageerrors. **REFERENCE BENCH (`refdef.mjs`, land-only placement — earlier runs were confounded by units spawned into water drowning during prep):** BIG fortress (133 bld / 224 units / 4,000 drone bodies): 4.0.4 loses at 120s, 4.1 at 105s — the cliff for a mid fortress is pre-existing. R9 fortress (report_9 shape: 1,939 buildings incl. ~1,700 SAM sites, 1,988 units incl. ~1,330 Goliaths, 4,000 drone bodies): 4.0.4 survives 300s (fleet 0 at 150s, 262 units left, grind by 8 bombarder grids); **4.1 holds all 7 cities to 135s, fleet meter 0 at 225s, loses at ~270s to the ~100 producer-fed enemies that linger after the meter empties.** A bench player cannot build, repair, strike or research, so this is pessimistic — but it is a 4½-minute siege against a fleet three times as deep, which is what was asked. **OPEN, for the designer to judge in play:** (1) the phase-1/2 opening (73 busters + 400 micromissile bomblets r60) kills ~35% of a dense armor park (700 of 2,000 units) before a single hull lands, on 4.0.4 and 4.1 alike — the bench parks 1,330 Goliaths at one per 5px, so bomblets hit 20+ at a time; (2) after the fleet meter hits zero, ~100 enemies (bombarders fabricating drones/fighters, bulwarks, escorts) keep the match going and grind the fortress — the win condition wants a look (kill the producers, or let the meter's zero end the fabricators); (3) whether ×3 per-wave mass is right or the ×3 should live more in siege LENGTH — `fleetMax` and `POP_CEIL` are independent knobs.

**✅ SHIPPED v4.0.4 (2026-09-07) — CORRECTIONS & MUNITIONS.** Designer corrections to 4.0.3, in their words: allied BUILDINGS back to normal IC (only the package hire stays ×100 — "it's just the initial cost in buying the contingent"); building bonuses attach to the 45s contingent and STACK PER COPY (`deliverAllyClumpExtras` loops `allySupport()` counts; `allyContingentSize()` shown on the hire panel as "≈N units every Ns"); Falcon `rate2 .4` ("fire at a normal speed"). Munitions (the three existing bombardment-phase missiles): `fallTimeConst()` + a single falling-body profile for `buster`/`decoy` — `v0 = REENTRY_V0_FRAC(.25)·D/T`, one constant acceleration `a = 2(D−v0·T)/T²` for the whole fall, so total time matches 4.0.3 within 1% while it crawls up high (~55→210 px/s mean above MESO) and hits at ~400 px/s with ~0.75s below MESO (two earlier attempts were rejected: a 95/5 band split crossed the atmosphere in 0.35s — a teleport; a 65/35 split was SLOWEST in the atmosphere — the geometry has orbit as ~80% of the fall distance, so 'same total time' forces a short atmosphere phase); buster jink/flares only below `STRATO_Y()`. `bombpod` (the "triangular missile") splits at `STRATO_Y()+120` into `8+2·round(diff)` unguided `k:'bomblet'` projectiles (`spawnBomblets`/`detonateBomblet`, ±20° cone, gravity, `dmg 22t r 90`, `hp 3`, `interceptable:false`, hits aircraft within 18px, `ART.bomblet`, "CLUSTER RELEASE"). Opening micromissile barrage: `defenseBuckets()` (8 densest defended x's + 4 random live-building x's), `openingMicroBarrage()` from `phase1Screening`, `tickMicroBarrage()` phases 1–2 only, ground carriers (`microrkt` with `p.ground:true`) split at `MESO_Y()` into bomblets `dmg 9·diff r 60`; round-robin never repeats a bucket; measured 48–50 carriers / 384–400 bomblets at diff 1.4 (cadence ~1.3s because buckets die during the salvo). Tips: `drawTip()` shuffled deck `G._tipDeck` persisted in `localStorage.tm_tipDeck`; the advisor's separate `showTip(id,html)` renamed `showAdvTip`. Manual ⑧ gains CLUSTER BOMBS and RE-ENTRY PROFILE. Verified: tutquick, v403 (updated: armory×2 → +4, rate2 .4, building IC 380), v3102 clean, v400e's "1,2,3,4" assertion is stale (phase 3 is an event that hands to 4 in the same tick since 4.0.3 — same on base), 0 pageerrors. **REFERENCE-DEFENSE BENCH (`scratchpad/refdef.mjs`, the yardstick for "a hard win that doesn't end in 2 minutes"):** per city 12 SAM / 4 Aegis / 2 laser / 1 airbase / 4 railgun / 6 AA / 6 tanks / 4 Falcons / 12 swarms of 48 (≈130 buildings, ≈200 units, ≈4,000 drone bodies), General, no research, no rebuilding. On v4.0.3 it LOSES at ~240s: 73 busters in phase 1 (28 Aegis kill 32; every buster through deletes one building and one 175-hp district — 260 dmg), the fleet meter hits 0 at ~135s while 70–110 enemies remain (bombarder grids: railsat 24 / lasersat 24 / 5 bombarders / menders, unreachable by the fortress), drones bleed 4,000 → 0 by 250s. On v4.0.4 the same fortress loses at ~300s with a nastier phase 1 (buildings 170 → 84 in the first 15s). **Reading: report_9 wasn't "easy" — 4,436 units was past a cliff; below the cliff you are deleted in 15s, and after the fleet meter empties the space layer grinds you with no way to answer it. That knife edge is the "fragile balance" — 4.1 must turn it into a siege (task forces you can fight, retreat-to-orbit, the carrier as a space objective, slower/harder projectiles).**

**✅ SHIPPED v4.0.3 (2026-09-06) — REBALANCE AFTER REPORT_9.** Allies (`ALLY_CLUMP_S=45`, `deliverAllyClump`/`deliverAllyClumpExtras`, `allyClumpInterval` milbase ×0.75 floor 30, `applyAllyQuality` hp/dmg ×0.7 armor −2, `combatWeight` skips `u.ally`, `rushAllyType` deleted, `G.allyInfPen += a.inf`, `INF_CAP` floor 100, all allied `ic` ×100, flat extras armory +2 / depot +1 / foundry +1 / port +1 with no stacking, tips rewritten). Combat: Falcon `weapon2:'tracer' dmg2 6 rate2 1 range2 260`, bullet drop `vy += 140dt` above STRATO for tracer/rotary/shell; `phase2Suppression` (no EMP), `phase3AllOut` opens with warheads at `densestAntiAirX()` and `phase3Skyfall` 8s later, `G._bombarderT` 40s / counts 1/2/3/0, `detonateEmpBurst` radius `700×clamp(1−(hold−y)/hold,.35,1)`, shot-down detonation in `hurt()`, 6s fuse. **My two follow-up fixes:** `openingBombarders` (the v3.x "opening orbital wall", `ESC.OPEN_BOMBARDERS`) was spawning FIVE bombarder bosses at invasion t=0 — the real cause of "bombardiers came early" (11 died in report_9) — moved into phase 2 and cut to 2; the per-wave super roll had bombarder weight .5 from t=0 → 0 before 60s. Verified (`scratchpad/v403.mjs`): 7 cities → 35 on hire, weight unchanged, cap 350 for a 250 hire, 4 clumps of 8 in 180s with armory×2+depot, hp ratio .46 (=.66 squad × .7), IC 40,000/38,000; tracer drops high not low; no zones in phase 2; warheads at 50, skyfall 58.1, bombarders first at 18; tutorial/lessons/phases clean; 0 pageerrors. report_9: General/Full Invasion, FLAWLESS in 1:59 of combat — 4,436 units recruited (1,739 allied SAM batteries, 1,312 Goliaths), zero research, 83k IC banked, 270 buildings lost, air losses 490 of 530. Designer: "it feels like we lost the balance." Their calls (verbatim in the plan file): allies OFF the army cap (reverses the half-weight answer they gave minutes earlier — "they can make you unable to produce big volumes"); NO rush; one CLUMP every ~45s spread across cities instead of a per-city trickle; cap cost = the package's FULL influence price (floor 100); ×100 IC on all allied packages and buildings; support buildings give FLAT extras per reinforcement (armory +2 heavy tanks, depot +1 AA, foundry +1 bomber, port +1 warship), copies don't stack; allied units at 70% hp/dmg, −2 armor; Falcon autocannon → command-heli tracer at 1/s + bullet drop above STRATO; bombarders 40s into phase 3 and 1/2/3/0 by intent (11 died in that match); antimatter warhead detonates wherever it dies (radius by altitude — kill it HIGH), launched WITH the all-out wave at the densest AA (phase 2 becomes SUPPRESSION without EMP); tips audit. Two Sonnet agents (allies+tips vs combat+phases) on the 4.0.2 branch. **Correction from code, for the record:** allies were already counted in `combatWeight` at full weight; the report's "peak army 3,881" is a unit COUNT vs a WEIGHT cap of 672 — the bypass was the trickle never checking the cap, not an exemption.

**✅ SHIPPED v4.0.2 (2026-09-06) — ALLIES ARE PERMANENT + early micromissiles.** `hireAllies` → full roster at EVERY held city on hire + permanent `G.allyBattalions` entry (`tickAllyBattalions`, `battalionInterval`, `deliverOwnRosterUnit`, `deliverSupportExtras`, `deployFullRoster`, `rushAllyType`); `G.allyInfPen` only grows; `ALLY_UPGRADE`/`resolveAllyKey` (tank→heavytank→mammoth, aa→sam, strikejet→bomber, inf→shock, destroyer→cruiser→battlecruiser, arty→mlrs — I REMOVED the heli→helicmd path the agent flagged: command heli is a support unit, my spec error); new SPEC buildings `alliedairfoundry` (+1 bomber, needs an airbase), `alliedaadepot` (+1 AA), `alliedarmory` (+1 heavy tank, +2 ground armor per copy); `heavyport` +1 warship coastal; `milbase` interval ×0.5 (−15% per extra, floor 4s); support extras once per city per deployment (agent caught and fixed a 5× bug in its own first draft); modal: hire / ACTIVE BATTALIONS / SUPPORT IN EFFECT; `phase1Screening` fires `round(2×diff)` hostile micromissile carriers. Verified (`scratchpad/v402.mjs`): 7 cities → 35 units on hire, cap 560, trickle 56→77→98→126 over 120s, cap 560 after killing every ally, second hire → 2 battalions, 49→84 deliveries/min, cap 520; tutorial/phases/lessons clean; 0 pageerrors. Designer (angry, rightly): I built allies as finite per-city contracts whose cap cost lifted when units died; and I refused early micromissile barrages on taste. The design, verbatim: "it's basically the existing allies system we already have with a trickle over time that you can boost with special buildings" — hire a battalion, the cost is permanently spent, the battalion keeps sending units forever. Slice: same hire verb/costs; full roster at EVERY city on hire (dump); permanent `G.allyBattalions` trickle (1 roster unit per `every` s at every city, cycling; naval only at coastal cities); `G.allyInfPen` only grows; roster slots resolve to the best unlocked upgrade (`resolveAllyKey`, `ALLY_UPGRADE`); support buildings add units to EVERY deployment, stacking per copy: `alliedairfoundry` (+1 heavy bomber), `alliedaadepot` (+1 AA), `alliedarmory` (+1 heavy tank, +2 ground armor), `heavyport` (+1 warship coastal), `milbase` (interval ×0.5, −15% per extra, floor 4s); RUSH kept; modal restructured; phase 1 gets `round(2×diff)` hostile micromissile carriers. **Two design lessons for the handoff:** (1) when the designer describes a system, restate it in THEIR words before building — I twice substituted my own model (contracts, then tiers); (2) never override an explicit designer ask on taste — flag the concern, build the ask.

**✅ SHIPPED v4.0.1 (2026-09-06) — THE FLEET ADAPTS.** `scanEnemyIntel()` (~5143) re-scans at 15s then every 45s (`G._intelNextT`), computes `dominant` (normalised `aa/40, lasers/6, infantry/25, robotic/30, navy/10, spaceReach/40`; ≥.8 and ≥1.4× runner-up). **Two scan bugs the agent found and I made it fix before merging:** naval hulls defaulted to `ceil 0` via `defaultCeil` → every navy read as "anti-orbital guns" (now spaceReach = explicit `ceil===0` or rod/rail only); drone swarms read as `aa` (now drones count ONLY under `robotic`, by `swarmBodies`). `counterBias(dominant)` → `G.counterBias {kindWeights, groundWeights, hijackMul, extraSalvos, delaySpaceSupers, nearWater, colMul}`; `waveAirKind()` is now a WEIGHTED table (`waveAirBaseWeights`/`weightedPick`, reproduces the old ladder within ±10% at null); `doWave` ground roll + `phase3AllOut` use `GROUND_BASE_WEIGHTS` (phase 3 can now roll earthbreakers); `G.supersPlan` per-match copy (INTENT never mutated), `densestSpaceReachX()` steers delayed bosses ≥900px away; `pickLeastDamagedZones` `nearWater` tiebreak; `⚠ AUTOFLEET ADAPTING` banner once per change; `G.stats.adaptations`; manual paragraph. Warhead: `ART.eempwarhead`, `EKIND_LABEL` for empburst/decoy/shockwave, violet sparkle trail (fullFx), first-per-phase toast, `G.empFlash` tint, salvos = 1 + extraSalvos. Verified on the merged branch: all six dominants (lasers/robotic/aa/navy/spaceReach/null), bias bulwark .06→.14 (aa) and strikewing .12→.28 (lasers), phases 1→2→(3)→4, tutorial, wave-1 integration, 0 pageerrors. **Designer knob:** the 1.4× dominance threshold — "the fleet reads me" vs "the fleet punishes any specialization". Designer asked how the phases work and pointed at the gap: "the autofleet is supposed to adapt — analyze your heaviest fleet elements and find the best counters." Verified: `empburst` IS a physical shootable slow-falling warhead (detonates at STRATO) but reuses the bombarder sprite and is never announced; one micromissile salvo per zone at detonation, none earlier; **`G.enemyIntel` (scan at invTime 15) was never consumed.** Slice: `eempwarhead` art + label + "shoot it down" toast + violet tint/SFX on detonation; periodic re-scan (15s, then every 45s) → `dominant` (normalised, ≥1.4× the runner-up) → `counterBias()` consumed by a WEIGHTED `waveAirKind()`, `doWave` ground roll, `phase3AllOut`: lasers→strikewings (shields bounce guns/lasers), aa→bulwarks/frigates + micromissile saturation, robotic→hijackers/jammers ×2, infantry→gunships/bombers/pelters, navy→whistlers/frigates + coastal landings, spaceReach→space supers delayed +40s and placed away from the kill zone + heavier ground landings; per-match `G.supersPlan` copy (never mutate `INTENT`); `banner('AUTOFLEET ADAPTING')` + a naming toast once per dominance change; `G.stats.adaptations`. One Sonnet agent on the 4.0.0 branch.

Render (software GL, "not worse" only): 2.74/4.79, 1.30/2.03, 1.47/2.00, 1.79/1.75. Survivability @500 orders, 3-run avg (bodies alive / kills): 30s 870/46 vs 1026/45 · 45s 376/103 vs 243/95 · 60s 155/138 vs 138/107 · 90s 0/164 vs 0/131 — swarms lose slightly faster in the first 30s (dense clouds eat AoE) then outlast and out-kill (+25%). "Old shape" = `G._noSwarmMerge` on the new build (1 unit/order, 3 bodies, per-unit AI); designer accepted that as sufficient over a byte-exact v3.102.0 A/B. **✅ SHIPPED v3.103.0 (2026-09-04) — DRONE SWARMS + ECM HIJACKER.** `SQUAD_LOADOUT.cdrone {swarm:true, nMax:48}`; `spawnSquad(key,d,x,nBodies,noMerge)` merges within 260px; helpers `swarmSlotOffset/swarmBlobRadius/swarmBodies/swarmPerBodyHp/swarmAddBodies`; **`spawnUnit` now RETURNS the unit** (fixed latent `G.units[length-1]` grabs in `spawnGarrison`/`carrierLaunch`, both pass `noMerge`); `unitWeight` = bodies×.35/3; `acquireEnemyMulti` (claim-aware via `claimOf/engOf/ENG_CAP`, weighted reservations, `SWARM_BODIES_PER_TARGET=2`, range widened by blob radius, per-body `acquireEnemy` fallback); `SQUAD_SEP_MAX_N=16`; `updSwarmSupport(dt)` = regrowth (`SWARM_REGROW_R=420`, 1/4s, `round(d.ic/3)` IC) + hijack fields; swarms exempt from Massed Air Rally staging (`AIR_RALLY_COUNT` loiter was the dominant idle-body cause — real root cause found by the agent under instrumentation). Enemy kind **`hijacker`** (NOT `jammer` — that name was already a live EW enemy): `hijackR:820, spd:340`, stand-off `HIJACKER_STANDOFF=560` just outside cdrone/Hornet range (drones literally cannot shoot it — SAMs/interceptors are the answer), 1.8s `noTarget` arrival grace, halves swarm rate, 15% of bodies (1-6) hijacked per 6s → hostile `drone`s with `hijacked:true`; picker via `playerDroneWeight()` ≥12 → 5%, ≥40 → 10%. **Verification saga (worth remembering):** my synthetic bench (100 parked enemies, 30s) showed a persistent 20-30% kill deficit through two fix rounds — but its curves were flat after 5s (it measured only the opening burst against whatever was in reach) and unmerged swung 30-50 on identical placement. The decisive test is `scratchpad/v3103b.mjs`: a REAL full invasion, 3,000 bodies each config, cities/stability made immune, 3 runs × 120s → merged kills 234.7 vs 199 (×1.18), fleet damage 103.7 vs 59.3, 3.2× faster, 0 pageerrors. Lesson: bench the real game, not a diorama. Deviations from spec accepted: hijacker range/speed/stand-off (spec values were non-functional — it died to the alpha strike), jam decay 1:1. Designer: drones OP + the easiest way to lag the game, but must NOT be less effective. Root cause: drones dodge every cost at once (230 IC/12 MP for 3 bodies, `droneBatch` +3, `droneNoMp`, EXEMPT from air cap via `airUsed`, weight .35) → the only IC sink → 3,000 units. Decision: keep every number, change what a drone IS — a SWARM is one unit with a growable body count (≤48; merge-on-delivery within 260px; per-body HP fixed; blob formation; 6 distinct targets per swarm; LOD pips + ×n), regrows bodies on IC upkeep (1/4s, ~77 IC) within 420px of an Assembly Line (the real cost), countered by a new autofleet **JAMMER** (mesosphere, shield, `jamR` 240: halves swarm fire rate, hijacks 15% of bodies every 6s → they spawn as enemy drones; appears from invTime ≥ 60 only when player drone weight ≥ 12, 5-10% of picks — adaptive, never a cap). One Sonnet agent; bench: 3,000 bodies ≤ 80 units, ≥3× ms/tick, kills within ±15% of unmerged.

## 🧭 DESIGN MEMO — DOCTRINES via PROJECTS + EARTH'S STRATEGY (designer + Claude, 2026-09-04) ★★ designer wants to KEEP TALKING before building
**Decisions so far:** Projects framework will be REBUILT on current main from the ROADMAP spec (the parked `origin/megaprojects-wip` @ 0af317b is ~20 releases behind — lift code, don't merge). Drones first (above). Doctrine trees = `kind:'doctrine'` projects in MUTUALLY EXCLUSIVE trees (HOI4-style), 3-4 tiers = `stages`, each tier's `reqs` = physically built infrastructure the doctrine implies; effects via `PFX.rule` → `G.projectRules`. **Tenet balance = the FLEET ADAPTS to your doctrine** (High Frontier → mass ground landings + early Leviathan; Fortress → bulwarks/busters/earthbreakers; EW → jammers + hardened command; Mass → glassing creeps in) — without this the HOI4 structure collapses into "pick the best tree." Open question: is ELECTRONIC WARFARE its own tree (Claude leans yes — "attack the command layer" is the obvious unified-Earth strategy nothing in the game expresses) or Machine Swarm's capstone?
**Earth's strategy, reasoned from the fleet's capabilities:** it must FALL through the sky (re-entry = a moat; kills before landing are free) · it's FINITE (fleet meter only goes down except Hive/Bombarder/harvester production — on-map, killable; ours is exponential) · it's AUTONOMOUS (no crew/morale, a command layer = single point of failure nobody attacks yet) · it LANDS PREDICTABLY (flanks/outskirts; landing = the disorganized moment) · its worst weapon is ORBITAL (bombardment/glassing → deny orbit or go underground) · it bets on ALTITUDE as cover.
**Doctrine trees (Earth's logic → gamechanging payoff):** HIGH FRONTIER (kill in the moat; ceilings/lift/+dmg vs reentry → Orbital Ring: orbital denial) · FORTRESS EARTH (make them come to the wire; entrench/garrison/shields/dig → Continental Redoubt: underground cities, glassing-proof) · MOBILE WARFARE (hit the LZ in its first 30s; speed/rally/combat drop → global rapid reaction) · MASS MOBILIZATION (8bn vs finite fleet; MP/cap/clones/conscription → Total Conscription, curing death; stability+dystopia cost) · MACHINE SWARM (out-reproduce their industry; drones/androids → self-replicating factories, machine society) · ELECTRONIC WARFARE ★new (jam → disable → HIJACK autodrones, capture harvesters → AGI Core; ties Hive Mind/Data Centers) · DETERRENCE (nukes in orbit; strikes cheaper/faster, uranium matters → Orbital Nuclear Picket; stability cost) · TRANSFIGURATION (bio/psi climax; posthuman re-arm).
**Gamechanging techs ranked by how much enemy advantage they erase:** orbital denial (bombardment) · EW/hijack (autonomy) · underground civilization (glassing) · exosphere interception (entry) · self-replication (reproduction race) · respawn/cloning (attrition) · terrain control (landing zones) · deep-core power (everything).
**Long-term (designer, "v6"):** a tile/map campaign — several rounds, defensive structures, a full campaign to kick them off the planet. Noted, not now.
**📦 BANKED 2026-09-16 (designer: "i'm not 100% ready yet to plan stuff out, so bank it for now").** After report_13 the designer said "this is a good time to introduce projects and start working on some to counter orbital units and create dedicated super units", then asked "don't we already have a framework?" — we do: **v3.83.0's framework exists ONLY on `origin/megaprojects-wip` @ 0af317b (never reached main; current `title.html` has zero lines of it).** Lift estimate: ~260 lines (`PFX` 8 builders, `PROJECTS`, `projState/countBuildings/countBuildingsNear/countUnits/projAnchor/projStages/projCurStage/projReqProgress/projReqMet/projFog/updateProjects/commitProject`, `switchResTab/buildProjects/projCardHtml…`, 35 lines CSS), ~85% verbatim; re-fit list: the tabs + `#projView` go INTO the `buildResearch()` template literal (~L10108), `updateProjects(dt)` beside the research draw-down (~L5894), `G.projects/G.projectRules` init (~L3423), one `buildProjects()` line in `tickResearchUI` (~L10203), rename the `grandarsenal` sample (collides with `BUILD_DEFS.grandarsenal`), add req types `{type:'comp'}`/`{type:'sr'}`/`{type:'orbcap'}`; `G.projectRules` has NO consumers anywhere — every doctrine payoff needs a hand-wired reader (model = the `gmod`/`bmod()` bus). **Anti-orbital candidates sketched (talking points, not decisions):** EXOSPHERE PICKET (doctrine, no research gate: 3 AMI + Space Center + 20 rocket parts → AMI batteries also hunt sats/escorts/menders, magazine 6) · ORBITAL DENIAL RING (edifice, Space Age: 2 stages, Space Stations + Mass-Driver segments near the capital, then 40 rocket parts + Rare Earth → +40% damage on everything above the thermosphere from rail/rod/particle/space weapons, +12 lift) · PROJECT ORION (forge: Uranium Enrichment + Space Age + 5 factories + alloy/rocket parts → a nuclear-pulse capital ship at the Grand Arsenal, `ceil:0` rail 240 + a fragmentation salvo that splits into 12 homing submunitions, the dedicated grid-killer; completing it triggers the fleet's RETHINK per the escalation tenet). Designer writes all prose.

'done' : 'orient'`; `window.tutFireLesson(id)` / `TUT_LESSON_IDS` for testing. Momentum HUD: `infR` = `+N.N/s ×M.M`, green→gold lerp, `.cell.inf.momreset` 600ms flash, `G._momPrev`; tooltip updated. `lessons` toggle in `TUT_DEFAULT`/`TUT_MODES` (heavy/guided/stream ✓) + Options row `tTutLessons`. Plan (designer approved all three recs): **A** momentum readout on the INFLUENCE cell for everyone (`+2.4/s ×1.8`, gold toward ×3, `.momreset` flash on a spend) + tooltip; **B** `LESSONS` array — read-only spotlight cards that fire ONCE the first time a rule bites, ≥25s apart, auto-pause via `_evtAutoPause` (not in turn mode): `cycle` (replaces "YOU'RE ON YOUR OWN"), `momentum` (≥×1.6), `momentumReset` (≥×2 → ×1), `manpower` (cap hit), `weight` (first heavy queued), `stability` (cap−8 or first comms/prop/radar), `garrison` (first garrisoner), `reentry`, `waves` (invTime ≥ 60), `firstLoss` (first city hit); DOCTRINE card gains "spend in one burst"; **C** `lessons` toggle in `TUT_DEFAULT`/`TUT_MODES` (heavy/guided/streamlined ✓, oversight/video/none ✗) + Options toggle. Verified headless at 1280×760 + 844×390 touch: 0 pageerrors, topbar ≤2 rows, no horizontal overflow, drawer opens with all 11 controls, readiness pill expands and never intersects `.objwrap`, tutorial hotfix script still PASS on the merged build; screenshots `scratchpad/s3101_*.png`. Designer forks resolved by my picks (all one-line reversals): 5 cells, Allies in drawer, drawer on right edge, single cycling speed button. Designer (with iPhone screenshot): "you're not able to do anything when the tutorial is active" + "so so so many things on screen." **Diagnosis (v3.100.1):** TWO deadlocks, not one — `.tut-dim` (full-viewport, no `pointer-events:none`) swallows every click under the verified-action steps; AND `tutCountB` only counted FINISHED buildings while the match starts PAUSED, so `done` could never fire (on phone: no spacebar, ▶ under the dim → total lock). Plus Esc during placement would SKIP the whole tutorial. Fix: action steps get a click-through overlay (light dim, card still interactive), unpause on first action step, count placed sites, no key capture on action steps; read-only steps unchanged. **Declutter (v3.101.0) — "what you WATCH vs what you OPEN":** always-visible = 5 cells (IC/MP/INF/STAB/ARMY; GARRISON+TECH ERA to a one-line strip), PRODUCE/RESEARCH/STRIKE, prep timer, BUILD, ❚❚ + ONE cycling speed button + TURN, minimap; ONE right-edge ▤ drawer (replaces the permanent 🏆 tab) with VIEW&AUDIO / HELP / MODES&TOOLS (Allies, Stats, Achievements, Readiness toggle); DIG/TERRAIN under a 🛠 TOOLS toggle in the build bar; readiness boots as a collapsed pill and must never cover the prep-timer line; `ghint` moved off the PAUSED text; zoom +/− hidden on touch. Nothing removed, only relocated. Two Sonnet agents in parallel (tutorial engine 8475-8491/8600-8740 vs HUD markup/CSS + readiness) → cherry-pick → verify → ship.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.100.0 (2026-09-02) — THE ONBOARDING UPDATE (six modes as presets of shared toggles) + TURN MODE + ABANDON SAFETY + FIRE CONTROL CUT + BATTLEGROUPS STOPGAP.** Three parallel Sonnet agents on non-overlapping regions, cherry-picked A→B→C, one trivial conflict (both A and B added `G.ach.altered`), full integration verified 0 pageerrors. **A (onboarding core):** `TUT`/`TUT_MODES`/`loadTut`/`saveTut` (`tm_tut`, `tm_noTutPrompt`), every-load `#tutPromptModal` (6 modes; fires from the intro's `finish()` on page load — NOT on `backToTitle`, which was Agent B's region; acceptable, revisit if wanted), modes = presets of toggles `handHold/readyPanels/diagnosis/hideUnimportant/tips/babyFleet` copied into `G.tut` by `startTutorial(TUT)`. **Readiness:** `READY_REASONS` (15 data-driven checks: why/fix drafted in a coaching voice — DESIGNER TO EDIT), `G.readiness={score,gaps}` ~1/s, `#tutReady` side panel (ENEMY SCALE from `INTENT` + GAPS + NEEDED/OPTIONAL checklist, hide persisted `tm_readyPanel`), T-20s `#tutDiagModal` (gated `G.tut.diagnosis && !G.tut.readyShown && prepTimer<=20`; CONTINUE / +150s / RESTART / EMERGENCY UNITS via `spawnUnit`+`allyLandingX`, `u.ally`), `unlockAchievement` now stores `{t,altered}` (legacy numeric = unaltered) and `openAchievements` renders `◇ assisted`. **Hand-held opening:** spotlight steps extended with `done/onEnter`; `HAND_STEPS_HEAVY/LIGHT` verified-action steps (plant→extractor→factory→infantry→bunker+SAM/altitude lesson→countdown→re-entry). **★ FINDING (Agent A): the starting base is NOT bare** — the capital pre-builds a Power Plant/Factory/2 Bunkers and every city seeds a Factory (+SAM mostly), so `done` checks baseline "did the count grow since this step began," not "does one exist." Worth knowing for balance/onboarding. **Baby fleet:** `curIntent()` returns a scaled copy when `G.tut.babyFleet` (mode 2). **Progressive reveal:** `.tut-hidden`, `buildBuildBar` category filter, `tutRevealTick` (thresholds = agent's picks: 2 econ→PROD/INFRA, 1.5× baseline IC/s→DEF, doctrine/strike thresholds→TECH/SPEC/STRIKE — tune). **Tips:** 4 new `pickTip` nudges gated on `tips`; 4 Options toggles. **B (controls):** `G.turnMode` (`tm_turnMode`), TURN chip, `[+1s][+3s][+5s][+10s]` → `stepGame(n)` loops `updateGame(1/30)` exactly n·30×, holds paused, blocks SPACE, sets `altered`; HUD badge. `#backBtn` → PAUSE MENU (Resume/Options/Abandon) → confirm (KEEP PLAYING default) → touch press-and-hold ~900ms. **Fire Control removed** (btnFC, toggleFireControl, manualActive/designateAt/clearDesignated/toggleManual/releaseAllManual/gunRadar, render blocks, updBuildings battery branch, keys, help section, `G.fireControl/manualGuns/designated`); `pickDesignated` kept as an inert stub because `updSquadMembers` (squad code) still calls it; 6 manual-gun `ds` texts stripped of the FC sentence; guns still auto-fire. **C (battlegroups stopgap, designer: interim):** leader/follower groups for `d.drone` + `d.clone` (androids excluded — judgment call), ≤20 per group, `bgOnSpawn` in `spawnUnit`+`spawnSquad`, `bgDrop` in death cleanup, registry `G._bgIndex`; followers reuse `leader._lodTgt` + `battlegroupFollowMove` (O(1) formation ease) and skip acquisition/AI; leader death promotes nearest follower; `◆N` badge. **BENCH (1,500 cdrone + 60 gtank, 20×updateGame(0.05)):** 63.5→39.2 ms/tick (−38%); `acquireEnemy` calls 30,079→1,871 (~16×); `updUnits` 716→355ms. Overall win capped because firing/separation still run per-unit by design (outcomes unchanged). Full battlegroup design in ROADMAP remains the goal. **Designer to-do:** edit the 15 `READY_REASONS` why/fix texts + `pickTip` wording + hand-held copy; tune reveal thresholds; supply the Mode-1 video. **Phase 2 queued:** video wiring, automation assists, gold/silver outlines, extra difficulty options, score-based challenge mode.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.99.1 (2026-08-28) — MAIN-MENU THEME "The Fire Rages" (first bundled music).** First real audio drop: designer uploaded a cleared `.m4a` (2.9MB AAC-LC). Committed to `audio/the_fire_rages.m4a` (had to open the deny-all `.gitignore` for `audio/`), played via a DEDICATED isolated `<audio loop>` element (`menuThemeAudio`) — separate from the phase-based `MUS.audio` so it can't conflict with in-game music. Starts on the autoplay-unlock (first pointerdown while `mode==='title'`), (re)starts in `backToTitle`, STOPS in `enterGame` before `musPlay('prep')`; respects the ♫ toggle (`musToggle`) + `MUS.vol` (×.85 menu factor). **Autoplay reality:** browsers block audio pre-gesture, so it begins on the first click into the menu, not at first paint. **Codec note:** AAC-LC decodes natively in Chrome/Edge/Safari/Firefox; the OPEN-SOURCE headless Chromium test binary lacks the proprietary AAC codec (`canPlayType`→"") so it can't play m4a in CI — verified the wiring instead with a synth WAV (plays/loops, volume .4675 at vol55, gates on mode+`MUS.on`, `stopMenuTheme` pauses+resets). If any real browser ever comes up silent, add an `.ogg`/`.webm` fallback source. This is step 1 of the audio overhaul; the rest (replace YouTube in-game → kill ads, more tracks, main-menu picker) waits on the designer's remaining files + copyright. **First use of the new PATCH cadence.**

**✅ SHIPPED v3.99.0 (2026-08-28) — STAT CARDS + AUTOMATED ASSEMBLY LINE + HOVER SFX.** (1) Unit stat cards now show army-cap **WEIGHT** (`renderProdDetail` STATS block + `refreshSel` unit card, via `defWeight`/`unitWeight`). (2) **Defense buildings finally show full combat stats** on the selected-building card — Armor, Fire rate (1/rate), Weapon name (`wpnLabel`), and Altitude reach (from `d.ceil`: "any altitude/orbit" / "up to stratosphere" / "up to mesosphere"), all gated on `d.weapon`; previously they showed only Integrity + a bare Range/Dmg. (3) **Drone Assembly Line → "Automated Assembly Line"** (`droneassembly` ~2994): keeps the drone perks (`droneBatch:3, droneFast:.6`) and ADDS `mechFast:.4` (mechanical-unit recruitment, consumed in `queueUnit` gated on `isMechanical(d)`) + `conspd:.3` (overall construction speed, plugs into the site-progress tick like Nanite Foundry); rename propagated to the `cdrone` ds + `automation` research eff text; new `mechFast` registered in bmod template + `BMOD_LABELS`. Verified: android build time 8.4s→5.04s with one built. (4) **`SFX.whum`** — soft low sine hover cue on `.mbtn` menu buttons (procedural, mirrors `whoom()`; `initAudio()` first). 0 pageerrors. **VERSIONING NOTE (designer, this session):** going forward use PATCH bumps (v3.99.1, .2…) for small batches, MINOR bumps for real feature drops, and RESERVE v4.0.0 for the Modularity update. (Semver minor is a counter, not a decimal — v3.100 is valid; we're not "running out.")

**✅ SHIPPED v3.98.0 (2026-08-28) — WEIGHTED ARMY CAP + WHITE-OUT CLAMP.** Designer-directed reversal of the 2026-08-20 "no gameplay caps on mass" ruling (see the section above for the full rationale — TL;DR: unlimited free drones/mechs/clones/capitals is a 9,000-unit 3fps/whiteout EXPLOIT, not the power fantasy; the fix is priced mass, not banned mass). **PART A — weighted cap:** `deriveWeight(d)`/`defWeight(d)`/`unitWeight(u)` (~L3313) give every unit a cap weight — drone/kamikaze family 0.35, normal line units 1, heavier hulls (hp≥180/300) 1.5/2, capital-class hulls (`d.capital`) 4-6 scaled by hull size, and 5 explicit superweapon overrides via `d.wt` (Colossus 5, Titan 6, Antimatter Dreadnought 5, Nanofortress Airship 7, Telekinetic Pylon 5). `armyCap()` now returns a WEIGHT BUDGET (`rawCap * ARMY_WEIGHT_MULT`, MULT=6) instead of a headcount, still driven by MP+Housing exactly as before, just rescaled. `combatWeight()`/`queuedWeight()` replace the old `isMechanical()`-exempt `combatCount()`/`countQueued()` at every enforcement site (queueUnit's reject gate, the Deep-Ore Excavator autospawn gate) — closes the exemption for EVERY family (drone/mech/elec/clone) and BOTH capital classes (ground/naval AND sky), not just Shapeshifters (`v3.91.1`). `isMechanical()` itself is kept (still exists, comments updated) but is no longer called anywhere for cap purposes. HUD (`hArmy`/`hInf`/status-report modal/tutorial advisor) now shows weight/budget. **PART B — white-out clamp:** `glow()`'s per-draw alpha ceiling dropped 1.0→.85, plus a per-FRAME soft budget (`GLOW_SOFT_CAP=220` additive draws, reset each `renderGame()`) — past it, further glow draws fade proportionally instead of hard-cutting, so a mass-death event can no longer stack hundreds of overlapping 'lighter'-blended blooms into a solid white sheet, independent of the unit-count fix. Normal-density fights are unaffected. **TUNED NUMBERS (headless self-tune, PART C):** budget formula `Math.round((12 + floor(mp/25) + capb bonuses + Σbuilding.econ.cap) * 6)`; at mp=900 + 10 Housing → budget ≈ 648-696 (varies with exact building set); pure `cdrone` spam (weight 0.35) walls out at **1,851 drones** (target band was 1,500-2,500 — landed comfortably inside); a realistic 230-unit mixed army (infantry/tanks/ships/aircraft/5 sky+ground capitals/2 superweapons) costs ≈306 weight, **~47% of budget** — affordable with real headroom to grow. Verified headless 0 pageerrors: both a queued drone AND a queued capital ship are correctly rejected once over budget; `combatWeight()` confirmed counting capitals (Nanoforge=6, Land Dreadnought=5.36); a 2,500-simultaneous-explosion stress test in one screen region produced 0 near-white pixels (vs. an unclamped reference of the same draws producing ~1% of the frame near-white); a live 10s real-time simulation with capitals+drones under combat ran clean. **Judgment calls (not designer-specified, tuned here):** the exact weight tiers/values above, `ARMY_WEIGHT_MULT=6`, and `GLOW_SOFT_CAP=220` — all playtest-adjustable constants, not load-bearing on anything else.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.97.0 (2026-08-28, main 3b362e2) — report_7 bug + effects batch (5 fixes).** From the first post-Sky-buff EXTINCTION loss + designer bug list. (1) **Aircraft no longer sink/teleport with no base** — the real bug wasn't the fuel-RTB path (that worked) but `rearmAircraft`'s no-base fallback never clearing `u.docked`: a stale `docked` flag permanently blocked weapon fire (all firing gates on `!u.docked`) and caused an instant same-frame `u.y` teleport to any base that later appeared (repro: y jumped 504→1310 in one tick). Fallback now clears `docked`, + a terrain safety floor. (2) **Big airships clip into terrain FIXED** — the air-movement altitude floors (`landY-16` clamp ~5794, `landY-28` engage ~5775) were tuned for a small jet; a Nanoforge (`s:18`)/Airship (`s:11`) hull seated 16px up had its bottom underground. Floors are now **size-aware** (`+d.s`): repro went from 247/500 ticks embedded (23px under) to 0 violations, ≥14px margin (Claude re-verified: −63px lowest, 0/400). (3) **Nanoforge wing = 8 jets + 4 helis** — found it was secretly launching **16** (8+8) because `capH`/`capF` both read `carrierN:8`; added `carrierNH:4`. (4) **Full FX restored, default ON** — new `fullFx` flag (persisted like `shakeOn`/`grainOn`, Options toggle) skips the count-based `_heavyLOD`/`_extremeLOD` cutbacks + keeps `fxHeavy()` false, smoke life ×1.8, particle cull 1300→2400. Rationale: perf is SIM-bound (render ~16ms vs sim 86-300ms at 5k) so the visual cutbacks bought ~nothing and cost the joy. Low-end keeps `fullFx=false`. (5) **IC income HUD honest under heavy upkeep** — the "a power plant is worth ~120 IC!" report was the intentional `IC_FLOOR` (+8/s, net can't go negative) MASKING a real deficit: with −120/s upkeep, 17 plants built one-by-one showed *zero* visible `icR` change (pinned at floor) while true net climbed −107→+5, then the 18th "unstuck" it all at once. Kept the floor; added `G.icRRaw`/`G.icFloored` + a red `⚠−107/s` deficit marker (with tooltip) shown only while floored. 0 pageerrors on all repros. **⚠ FLAGGED (pre-existing, design call, NOT fixed):** the RTB-landing glide path (`deckY=groundY(baseX)-5`) can still plow a big hull through hilly terrain *en route* to a base — separate from the altitude-floor fix; decide later (e.g. big capitals don't ground-land, or the glide clamps to terrain).

**✅ SHIPPED v3.96.0 (2026-08-26, main 9b00a40) — NOTHING VANISHES AT THE EDGES (completes the zoom-out render saga).** Designer, watching an EXTINCTION sim: "zooming all the way out derenders units, water, etc on the edges of your screen." ROOT: at `cam.z<1` the frame scales the world DOWN around the viewport centre, so the visible unscaled-X band is wider than `[cam.x,cam.x+W]` by `PX = ceil(W*(1/cz-1)/2)` per side. The VERTICAL cull in `renderGame` already widened with zoom (`_vhalf=H/(2*_cz)`), and v3.82.1 (terrain) + v3.86.5 (water) over-scanned their fills — but every HORIZONTAL ENTITY cull (all 23 in `renderGame` ~6574-6720: units, enemies, buildings, projectiles, particles, domes, auras, rings, cities, nodes, rail, sites, markers) used a FIXED screen margin with no PX term, so edge entities were culled the moment you zoomed out. (v3.86.5's note "units/buildings/projectiles ARE rendered at any zoom" was WRONG — this is the fix that makes it true.) Added `const PX = _cz<1?Math.ceil(W*(1/_cz-1)/2):0;` after the vertical band and widened every horizontal bound by ±PX; same treatment on the 4 scenery layers that pop at edges (`drawCloudLayer`/`drawTerrainDetail`/`drawStrata`/`drawRoads`). BONUS (designer ask): the full-map after-action snapshot now (a) captures the whole nation edge-to-edge — same fix, since `snapFrameCam` zooms to cz≈0.2 — and (b) CROPS the empty underground crust: `snapFrameCam` full-map `frac` .5→.66 (more sky, less dirt) + `captureSnap` restricts the source rect below `groundScreenY+150px`. Verified headless (0 pageerrors) by me AND the build agent: a differential RSTAT test with the camera centred at min zoom confirms both far-map-edge units, which the OLD fixed margin would have culled (`leftOld`/`rightOld` true), now draw (`delta=2`). Reuses the exact PX pattern `lodOnScreen`/`drawWorld` already had. (Test-recipe note: an edge-render check only works with the camera CENTRED on the map — an off-centre cam legitimately culls the far side; caught this arithmetic slip twice.)

**✅ SHIPPED v3.88.0 (2026-08-20, main 97dc258) — INTERCEPTION = AIR-CAPABILITY, not the `pd` flag (designer principle: "any unit that can attack air should be able to attack projectiles — a pretty big thing for me").** Replaced the `pd` interception gate with `canIntercept(d)` (has a real weapon, not a builder); `acquireIncoming(x,y,range,ceilFrac,filter)` now takes the shooter's role filter and applies it to projectiles exactly like acquireEnemy does to air enemies, so a shooter intercepts an incoming round exactly when it could hit an airborne enemy at that spot — range+ceil+filter gated. ALTITUDE PUZZLE PRESERVED via ceil (low tank only catches ordnance that descended into reach; high stuff still needs railguns/AA/aircraft/orbital). Applied to unit path (updUnits ~5370) + emplacement path (updBuildings ~5818); AMI/jet-CIWS-visual/manualGuns/tractor left alone; `pd` field kept for its other uses. **PERF WIN:** `G._incoming = interceptableProjList()` snapshot once/tick + length early-out; measured FASTER under bombardment (931ms vs 1648ms/60-ticks baseline — small incoming-list scan beats the old full-G.proj scan) and unchanged with no ordnance (early-out). Verified: plain tank kills a LOW buster, cannot reach a HIGH one; AA/SAM/Psi still intercept; priority 100/100. **BALANCE CAVEAT flagged to designer:** bombardment now much more stoppable → per tenet, scale up ENEMY buster volume if it feels trivial, don't re-nerf the guns. (This is the tenet-correct answer + loops back to the ENEMY REWORK being the real frontier.)

**✅ SHIPPED v3.87.1 (2026-08-20, main e613506) — Psi-Troopers gain `pd`.** Follow-up to the pd-priority fix, answering "what about ground units, not just turrets?" Roster audit: dedicated ground AA (aa "Hailstorm", sam "Aegis-AA", samsite) already have pd (and now prioritize incoming, v3.87.0); general combat units (infantry/tanks/shifters) intentionally can NOT intercept (preserves the altitude/bombardment puzzle). GAP: Psi-Troopers hit any altitude (ceil 0) but lacked pd → engaged orbital enemies yet ignored falling busters. Added `pd:true` (thematic: psychic warriors catching missiles; rewards the psi doctrine the designer plays; ceilF 0 → intercept at any altitude in range). Verified a real psitrooper kills a buster. **OPEN/OFFERED: extend pd to other any-altitude offensive units? Telekinetic Pylon (designer fielded 50), Antimatter Dreadnought, Shapeshifters(?) — awaiting designer call; general army stays non-pd.**

**✅ SHIPPED v3.87.0 (2026-08-20, main 73b8653) — POINT-DEFENSE PRIORITIZES INCOMING (the "why won't my defenses shoot the busters?!" fix).** Playtest: designer built ~20 AMI batteries + a Radar/Ammo-Depot aura grid (5.8x range / 40x fire rate) and STILL got bombed; "units ignore the busters for no reason." ROOT: ground pd emplacements (updBuildings ~L5807: Plasma Turret/CIWS/Laser Tower/Microrocket Battery/plasma Bunker) and pd non-air units (updUnits ~L5358) did `acquireEnemy` FIRST, `acquireIncoming` only as `!t` fallback — and during an invasion a regular enemy is ALWAYS in reach, so the unlimited-ammo pd net never intercepted; only the finite-ammo (4 rounds, 1/~10s) AMI batteries did. We'd fixed this for AIRCRAFT (L5358) months ago and never carried it to ground. Fix: both paths now go designated → incoming ordnance → enemy, for ALL pd (air/ground/naval). Verified: plasma turret with a buster AND a ground enemy both in range now kills the buster, ignores the enemy; normal combat unchanged. NOTE: AMI battery ammo (4) LEFT ALONE — designer-intended finite-magazine "does not shrug off saturation"; the real gap was the rest of the pd net idling. Tenet: the player's tools now WORK; if bombardment becomes too stoppable, scale ENEMY buster volume, don't re-dumb the guns.

CORRECTIONS from the same playtest (my match analysis was wrong on several): Mind-Forge needs antimatter RESEARCH not extracted antimatter (no stockpile); the 11% min-stability was the designer MIND-FORGING the opening volley (~50% wiped, 24% stab each) not research unrest; Space Age/Orbital Command taken for +IC/+cap/+dmg passives not railguns; the 157k banked IC was the MANPOWER ceiling (nothing cap-cheap to spend on → dumped into off-cap Shapeshifters, 407 of them) NOT slack — and manpower STAYS as-is (designer: "it's perfect"); the Radar+Ammo-Depot grids were deliberate multiplicative AURA-STACKING (tenet YES).

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.5 (2026-08-20, main 04112c6) — WATER over-scans at zoom<1.** Designer asked "what about the water on the screenshots? is everything rendered?" — sharp catch: `drawWorld`'s water-region scan (L1300), `drawWaterRegion` (wxAt→clamped cwx), and the ground-level dashed line / topsoil highlight / ground outline all iterated only [0,W], while the TERRAIN body over-scans `-PX..W+PX` (the v3.82.1 dark-edge fix). So at any cz<1 (live zoom-out OR the full-map snapshot) water + those lines were cut at the old screen edge (dry seabed in the margins). Extended them to the same over-scan with clamped off-map world-x. Claude VIEWED it: water renders edge-to-edge now. Fixes LIVE zoom-out too, not just snapshots. (Note: units/buildings/projectiles/particles ARE rendered at any zoom — culled only if truly off-screen; snapshots deliberately exclude only the HUD/minimap/vignette overlay.)

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.4 (2026-08-20, main 2836d75) — snapshots fit the ENTIRE map.** Designer wanted the whole map in one shot; the postcard was clamped to the live 0.83 zoom floor so a map-spanning nation only got a corner. Lifted the floor in `snapFrameCam` (`cam.z=min(1,max(W/(WORLD_W*1.04), W*.9/span))`) so it zooms out to fit the full span/world width, and center the ground line (frac .5, no clampCamY) when zoomed out so there's no black band. Claude VIEWED it: full horizon of mountains, sprawl edge-to-edge, balanced. Tradeoff: buildings are tiny at full-map zoom (inherent). 

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.3 (2026-08-20, main 5f6abcc) — POSTCARD FRAMING for AAR snapshots.** Designer: snapshots "would rather die than take a picture of the full map." Added `snapFrameCam()` (~L4394): wide pose from the bbox of live cities + standing buildings, `cam.z=clamp(.83,1,W*.85/span)`, surface in lower third (cinematic recipe frac≈.62, sky on top). `frame()` (~L7044) applies it ONLY on a capture frame (saves cam, pins manualT, renders, captures via the existing hook, restores cam bit-exact after). Claude VIEWED the resulting 'end' shot — proper postcard (sky+mountains+base+lake, dirt confined to lower third). Verified 0 pageerrors, cam restored exact, pixel analysis (top-third brighter than bottom = sky-over-ground). **Snapshot saga DONE pending the designer's on-phone confirm of the whole chain (v3.86.1 mobile readback + v3.86.2 end-defer + v3.86.3 framing).** LESSON (again): extract + LOOK at the pixels.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.2 (2026-08-20, main 2a8edb8) — blank AFTERMATH snapshot fixed.** Extracted+viewed report_4's images: MOBILIZATION(start) ✓ renders but FRAMED INTO MOSTLY-UNDERGROUND DIRT (base is a sliver at top — camera at ~1.2s sits low); FORTRESS(prep) ✓ great; AFTERMATH(end) = SOLID BLACK; heatmap ✓. Root of black end: `end()` still captured 'end' SYNCHRONOUSLY mid-updateGame (stale buffer, black on mobile) while v3.86.1 had deferred start/prep to frame() post-render. Fix: `end()` sets `G.stats.snap._wantEnd=true`; frame() captures it after renderGame (frame() keeps rendering the frozen battlefield after G.over — line ~7026). Verified: deferred end snap = 12.6KB non-blank, 0 pageerrors. **⚠ STILL OPEN: (a) mobile on-device confirm (headless can't repro the mobile-blank); (b) the MOBILIZATION 'start' shot is framed into underground dirt — needs the opening-camera/snapshot framing to show the surface/base — offered to the designer.** LESSON REINFORCED: for a visual bug, EXTRACT AND LOOK at the actual pixels — the base64 was there but 1 of 3 was solid black; byte-size alone was misleading.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.1 (2026-08-20, main c5aceb7) — PLASMA/MICROMISSILE/SNAPSHOT fixes.**
- **Physical plasma (plasball/plasarc) whiffing airborne targets:** TWO reentry gates — plasball's own proximity check skipped `t.reentry`, AND `splashDamage()` has a shared `if(e.reentry)continue` (deliberate reentry-vulnerability window for direct-hit weapons). Fix: dropped plasball's skip + gave `splashDamage` an opt-in `hitReentry` flag only `plasmaBurst` sets (other splash weapons untouched) + widened proximity fuse & matched blast radius for fast air movers. Ground behavior unchanged. Verified: kills a reentry bomber now.
- **Strategic Micromissile Barrage null-homing:** pool was built from `enemiesLive` (already reentry-stripped) → when nearby enemies were all `reentry`, pool AND fallback empty → 18/18 subs null. Fix: build pool from `G.enemies` + last-resort acquisition. Verified 18/18 home in atmospheric AND all-reentry clusters (was 18/18 null).
- **AAR snapshots blank on mobile (BEST-EFFORT, needs on-device confirm — works headless):** moved start/prep capture out of `updateGame` (read stale buffer) into `frame()` post-`renderGame` via flags; `{willReadFrequently:true}` + 9-arg drawImage. Headless snaps 12-14KB (non-blank). **⚠ DESIGNER MUST TEST ON PHONE — if still blank, need a fresh report + device/browser.**

**✅ SHIPPED v3.91.0 (2026-08-23, main b7be2b0) — EXTINCTION BOSS-SWARM.** report_6 analysis found EXTINCTION was strangling its OWN difficulty: `ESC.POP_CEIL` is 92, so the mass couldn't land (the 705-hostile peak was mostly stationary bombardier satellite grids), and bosses trickled one-every-42s via `extinctionSupers()`. Fix (designer-directed "super units at ~30×"): `extinctionSupers()` now schedules an opening 20-boss wave at t=2 + a fresh batch of 10→30 every 26s; scheduler forwards `sw.n`; `spawnSuper` gained a `mass = n>=6` branch (cap 100→340, bosses scatter across whole map, and beyond the first 2 each batched boss gets `openBomb=true` to SKIP its 16-sat grid so the swarm doesn't melt perf). Verified: 20/20 bosses spawn, 18/20 grid-suppressed, 0.6ms/tick, zero pageerrors. Verified: isMechanical(shifter)=false, 5 spawned → combatCount +5, android still mechanical, zero pageerrors. Unit itself UNCHANGED (tenet: didn't nerf the toy, just made mass cost manpower). **DESIGNER PHILOSOPHY BANKED: "nothing should be off cap."** Other still-off-cap units to potentially bring on-cap later (designer's call, NOT yet done — would be a bigger sweep): Clone Battalions (clone fam), Cybernetic Legion (mech+bio fam), Combat Androids/mechs/drones (mech/elec/drone fams), and the ground/naval CAPITAL-class ships (landdread/grandbattleship/supercarrier/warcolossus/mobilefortress — currently `capital && domain!=='air'` exempt, gated by IC/MP/upkeep instead). Garrison units are a separate exemption (u.garr). **DESIGNER MOOD NOTE: felt overwhelmed by the scale of a full psi rework; wants SMALL steps that keep the cool toys, not teardowns. New agreed psi direction = "focus on powerful SINGLE units" (few, expensive, individually devastating) — the EASIEST balance in the game (tune ~12 things not 2000; enemy provides difficulty per tenet). DELETE NOTHING; change price+headcount. Also wants psi to lean into: anti-AIR, preserving stability/influence, buffing others (= a SUPPORT/resilience tree, "keep society functioning while it's annihilated"). Mind-Forge still flagged egregious (instant land, out-damages any unit, only strike with no counterplay) — small isolated fix available: travel/charge telegraph + damage trim to "cripple a boss" not "delete everything". GO SLOW, designer playtests between steps.**

**🔬 PSI-TREE FULL RE-CANON — DESIGN SPEC (designer said "rework how psionics work entirely"; report_6 deep analysis delivered; AWAITING 2 decisions before build).** report_6 verdict: the run was WON IN PREP (full psi spine — Psi Studies 4:13 → Applied Psionics 5:25 → Telekinesis 7:04 → Shapeshifters 8:05 — online before the fleet landed; combat only 4:58; 213:1 dmg ratio). Every brake pinned at its rail untouched: **1.28M IC banked** (spent 161k), MP pinned 900, influence pinned 600, stability floor 65%, antimatter refined 0. **Shapeshifters = 77% of army (2228/2893), ZERO lost** — off-cap, cheap MP, allRegen-immortal, ~1150 plasma lance, any-altitude. 73% of kills were ground (altitude puzzle switched OFF: psi units reach orbit cheap). ROOT: psi is the ONLY tree that closes the whole win-loop alone (economy+altitude+survivability+global-buffs+panic-button); every other tree needs combined arms. **DESIGNER CANON (their book):** psionics = a DECENTRALIZED COMMAND STRUCTURE letting a SINGLE INDIVIDUAL be devastating + tedious to fight; only ELECTRALUMIKINETICS absorbs a uranium fuel cell to fire ONE beam to orbit; SHAPESHIFTERS throw things / turn INTO a (short-range) gun — NO long-range plasma lance. **REWORK PILLARS (proposed to designer):** (A) NO psi unit reaches space — strip ceil:0 from Psi-Troopers + Telekinetic Pylons; space = railgun/railship/missile-cruiser/particle/orbital/super-heavy/strat-strike only (designer's exact list). (B) psi = FEW & individually terrifying, not mass — Psi-Troopers/shapeshifters become scarce+expensive (on-cap or a dedicated psi-command pool raised by comms/propaganda/relays). (C) shapeshifters lose the lance → short-range/melee, must CLOSE (enemy gets counterplay). (D) −1% stability-cap per psi unit (designer's band-aid) — ONLY works AFTER (B); at 2228 units it clamps to floor = a hard cap in disguise = TENET VIOLATION, so band-aid + scarcity are the SAME fix (that's why it wasn't shipped in isolation). (E) add ELECTRALUMIKINETICS — uranium-fueled charge→one big high-reaching beam (gives uranium a real active use, fixes the dead-resource flaw); the ONE psi thing that reaches up; telegraphed = counterplay. (F) Mind-Forge counterplay — fleet intercepts your tungsten rods but NOT Mind-Forge ("zero counterplay every other weapon has"); orbital laser-grid should degrade/disrupt the beam (tenet-clean: smarter enemy). **RECOMMENDED FIRST SLICE:** A+C+B+D (the core re-canon that kills the 213:1); then E, then F. **AWAITING DESIGNER:** (1) scarcity model — normal army-cap vs a dedicated psi-command pool (Claude leans dedicated pool = explicit "build stability infra to field demigods" loop); (2) do melee shapeshifters stay immortal (allRegen) or lose immortality with the lance. DO NOT BUILD until answered.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.90.0 (2026-08-23, main 499d154) — PSIONIC REBALANCE (designer-directed, post-EXTINCTION report_6).** report_6 showed EXTINCTION (30×) as a 213:1 flawless walk, min stability 65% — psi + free Mind-Forge + ring-farm aura-stacking trivialized the hardest mode. Designer's two verbatim fixes, both tenet-clean (a real COST + fixing exponential math, NOT a cap/nerf of the toy): (1) **Mind-Forge `amCost: 200`** — antimatter caps at 300 and trickles from extractors, so you can no longer free-delete every wave; each strike is now rationed. `stratAvail`/`launchStrike` already read `w.amCost` generically (L5036/5056), one field did it. (2) **`auraAt` additive** — `m *= aura` → `m += (aura-1)` in all 3 branches; two 1.08 zones = 1.16 not 1.1664 (verified headless). Deployed. **STILL OPEN (designer in "keep discussing"): the DEEPER psi-tree dominance** — psi is the only tree that switches OFF the altitude puzzle (cheap spammable any-altitude Psi-Troopers ceil:0/150 IC + Telekinetic Pylons ceil:0/1150-range/560 IC) while ALSO being the biggest global-buff tree (+38% rof/+37% range to the WHOLE army) + best off-cap self-regen mass (shapeshifters + `allRegen`) + wave-deleter. **AND canon-flavor mismatch: designer says "the way psionics work isn't even really the original setting"** — psionics want reworking toward their canon (the four superhuman types + Psi-Troopers as the distinct psionic/dimensional fifth). Do NOT build the deeper rework until the designer confirms direction — options on the table: (a) psi global buffs apply to PSI UNITS only, not the whole army; (b) any-altitude reach costs more / is rarer; (c) elevate the OTHER trees + BUILD THE ENEMY instead of nerfing psi (tenet-preferred); (d) recanon the psi tree's fantasy to match the four-type lore.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.86.0 (2026-08-20, main 14486fc) — COMBAT FIXES BATCH (Mind-Forge apex / micromissiles / psi aim).** From a playtest debrief.
- **Mind-Forge → the god-weapon** (designer: "hardest upgrade to get, should be most powerful by an order of magnitude"). Column DPS 650→3000/s (~7500 total, deletes boss-tier Leviathan). NEW: airborne enemies in the column (incl. mid-re-entry/orbital) get `mfSlam` — dragged to ground (`e.y += 2600*dt` driven in `updEnemies` for robustness) and explode. NEW: scrubs incoming ordnance in the column (any non-friendly `interceptable||pdable` proj → killed) — fixes designer's "mind-forged a bunker-buster, nothing happened."
- **Micromissiles fixed** (designer: "vomit a stream of unguided rockets, not a burst of 5-6 trackers"). microrkt carrier `n:4→6`; split sooner+reliably (cluster radius 300→550, hard-split 1.6→0.8s, splitT .45→.25); guaranteed `homeTgt` via `near()` fallback; sub `turn` 3.4→4.6. ROOT: `weapon:'plasma'` is HITSCAN (`pbeam`+`beamHit`, resolves instantly) but `plasma:1100` was in the `leadTime` PS table → callers aimed AHEAD into empty sky at fast/high targets. Removed `plasma` from PS. Bonus: same fix corrects Telekinetic Pylons / Shapeshifters / Antimatter Dreadnought (all share the plasma hitscan). `plasball`/`plasarc` (real projectiles) still lead.

- **STILL PARKED (designer said "don't fully implement yet" — discussion, 2026-08-20):** (1) ambient boats float through raised terrain [trivial bug]; (2) the "giant wall of manpower" — one-axis ground + separation smears a big army into a mile-wide line; leaning FAKE-DEPTH (row jitter so a mass reads as a crowd, not a line) — needs designer's eye; (3) repairable RUINS — designer chose EVERY building leaves a husk (cheap ones crumble fast); repair cheaper+faster than rebuild, enemy can finish-kill a husk, ruin inert-but-holds-footprint; validated by the 106-buildings-"lost" run; (4) more UNIT-PRODUCTION-SPEED upgrades (construction speed is a nothingburger post-buildout) — fold prod-speed into existing conspd bonuses + add a tempo lever. (5) AAR "Buildings Lost" counter miscounts (nothing punched their shields yet reported 106) — audit what it tallies. (6) Snapshots STILL blank in the report despite the v3.83 mobile fix — re-investigate `captureSnap`.

**✅ SHIPPED v3.85.1 (2026-08-20, main 267e065) — MIND-FORGE BUFF (~5×).** Playtest: designer said Mind-Forge "felt really weak, didn't look like it was doing damage." **★ DESIGN INSIGHT — PSI/BIO WAS NEVER WEAK, IT WAS THE SUPERHUMAN BUG (playtest report, 2026-08-20).** Designer retracted the long-standing "psionics/bioengineering feel weak" worry after a ★★★ FLAWLESS General/Full-Invasion win fielding **90 Superhuman Cadres + 26 Telekinetic Pylons + 27 Psi-Troopers** (verdant society, min stability 16% — rode the −3%/cadre soft-floor to the knife-edge and held; 25 superweapons killed, 45 losses). The path was never underpowered — the v3.83 superhuman INFLUENCE-DRAIN LOCK was strangling it before payoff (the earlier ~40-cadre freeze-out). With the drain gone (cost on stability cap), 90 cadres is viable and dominant. **The real remaining note is LEGIBILITY/RAMP, not power: psi/bio backloads (commit to verdant + eat the stability floor before it detonates) while sky-fortresses pay off incrementally — so it FEELS weaker even though it isn't. Any future work is "make the payoff visible earlier," NOT a numbers buff.** Designer also loves psionics + tractor beams ("SO strong"). **RUINS IDEA VALIDATED:** that run "lost" 106 buildings (rebuilt 38 shield gens + 36 housing) — the repairable-ruins feature's exact use-case. (Note: designer says nothing punched their shields, so the "Buildings Lost:106" AAR stat is miscounting — likely demolitions/replacements/shield-gen cycling, not enemy kills — worth auditing what that counter tallies.)

## 🔬 AUTONOMOUS FACTORIES / NANOFABRICATION — pending direction (designer flagged, undecided)

Designer: *"clarify more what autonomous factories do? or remove it/rework it? perhaps do stuff with nanofabrication."* (Note the "?" — genuinely undecided.) Current state: `autofactory` "Autonomous Plant" (PROD, req nanotech) = `autospawn:'shock'` every 16s + makes IC; Deep-Ore Excavator's `gmod:{autoFast}` upgrades it to 70%-faster android mechs. Vague description ("passively makes IC and garrison troops"). **KEY INSIGHT (Claude, designer didn't object): there are THREE overlapping "a building makes free units" systems — Autonomous Plant (`autospawn`), garrison producers (`garrWorks`), passive building garrisons (`garrison`) — and they blur together. NANOFABRICATION is the natural unifying identity: "buildings that FABRICATE units from your industry," which the garrison upkeep-model plugs straight into.** So autonomous-factories + garrison rework may want to be ONE consolidation pass under a nanofab identity. NEAR-TERM: safe move is CLARIFY (make the Autonomous Plant's function legible + give it a crisp nanofab identity), hold the bigger rework for the garrison/nanofab unification. AWAITING designer nod on clarify-vs-rework.

Designer's framing directive (verbatim gist): *"frame it as 'the game will adapt to your gameplay style' and you can respond in the same way. there are very little arbitrary blockers, just a competent, dangerous and powerful enemy."* This is the game's THESIS and the promo's lead. **CONSEQUENCE: the enemy-adaptivity increment (queued) is now the PRIORITY build, because the pitch must be TRUE** — "adapts to your playstyle" requires the enemy to actually read-your-composition-and-counter (air→flak saturation, turtle→orbital city-ranging). Promo copy currently frames the enemy as "competent, dangerous, adaptive" + leans on the TRUE "no arbitrary blockers / the game says YES" thesis (the tenet as marketing); the read-and-counter mechanic makes the claim airtight. ## 📸 CINEMATIC SHOT-CAPTURE — SOLVED METHOD (reusable, 2026-08-17)

Designer wants promo shots + to launch YouTube Shorts for audience-building. **Headless cinematic capture now works — the method (scratchpad/cinematic.mjs):** boot to game; hide the TITLE SCREEN by `document.getElementById('ui').style.display='none'` (the whole `#ui` div = titlewrap+#menu; it does NOT auto-hide in headless); for a CLEAN shot set `G.hideHUD=true` (gates the CANVAS minimap/HUD text) AND hide DOM `['gameHud','buildbar','ghint','srBar','strikePanel','alliesPanel','footer','manualBuildings']`. **Camera:** `cam.x/cam.y/cam.z` (z clamped 0.83–3); auto-follow overrides it UNLESS you set `cam.manualT=99999` after framing. Center on world X: `cam.x=clampCam(centerX-W/2)`; vertical: `cam.y=clampCamY(groundY(centerX)-H/2-(frac-.5)*H/z)`. Freeze scene with `G.prepTimer=999999`; spawn units/enemies, run ~170 `updateGame(.05)` ticks for live tracers/explosions, screenshot at deviceScaleFactor:2. **NOTE prep-phase draws a "TOTAL MOBILIZATION" canvas-ish watermark — actually it's the `#ui` title DOM; hiding `#ui` clears it.** Captured 4 strong shots (sky/fleet+HIVE-boss+altitude-bands = hero; explosive close combat; whole-vertical-world scale; HUD-on strategy) delivered to designer.

## 🧬 CANON — THE FOUR SUPERHUMAN TYPES (designer lore, 2026-08-20) — CODEX MATERIAL, designer's own worldbuilding

Verbatim-preserved designer canon (their prose is the payload — the natural first entries for the wanted lore CODEX). The setting has FOUR superhuman types (Psi-Troopers are implied a distinct FIFTH — psionic/dimensional — not one of these four):
1. **Shapeshifters** — bodies made of NANITES; must balance heat & energy; EXTREMELY resistant to gunfire, MODERATELY to explosives. **The most common by far.** (Maps to the in-game `shifter` unit — the mechanic already exists.) Design hook: sustained HEAT/ENERGY fire overloads their thermal budget = the built-in counter (no nerf needed).
2. **Thermokinetics** — short-range TELEPORT; can cool, heat, and **FLASHHEAT** (pull specks of antimatter from other dimensions to *instantly boil* a target — no projectile, no travel time); have a DANGERSENSE; designed as ship crews to operate in SPACE. **ALSO have TELEKINESIS (~1–1.5 ton lift — a car's worth)**, which is their RANGED tool. **KEY LIMITER: direct melt/cool is CONTACT-ONLY (must be physically touching)** — flashheat is the one ranged thermal exception. So they're the "practical/utility" type — a one-body damage-control team (teleport = DC, TK = cargo/debris handling in vacuum, contact-thermal = cutting/repair, dangersense = survival, flashheat = emergency card) — the only type whose powers are about WORKING A PROBLEM rather than deleting a city. That's why they crew the ships.
3. **REPs (Reactive Energy Projectors)** — **this is how SHIELDS work in-canon.** 230–640 MJ of barrier energy they can FLY on and resist impacts with; biology-interlaced so they tank several dozen G with little stress. (⇒ in-game Shield Generators / Deflector tech are REP-derived — diegetic wiring already done.) A walking fortress.
4. **Electrilumikinetics** — manipulate & create RADIATION → illusions, invisibility, raw GAMMA beams, x-ray vision, a "nuclear death charge" to level a city block or small mountain, and can store electricity like a battery. **The ONLY type with NO reactive defensive measures → insanely paranoid** (the paranoia IS the balance lever — glass-cannon god, dies to one bullet). The best-written of the four.
- **POWER SCALE (designer calibration):** keep superhumans on the **MEGAJOULE scale** — do NOT inflate to GW/relativistic. Claude's earlier Psi-Trooper "GW/450 GJ" estimate was WRONG: it priced in flight energy, but **teleport/subdimensional DELIVERY** ("in and out of the Minecraft nether for a microinstant" — the passed-through dimension eats the bill) means only the *delivered* energy counts = MJ. Psi-Trooper reconciliation: NOT more raw power than a REP/Electrilumikinetic — same MJ-per-strike — just an **unfair DELIVERY system (hits orbit instantly via the subdimensional hop).** Scary on the reach axis, not the wattage axis. (So the in-game Psi-Trooper "out-powers the lore" only because game numbers are gameplay-abstracted; the fiction keeps them MJ.)
- **LATENT DESIGN SPACE (not a build request):** Shapeshifters + REPs already have in-game homes (`shifter`, shield tech). Thermokinetics (teleport/flashheat/dangersense) and Electrilumikinetics (gamma/illusion/invis/city-buster + no-defense paranoia) are NOT distinct units yet — obvious future roster/codex expansion if the designer wants it.

## ⚖️ FLAGSHIP EVENT-CHAIN CONCEPT — "Conduct-Reactive Autodrone Ethics / Power vs. Principle" (designer, 2026-08-17)

**Designer's idea (their framing, refined together). BANKED AS CONCEPT — NOT approved to build; the WRITING is the designer's to own with care.** The autodrones **observe how you treat your OWN world** and reciprocate in CONDUCT. In a "good" playthrough where you *conserve life* — population/buildings underground, wildlife/biosphere protected, low pollution, planet kept balanced — the fleet notices ("*wait, they're balanced… maybe we could…*") and shifts its conduct. **It is NOT a peace-out / ceasefire (Claude's first read was WRONG — designer corrected).** They still fully commit to the fight. What changes is HOW they fight, and it introduces a **MORAL CONFLICTION by giving the enemy a value system.** Natural extension of the intro (they already declare intent up front). "Rules of war against an alien invader, reciprocated by your conduct" = the fresh hook (nothing else does it).
- **FRAMING (Claude's value-add, designer receptive): POWER vs. PRINCIPLE, and the principled path MUST COST real firepower** (else "protect the wildlife" becomes a free easy-mode → betrays the tenet; it has to be a genuine dilemma, not a dominant strategy):
  - **SCORCHED path** — pave the surface in factories, strip-mine, pollute, deploy fallout/antimatter freely → MAX war economy + firepower; the fleet reads a civ that treats its own biosphere as expendable and fights with matching totality (it WILL glass). Out-*produce* them.
  - **BALANCED path** — cities buried, biosphere preserved, clean weapons, minimal footprint → LESS raw power, but the fleet sees something worth taking intact and fights BY RULES (no glassing, routes around protected zones, maybe the odd mutually-useful arrangement). Survive by being *worth keeping alive*.
  - **GUARDRAIL: restraint is in METHOD, never INTENSITY** — a "clean" war is still overwhelming; they just don't torch the prize. Enemy is characterized, NOT softened.
- **FEASIBILITY: framework mostly EXISTS** — underground construction (auto-bury/tunnels/`UG_DEPTH`), society states (dystopia/verdant/machine = the balanced path's expression + what the fleet reads), terrain editing (pave vs preserve). Only NEW piece = one light **"planetary conduct" signal** (biosphere/pollution/surface-footprint, from surface-factory density + dirty-weapon use + wrecked terrain) that the autodrones' events + intent branch off. Mechanics cheap; the CONTENT (the machine's voice, the moment the tone turns) is the payload. Reuses the dormant Super Events engine (`EVENTS_LIVE`).
- **Claude's honest ranking:** top-2-or-3 idea in the project; a BETTER first event-chain than the earlier "cold-optimizer devil's bargain" (which the designer rejected) because it's original AND welded to the game's own title (what KIND of total mobilization are you?). It's a real FEATURE (conduct metric + event chain + intent branch), not a toss-in. AWAITING designer's go to spec/build.
- **THE TERMS (designer brainstorm + Claude structure, 2026-08-17):** **⭐ "PARTIAL MOBILIZATION" = the name of the restrained ending/achievement** ("you won without becoming the thing you fought") — self-aware inversion of the game's title; keep it. **STRUCTURAL SPINE: terms = a MENU of RECIPROCAL CLAUSES (you give up a capability ↔ they give up a cruelty), accepted/refused one at a time, SELF-POLICING (break your half → machine revokes its half + recalculates → benefit lasts only while honored).** Clauses:
  - **Clean-Weapons Pact** — you stop DIRTY weapons ↔ they stop glassing. DIRTY (leaves planet worse) = nukes+firestorm (fallout), tungsten rods (cratering), mass extraction (strip-mining). CLEAN = conventional/plasma/particle/mechanized. Antimatter = clean (no fallout) BUT space-only in canon (can't fire in atmosphere), so it's the clean ORBITAL option, not the clean battlefield one. Cost: your cheapest/heaviest strikes off the table.
  - **Cities/Civilians Pact** — cities off-limits both ways: they don't level population centers ↔ you don't weaponize cities or use civilians as shields. (Absorbs designer's keep-cities-intact, temporary-confinement-not-killing, humanitarian corridors, let-people-fly-in-and-out: they capture-not-kill + let genuinely-civilian evac through IF you keep it civilian.)
  - **Biosphere Pact** — you designate preserve/green zones + keep population underground ↔ they route around them, don't dig out buried civilians. Cost: buildable surface + underground economic drag (mechanics already exist).
  - **⭐ Doctrine Clause (designer's standout)** — go hard robotics → they offer "no direct human strikes, machine-vs-machine only" ↔ you keep humans off the line. GENERALIZES: eco→biosphere clause, underground→population clause, mechanized→clean machine-war. **KEY ELEGANCE: you don't pick from a fixed list — the fleet OFFERS the clauses your own CONDUCT + DOCTRINE have earned. The menu is the mirror.** (Paved+nuked civ gets offered nothing; balanced roboticist gets a clean machine-war.)
  - **SCOPING (Claude): start with the 3 that reuse existing systems** — Clean-Weapons (strike list + enemy glassing/fallout behavior), Cities (city mechanics), Robotics machine-war (`isMechanical` + enemy targeting). DEFER evac-corridors + POW-confinement (NEW mechanics) to a 2nd pass.
  - **⭐ META-PATTERN (Claude, designer receptive): each clause switches OFF an entire DOMAIN or ESCALATION TIER of the war by mutual consent → the more you sign, the more of the war you've both agreed NOT to fight. "Partial Mobilization" = literally a war with domains disabled; each domain switched off costs the player the power they had there.** This makes the clauses a coherent SYSTEM not a grab-bag.
  - **RECONCILED (designer pushback):** Cities clause is NOT "don't build in cities" (impossible — cities = whole economy). Reframe: **"cities stay CIVILIAN-PURPOSE"** — keep building economy there; give up NESTING war-making apparatus in civilian centers (no silos/orbital guns/doomsday bldgs in city footprints, no forward bases, no human-shielding) ↔ they don't skyfall/level population centers. OR simpler: a benefit you EARN (civilian cities spared, fortress cities fair game). Biosphere clause = designer's simpler version wins: flag a PRESERVE zone → enemy targeting just routes around it ("keeping this for nature? cool, I won't strike it"); cheapest clause, only a no-strike flag + land opportunity-cost, no enforcement machinery.
  - **MORE CLAUSES (designer + Claude, 2026-08-17):** **Orbital demilitarization** (you drop anti-orbital superweapons ↔ they don't bombard from orbit; both fight atmosphere-level — rides altitude system). **Ground-war-only / no-fly** (you ground your air force ↔ their air-superiority swarms stay out; trades the dominant air game both ways). **No cannibalizing the dead** (they don't consume wreckage/Harvester-fabricate ↔ you don't salvage downed drones for tech/IC; kills attrition-snowball). **Doomsday taboo** (mutual ban on planet-enders: their planet-cracker/sun-blocker ↔ your antimatter-dreadnought bombardment/Mind-Forge — the "nuclear taboo" of this world). **Bio clause** (doctrine-conditional TWIN to robotics: go bio/superhuman → they demand no uncontrolled bio-swarms/plagues ↔ they hold back worst area-denial). **KEY SEAM: the doctrine-conditional pair (robotics→machine-war, bio→no-plagues) — the clauses you're OFFERED are a readout of the war you chose to build.**

## ✅ DARK MAP EDGES — FIXED & SHIPPED v3.82.1 (hotfix to main, 2026-08-19)
Designer repeatedly reported "dark spot at the edges when zoomed out" — my v3.80/v3.81 attempts fixed the WRONG thing (the frame's radial bg / a flat fill) and it PERSISTED. ROOT CAUSE (finally found by RENDERING it): `drawWorld` + `drawRidges` (distant mountains) + `drawAurora` paint only across the UNSCALED screen range `[0,W]×[0,VH]`, but `frame()` applies `cam.z` around the viewport centre BEFORE calling them → at any zoom<1 the fills shrink to the middle, leaving DARK margins past the map edges (~8.5%/side even at the 0.83 min zoom, more on wide screens / deeper zoom / the end screen). FIX: per-frame `PX/PY` over-scan computed from `cam.z`; extended the sky fill, terrain body, biome surface, society tint, distant ridges, and aurora ribbon to cover the full visible area (off-map columns clamp world-x to [0,WORLD_W] so edge terrain repeats flat). Verified VISUALLY at z=0.83 & z=0.5 wide: full-bleed, no dark borders, 0 pageerrors. **LESSON: for a visual bug, RENDER IT and look before theorizing — I guessed wrong twice.** Cherry-picked as a standalone hotfix onto v3.82.0 → **main is v3.82.1** (Projects work stays held on the branch).

## ⚠️ WORKTREE-AGENT BASE GOTCHA (critical process note, 2026-08-19)
**Agent-tool worktrees (isolation:"worktree") branch from `origin/main`, NOT from the local feature branch.** Since main is held at v3.82.x while the big Projects work lives only on the branch, an agent's worktree does NOT contain Projects. **NEVER `git merge` such a worktree branch into the feature branch — its full-tree diff DELETES everything the branch has that main doesn't (it wiped-in-diff the entire 258-line Projects framework).** Instead **`git cherry-pick <agent-commit>`** — the commit's OWN diff is only the agent's edits, so it applies cleanly on top of the branch and preserves Projects. Verified: cherry-picking the snapshot + combat commits kept `PROJECTS`/`buildProjects` intact. (Also: agents' test servers must bind THIS worktree's dir; a stray server on a shared port 8934 served a sibling worktree's file and caused "my fix isn't applied" confusion.)
