## 📋 BACKLOG (prioritized; move to Shipped as done)

### 🔥 COMBAT REWORK — from the latest playtest (a big coherent vision; sequence carefully — shared `hurt()`/projectile hot path)
The designer's combat philosophy: **glass cannons everywhere.** Most units (even large ones) die in 1–2 hits; combined defensive fire instantly deletes enemies (satisfying); **SHIELDS are the ONE survivability layer.** Pieces:
- ~~**Lethality: most units die in 1–2 hits.**~~ **❌ REVERSED by the designer (do NOT build).** Reason (their insight): fast deaths favor the side with MORE guns = the PLAYER, so it makes the game EASIER, not harder — and it kills the prolonged sieges that are the whole point. The real problem was never durability, it was the free auto-emplacement wall (see CIWS/emplacement item). Leave `hurt()` durability AS-IS.
- **PLAYER shields work fine — it's ENEMY shields that are broken.** (Fable misread this.) The player's Shield Generator dome blocks correctly. The fix: make ENEMY shields (beachhead domes / shielded enemy units) BLOCK incoming player fire the SAME WAY the player's shield does. Do NOT rework player shields; do NOT add threshold immunity; do NOT auto-shield big units (that's a later Battleships piece, deferred).
- **The FREE auto-emplacements are the real problem** (RESOLVED framing): the MG nests / flak / field guns that spawn AUTOMATICALLY when infantry dig in are an unstoppable "wall of bullets" that shreds everything — and they're free + absurdly scalable, so they trivialize the game. NERF THESE SPECIFICALLY (fire rate + damage) so a wall is strong-but-beatable and the tankier/saturating enemy can push through. This is NOT a tenet violation — a free auto-win-wall isn't a "toy," it's the game not being a game. **Do NOT make built guns miss/whiff** (whiffing feels broken, not hard) — Fable's counter-proposal accepted by designer's clarification.
- **CIWS perf — the explosion spam is the killer (designer confirmed):** every connecting CIWS round spawns an impact explosion → thousands/sec of pure-cosmetic FX = the lag. Fix: **invisible bullets + a few visible tracers, and KILL the per-round explosion** (sparse spark at most). Also make CIWS read as the anti-ordnance/anti-air SPECIALIST, not the one-true-answer (the lethality change normalizes much of this on its own).
- **Airship → capital ship:** shield + 6+ machine guns + anti-tank guns + MLRS (multi-weapon; Battleships vision).
- **Remove PD lasers from the Fighter** (a fighter carrying anti-missile lasers is a stretch) → make anti-missile lasers a **dedicated aircraft**.

### 🌊 ENEMY ESCALATION v2 — from the lethality-reversal playtest (NEXT enemy build; the *right* way to make it hard)
Instead of nerfing the player, make each landing GROW and blanket the map:
- **Drops ESCALATE:** a drop that starts as ~3 airships can balloon into ~9 airships + a ground landing drop + leviathans, etc. — each landing has the potential to snowball if not answered.
- **Groundfall WARNING banner for ALL super-unit types** (currently only the Land Leviathan gets the "⚠ HAS MADE GROUNDFALL" alert) — give the same warning to bombarders, hives, harvesters, etc.
- **Open the invasion with ~5 bombarders spread across the whole map** (covering every stretch of ground) right AFTER the first missile barrage — so orbital-plasma pressure blankets the ground from the start.
- This is the "difficulty from an overwhelming, escalating enemy" path — the correct alternative to the reversed lethality change.

### 🛰 ENEMY LANDINGS & SUPER-UNIT OVERHAUL — ✅ SHIPPED v3.33.0 (kept here for reference)
- **Every landing includes a super unit; super units come in GROUPS.** Each reinforces if not killed in ~30s (extends existing `BOSS_REINFORCE_T`).
- **Each super unit acts like a CARRIER** — spawns a small group of units.
- **Bombardier kit:** 2× PD fire rate · on arrival deploys **10 railgun sats + 10 laser-defense sats** · arrives with a **support ship that repairs in a radius** · gains a **plasma cannon that snipes GROUND units from space** (oppressive to ground; canNOT snipe air).
- **Bombardment missiles get DURABLE decoys** (take a few hits before breaking apart; current buster decoys are hp:1 — bump them).
- **Ships: fewer drops, ~2× units per drop, targeting the LEAST-defended areas.** Generally units land more in undefended areas and are stronger.

### 🧩 SQUADS — collision rework (designer's revised model; re-touches recent squad work)
Current model = one entity + footprint collision (reads as a blob). Designer wants: **separate collision + separate hitboxes per body, but a shared "brain" (targeting/AI) + a cohesion "pull force"** keeping the bodies clustered. Tightly linked, mostly independent. NOTE: separate collision reintroduces per-body cost — mitigate by keeping the AI/targeting shared (the expensive part stays single) + the existing render LOD.

### 🧵 MULTICORE / WORKERS — Fable's honest verdict: probably NOT worth it
Designer asked to offload targeting math to a worker so the main thread just renders. Reality: Workers can't touch the live object graph — you'd `postMessage`-copy all entity positions out and results back every frame; for thousands of entities that serialization usually costs ~what it saves and adds a frame of targeting latency. Profiling says **RENDERING is the bottleneck, not targeting math** — so the high-value fix is render-side (invisible bullets/tracers, LOD, glow cheapening), not multicore. Revisit only if targeting math ever becomes the profiled hot spot.

### 🚢 CAPITAL SHIPS & THE MODULAR ARSENAL (designer's big convergent vision — THE next major build; design mostly locked below)
The designer talked their way into the whole Battleships keystone + more. It all converges on one system: **a capital-ship / titan tier that your entire tech tree feeds via modular weapon mounts.** Pieces:
- **KEYSTONE — "Miniaturized Shielding"** research (pre: `shieldtech` + `spaceage`). Flavor: the deflector generator shrunk small enough to bolt onto a *moving hull* (that's why it needs Space Age). Does two things: (1) **adds a `shield` pool to your existing big units** (Nanofortress Airship, warships, Colossus/Titan-class ground vehicles); (2) **unlocks the capital-ship tier** (the massive naval battleship). Make it the SHARED ROOT both battleship branches grow from (one "we can build capital ships now" moment).
- **SKY WARSHIP ROSTER (concept art in exploration — 3 design languages being rendered: dirigible / dreadnought / nanotech; designer picks a direction).** These are ~100m armored AIRSHIPS with multiple gun batteries — a CLASS SEPARATE from aircraft (no runways). Roster: **Sky Destroyer** (fast escort/AA, small batteries ~ naval destroyer), **Sky Missile Cruiser** (VLS/standoff ~ naval missile cruiser), **Sky Patrol Ship** (cheap light picket/scout). Weapon loadouts echo their NAVAL counterparts. Each ship has a **weapon PLATFORM that upgrades as you research** (modular mounts — particle/plasma/laser slot in with tech).
- **BUILD FROM A NEW "SKY DOCK" BUILDING** — NOT airbases (airbases are for aircraft; a 100m gun-airship is a different beast). Its own production building.
- **SHIELDS ARE OPTIONAL, NOT REQUIRED** — Miniaturized Shielding is an *upgrade you can choose to add*, not a gate.
- **"Sky Fortresses" research = the unlock UPGRADE-LINE for the big sky ships** (repurpose the existing `airfortress` node), and **the Nanofortress Airship folds into this line as its apex.** Miniaturized Shielding combines in as the optional shield tier. (Research-tree placement finalizes AFTER the art/roster is chosen — design the ships first, shelve them second.)
- **"Superheavy Aircraft"** was the old framing → now folded into the Sky Fortress line above.
- NOTE: these are ROSTER content, NOT tied to the 4.0 milestone (4.0 = the STORY/lore aspect). Roster grows continuously.
- **✅ CONCEPT ART LOVED (all 3 languages).** Designer clarification: **SLEEK IS FULLY APPROVED** — "my problem isn't them LOOKING like a spaceship, it's them BEING a spaceship" (the no-spaceship rule is about game BEHAVIOR/going-to-orbit, NOT visuals). Scale read as "exactly right."
- **LEADING DIRECTION — EVOLVING HULL (designer loves all 3, can't pick):** make the three languages a TECH PROGRESSION — early sky ships look like **dirigibles** → mid like **dreadnought gunboats** → late like **nanotech lifting-bodies** (the modular-mount re-skin system extends to the hull). Turns "which look" into "all of them, as a progression." NOT locked yet, but strongly favored.
- **MORE SHIPS (concepts rendering now, one per language):** **Sky RAILGUN SHIP** (spinal heavy railgun, orbit-reach long-range artillery) + **NANOFORGE CARRIER** (flagship, larger, self-repair, flight deck that launches AIRCRAFT **and HELICOPTERS** — the sky heli-carrier).
- **★ AIRCRAFT ART GLOW-UP (⏳ CONCEPT PASS IN FLIGHT):** redraw the whole PLAYER JET roster (Falcon/Wraith/Solaris/Warthog/Reaper/Warden) prettier — **✅ SHIPPED v3.40.0 — EXOTIC aircraft redraw LIVE.** All 6 player jets (fighter/stealthjet/laserjet/warthog/gunship/pdjet ~L1223-1469) replaced with the approved exotic silhouettes, symmetric wings, player-only (enemy art path `egunship`/`einterceptor`/etc. untouched; tint helpers preserved). NOTE: `gunship`/`stealthjet`/`laserjet` keys are vestigial (no live UNIT_DEF) — redrawn anyway. STILL TO GLOW-UP: naval/ground/drone/enemy art, and the "jets evolve modern→exotic with tech" progression option remains available (era-upgrade reskin) if the designer wants it later. (This also finally kills the long-hated "ugly plane silhouette.")
- **RAILGUN = SMALLER TURRETS, not only the giant spinal gun (designer):** the railgun TANK proves a turret-scale railgun works. Plan: keep the giant SPINAL railgun as the dedicated Railgun SHIP's signature (one big gun = its identity), AND add a railgun TURRET as a weapon MOUNT any multi-turret ship can carry. Both.
- **★ MULTI-TURRETED HEAVY ROSTER (designer):** EVERY naval vessel + heavy airship should be MULTI-WEAPON / multi-turreted like the Land Leviathan (which already fires multiple weapons). This IS the modular-mount capital-ship system applied roster-wide — warships/big-airships carry several turrets, each mount upgradeable by research. Fold into the capital-ship build.
- **⚠ IDENTITY RULE (protects the whole game — designer flagged):** the player ENGAGES up but never OCCUPIES up. You hold surface/sky/sea; the enemy keeps orbital initiative — you're still the besieged defender, just with huge guns. Capital ships FIRE into orbit (like railguns/particle already do; consistent with the v3.38.1 aircraft "fire up, don't fly up" fix) but do NOT live in space. **So 4.0 capital ships = SKY (atmospheric hover-fortress, the airship's apex) + SEA (giant naval super-carrier) ONLY.**
- **SPACE BATTLESHIPS = DEFERRED TO 5.0, behind the ORBITAL RING megaproject.** Do NOT build a space navy in 4.0 — that breaks the defender fantasy. Space combat is EARNED via the ring (the war-flip moment), not default. Sky/Sea now; Space later.
- **★ THE MODULAR MOUNT SYSTEM (the star — fixes "mechanics don't chain", complaint #6):** capital ships (and more units — designer wants modular weapons broadly) have weapon MOUNTS that your OTHER weapon research auto-equips. Research Particle Beams → mounts gain particle cannons; Directed Energy → laser point-defense; plasma/microrockets/etc. slot in. The ship becomes a **living readout of your whole tech tree** — mixing trees finally pays off visibly. IMPLEMENTATION: extend the existing `UPGRADES`/`effDef()` override pattern (already "swaps a unit's weapon when you unlock a tech") into a per-hull `mounts[]` that each weapon-tech toggles on. Enemy super-units are ALREADY multi-weapon (bombarder = PD + plasma cannon + barrage) — same tech, player side.
- **CAPITAL SHIPS ARE CARRIERS — LOCKED.** Graft the working naval-carrier `u.d.carrier` deck-launch (fighters fly out, spend ordnance, land, rearm, relaunch) onto the capital hulls. Flying carrier = the airship's apex form.
- **GIGANTIC-SUPER REWORK:** existing super units get massive bonuses + **gigantic COST** (designer floated ~6× cost / ~2.5× size; a Nanoforge airship the size of a city, dozens of weapons, ~3500 IC — but "expensive enough to justify", tune it). **TENET STEER (important):** gigantic COST is tenet-perfect (game says YES; cost is the balance). A hard "super-unit CAPACITY cap" brushes the tenet — instead use **upkeep + build time + hangar/orbital-lift logistics** as the emergent limiter (a city-sized airship eats a huge chunk of air-command slots + bleeds upkeep → you CAN field a fleet, but the economy screams). Same felt limit, no hard wall, fantasy intact.
- **DECISIONS LOCKED:** (1) capital ships are a NEW TIER ABOVE the airship/carrier, not replacements (you climb to them); (2) Miniaturized Shielding = shared root; (3) they ARE carriers. (4) **✅ HULL AESTHETIC = NANOTECH** (designer picked — sleek anti-grav lifting-body).
- **✅ PHASE 1 SHIPPED v3.42.0 (nanotech):** the 5 sky warships (destroyer/cruiser/patrol/railgun/carrier) as buildable atmospheric capital ships (excluded from air/rotor caps), from a new SKY DOCK building, gated behind the Sky Fortress (`airfortress`) line (Nanofortress airship folds in), carrier launches aircraft+helis, railgun reaches orbit. Fixed loadouts for now. **PHASE 2 (later):** modular weapon-mounts (research reskins weapons onto ships), multi-turret-everything, the optional Miniaturized-Shielding upgrade, and the evolving-hull option (still available). NEEDS PLAYTEST when it ships.
- **★ PHASE 2 MODULAR-MOUNT MAP (designer-approved spec — sky AND naval capital ships):** each ship has mounts (role-fixed + shared hardpoints); weapon research auto-swaps the upgraded module in. SHARED (all ships): Point Defense→flak CIWS · Directed Energy→laser PD + laser secondaries · Miniaturized Shielding→deflector shield · Nanotechnology→nanoforge self-repair · Robotics→armor plating + auto-defense drone turrets. MAIN (by role): base kinetics→railgun turrets · Plasma Weapons→plasma batteries (destroyer/patrol/gunships) · Particle Beams→**particle cannons = the ATMOSPHERIC APEX** (heavy mounts + railgun-ship spinal) · Microrockets→splitting micro-rocket VLS (cruiser). **⚠ NO ANTIMATTER on atmospheric ships (designer physics correction):** a direct-fire antimatter beam/projectile annihilates with AIR at the muzzle → suicide weapon; that's WHY the Antimatter Dreadnought is space-only. So **antimatter direct-fire = SPACE-EXCLUSIVE** (Dreadnought now; the space battleships unlocked by the Orbital Ring in 5.0). This makes antimatter the concrete REWARD for reaching orbit — atmospheric fleets top out at particle beams; you must LEAVE the sky to wield antimatter. (Lore-legal exception: a CONTAINED antimatter MISSILE warhead — annihilates only on impact — is fine through atmosphere, exactly how the Antimatter Strike already works; only the beam/gun can't exist in air.) EXOTIC: Automation→carrier drone bay · Telekinesis→tractor-beam mount · Applied Psionics→psi-disruptor. Goal = a fully-teched Nanoforge Carrier is a living readout of your whole tree.
- **TITAN / new hulls:** superheavy tank; **hover tank past Fusion Age** (BUILD EARLY, cheap+huge payoff — fixes the oldest ground complaint: tracked units become FASTER and AMPHIBIOUS, driving over water like solid ground; clean UPGRADE-table swap).

### 🚀 SUPER UNITS BEYOND THE TREE (designer has "tons" — exotic acquisitions, not normal research)
- **PROJECT ORION** (designer) — nuclear-pulse-propulsion behemoth (rides a wave of nuclear detonations) aimed at the orbital capital ship; on approach it **FRAGMENTS INTO A SWARM OF INTERCEPTOR MISSILES**. Reuses the MIRV/micromissile split-munition code. A boss-killer superweapon with spectacle. Acquire outside the normal tree (decision tree / special unlock).
- Designer wants a whole class of these exotic supers acquired via decision trees / events rather than the research board. Ties to the SUPER EVENTS engine (already built, dormant) + the dangerous-tech events vein.

### 🌌 SANDBOX / "TOTAL WAR" MODE (the north star — justifies every gigantic toy above)
A fully-researched nation (all tech unlocked) vs escalating / themed enemies — including **fictional-universe aliens** — to feel out "how much could we actually fight off." Low-ish effort: an all-unlocked scenario + a tunable/escalating enemy, riding the existing `SCEN` custom-scenario editor. Build it RIGHT AFTER capital ships so there's an arena to bring them to. This is the mode the whole arsenal exists for.

### 🎮 "PLAY AS THE AUTOFLEET" + TWO-PLANE MULTIPLAYER (designer idea — great fantasy, honest costs)
Designer: "one player playing the autofleet would be super fun!" — and it's right (asymmetric besieger-vs-besieged is proven fun; the game already has a rich decision-making attacker AI to inherit). **Fable's honest cost read (why 2P is a 2.0/sequel-scale bet, NOT near-term):** (1) **NETCODE is the monster** — this is a single-file, client-side, zero-network game; real-time 1v1 needs state sync / relay-or-P2P / latency handling = arguably a bigger lift than the whole rest of the roadmap combined. (2) **Attacker has nothing to do during PREP** → you'd have to design+balance a whole SECOND (attacker) economy/build-up game. (3) **Asymmetric balance is a forever-job** vs a human who exploits/focus-fires/times pushes; SP tuning won't transfer.
- **★★ THE ACTUAL BUILD PATH (designer's refinement — solves the defender-AI problem entirely): SIEGE A SAVED DEFENSE.** No 2P, no AI. The defender is a **SAVED SNAPSHOT of a real base** — and a base already runs its OWN guns (emplacements/CIWS/garrison/interceptors/shields all auto-fire), so no defender AI is needed at all: it's a self-defending fortress. You show up as the autofleet with a **FIXED STOCKPILE of strikes + deployment drops** (a loadout budget) and try to crack it. It's a PUZZLE / SCORE-ATTACK ("here's a fortress + 12 bombs + 4 drop pods, break it"), not a balance nightmare. Win = level the cities / a %-destroyed + time score.
  - **SNEAKY UNLOCK — async social w/o netcode:** because a defense is just a SAVED FILE, you can attack SOMEONE ELSE'S saved base (share fortress → friend assaults → compare scores). ~80% of the 2P social juice, zero networking.
  - **THE ONE DEPENDENCY: a base SAVE/SERIALIZE system** (game is currently session-only). Snapshot `G.buildings`/units/terrain/garrison → JSON → restore into a fresh battle. Bounded work, and it gives normal save/load as a bonus. Attacker toolkit = reuse the existing STRAT/strike system. THIS is the path (supersedes the earlier "SP vs defender-AI" idea).

### 🏛 MEGAPROJECTS (5.0 — designer wants each as its own thing)
Wonder-scale endeavours, each a distinct build (not just a research node): the "trump cards" that push the faction UP a power bracket. Examples in flight from the powerscaling chats: **Project Orion** (nuclear-pulse boss-killer that fragments into an interceptor swarm), **relativistic kill vehicle** (antimatter slug flung from the system's edge at ~0.9c — surface-scouring/extinction-tier, a genuine planet-ender), literal planet-crackers (require absurd energies — a megaproject, never a strike button). Megaprojects are the 5.0 headline.
- **★ ORBITAL RING (the 5.0 centerpiece — designer's, and it's great):** a colossal megaproject that pushes the exosphere ceiling WAY up (~200% taller space band), unlocks a whole new tier of **space-scale construction**, and — crucially — is **THE gate that lets you finally build/field in ORBIT.** This is the moment the war FLIPS from defense to offense. Canon justification: capital ships of true space scale are "too big to assemble on Earth" → you need the ring. Fighting the autofleet IN SPACE lives behind this. Pairs with the TM2 counter-offensive arc.

### 📖 LORE / POWER-SCALING ANCHOR (worldbuilding — seeds the lore layer + sandbox enemy tiers)
Established in-conversation, keep consistent: **the faction is a single fully-mobilized planet/nation ≈ upper-0.9-to-1.1 Kardashev-I** — antimatter (extractors → mass antimatter), orbital kinetic bombardment, particle weapons, psionics (Mind-Forge), superhumans, nanotech, self-repair — but **sublight, defensive, cannot leave orbit**. The ultimate PLANETARY LAST STAND; can gut anything sublight, breaks against FTL empires that park in high orbit and Exterminatus. **Key scaling levers:** (1) capital ships are ~5 km (bigger than a standard 1.6 km ISD — we out-mass most fictional *line* ships; only top-1% outliers like SSDs/Death Stars/Hive Fleets/Necrons/committed Crusades are "nope"); (2) **telekinesis denies orbital supremacy** — both sides can tractor capital ships OUT of orbit, so "win orbit then glass" (most invaders' whole plan) fails against us; (3) the **autofleet itself is an S-tier (~Covenant-tier) combined-arms antagonist** with RKKVs — beating it is the measuring stick. (4) **ENGAGEMENT-ENVELOPE EDGE (the sharpest lever):** our entire tree reaches ground-to-orbit (`ceil:0` railguns/particle/missiles/orbital strikes) while most fictional fleets (esp. Star Wars) fight at close/visual range behind shields. We pick the range and deny theirs — enemies fight in OUR band (sustained antimatter+particle+kinetic + tractor pull) or not at all. Plus **shield-attrition**: continuous multi-vector annihilation is a damage curve turbolaser-tuned shields aren't built for, and **nanofabrication rebuilds emplacements in seconds** → we out-TEMPO attrition foes and win the long war by default. Useful for: the sandbox "fictional aliens" roster tiers and any future lore/story layer.

### 🌐 SETTING CANON — THE WARP CATAPULT (designer lore, keep consistent; seeds story layer + TM2)
- The aliens who sent the autofleet used a **WARP CATAPULT** to FLING the ships here — this is WHY the autofleet has no native FTL (one-way launch). It's the single fact that keeps them beatable.
- **ONE catapulted fleet, not endless launches** (designer tightening): the catapult flung a SINGLE autofleet that then spent YEARS massing in orbit to build the threat — the **PREP countdown IS that buildup finishing**, then it descends in waves. A contained, FINITE force → "destroy the fleet" stays a real win condition (which the game already treats it as). NOT infinite FTL reinforcement.
- **Endgame / sequel arc ("TM2"):** "it's only a matter of time before we figure it out" — humanity reverse-engineers the catapult, gains FTL, and the campaign FLIPS: survive the siege → colonize the solar system (space elevators + orbital rail already exist) → eventually shoot BACK down the catapult at whoever sent it. Post-siege colonization/counter-offensive is a whole future mode.
- **Plasma-signature capital hull:** fold into the CAPITAL SHIPS build — a distinct plasma-leaning battleship (the modular mounts make it trivial) next to the antimatter and kinetic hulls. Designer wants a "wall of superheated death" ship.
- **The death-gun stays (canon-justified):** the aura/ammo-box network turning a particle beam / railcannon into an "880mm supersonic sabot machine gun" is gloriously silly — KEEP it (designer's call), lore = "we solved the logistics so the gun never stops." (Matches the existing "don't nerf the super mega death gun yet" note.)

### NEXT BIG BUILDS
1. **Sky/Sea/Space Battleships** → now the CAPITAL SHIPS system above (design locked; spine of the next major build).
2. **Garrison v2 — supply chain (emergent cap, no hard wall):** upkeep-heavy **Factories** → nearest **Depots** → nearest **buildings** (proximity logistics). Remove per-unit upkeep (upkeep lives on factories). Limits are economic + logistical only. (Current garrison is v1: passive per-building auto-fill + per-unit upkeep + a hard global Stockpile weight cap.)
3. **Real helicopters** (designed long ago, NEVER built — biggest miss; designer LOVES these, strong "do next" candidate — self-contained). Full spec (designer): **garrison basically everything** (no runways needed) as CLOSE AIR SUPPORT; slow/cheap/close-range, **low ceiling** (tropo-only, can't reach high enemies — keeps them out of air-spam), land in cities/depots to rearm. **SEPARATE from Air Command** — their own capacity pool (a "$60M heli vs a $300M jet are different logistics"); own build category. **Different combat AI** than jets (hover, hug terrain, loiter CAS — not the fast fixed-wing RTB model). **Helicopter-depot buildings + infra upgrades** that let buildings garrison &/or refuel helis, and turn city tiles into heli garrisons/swarms. Sensible defaults if built: new "Rotor Command" capacity filled by helipads/heli-depots; helis garrison into buildings & cities; low-alt CAS AI. NOTE: roster content, not milestone-gated. **✅ SHIPPED v3.39.0:** ROTOR COMMAND pool (`rotorCap`/`rotorUsed`, separate from Air Command; cities give baseline + `helidepot` building +6), no-runway rearm at cities/depots/carriers (`nearestHeliBase`), low-alt HOVER CAS AI (`d.rotor` branch — closes, hovers, drifts with target; tropo ceil .78), Attack Heli "Kite" (rotor unit) + Microrocket Kite upgrade, and naval Carrier launches a gunship heli (`heliCarrier` hook for the future sky Nanoforge Carrier). TUNABLE: `ROTOR_SLOTS`/`CITY_ROTOR`; heli-depot uses `prod:'air'` so it can also roll jets (easy to restrict).
- **⏭ ROTOR ROSTER EXPANSION (designer wants — currently only the Attack Heli):** add more rotor units — a helicopter with **short-range air-to-air missiles** (heli-vs-air interceptor), and **large rotor-based vehicles** (heavy transport/gunship rotorcraft, tiltrotors). Real helicopters aren't just one CAS gunship. Queue as content.
4. **Aerowork-style garrison buildings:** buildings that grant garrison capacity / unit-upgrades / new unit-types to *neighboring* buildings; "+1 missile capacity" buildings; **helicopter-depot** buildings that give city tiles a helicopter garrison → turn a city into a helicopter swarm.
5. **Units enter buildings → become turrets** (huge perf + cinematic win; ties to garrison): a stack cap, click a building to see who's inside, building-type buffs.
6. **Apocalypse as honest ENDURANCE mode:** replace the demoralizing "survived 9%" with a clear survival objective (e.g. HOLD FOR X) + a personal best to beat. General & below stay winnable; Apocalypse is a last-stand you chase a record on.

### MEDIUM / FEATURES
7. **Anti-missile interceptor building** — laser-tower-sized, ~1500 IC / 100 MP / 30 influence, 4 missiles, reaches space, slow reload, buriable in a silo. The dedicated bombardment counter. (Bombardment code now merged — UNBLOCKED.)
8. **Aircraft reach one band up (thermosphere)** — partial walk-back of the hard mesosphere cap; exosphere stays space-only. (UNBLOCKED now.)
9. **Multi-weapon big units** (titans, land leviathans, warships get secondaries) — largely folded into Battleships.
10. **Lasers → damage-over-time** (a beam melts, it doesn't "hit"). Shifts lasers to the "melt the big slow thing" role; flak handles swarms.
11. **Conventional reinforcement — "you're not alone":** if the game detects the player is woefully underprepared, allied conventional forces surge in (≈4 carrier strike groups, 4 armored battalions, city defenses, underground barracks); some aid even if prepared. Cities also self-improve (bunkers, heli bases).
12. **Inherited-nation start:** cities begin with a few garrison stockpiles/factories + 2–3 pre-placed missile silos, so you inherit a country instead of an empty map.
13. **Influence "hire allies" sink:** buy a Carrier Strike Group for ~200 influence + IC + MP, like a strike, especially pre-invasion. Late-game influence sink.
14. **Naval identity:** differentiate Missile Cruiser (AA/point-missile escort) from Arsenal Ship "Leviathan" (VLS saturation); untangle the name clash with the (now-removed) Arsenal Ship building. Distinct warship hulls already shipped.
15. ~~**Achievements + coup screen**~~ ✅ SHIPPED v3.32.0 — coup defeat reframe + 5 medals (`couped` "Regime Change", `nukehappy`, `oops`, `forgotresearch`, `pacifist` "Who Needs Science?" = win with zero research) + unlock toasts.

### SMALL / QoL (new from latest playtest)
23. **MAP-GEN: too much water + gigacity clumping (designer playtest, mild).** Some seeds roll almost entirely SEA ("Waterworld") with only a few dry spots, and development piles into ONE gigacity because there's no land to spread onto. Keep map VARIETY (designer likes varied maps) but tune the terrain generator: guarantee a MINIMUM usable coastline/land fraction, and spread city seeds apart so you don't get one mega-blob in an ocean. Low-effort generation tuning; not urgent ("it's fine to be honest").
17. **Fortified/dug-in units should NOT block building placement** (currently you can't build where units have fortified).
18. **Click-through occluded buildings:** if a unit is in front of a building, clicking should let you reach/see the building behind it (selection cycling / building priority in `pickAt`).
19. **End-screen post-game graphs** ("the 9000 graphs") — rich after-action charts.
20. **Special-building effects listed in BOTH the SPEC menu AND the Infrastructure menu** (currently only one).
21. **Factory production readout:** show what garrison factories are producing, how much, how fast.
22. **Descriptions audit (partly done):** stale unit/research text. CONFIRMED BUG: the **Nanotech** research node only advertises the Autonomous Plant — it does NOT mention the **Nanite Foundry** and **Nanite Turret** it also unlocks (both `req: 'nanotech'`). Fix the node's blds list/description.

### AUDIT — DECIDED CUTS / FIXES
- **CUT:** Mass Drivers (pure +35% dmg stat), Scout "Lynx" (recons nothing — no fog of war), Hive Mind (never integrates cleanly). **CUT or fold power into something real:** Void Engine (pure +80% IC/+20% dmg). Re-point any prereqs when removing nodes.
- **FIX:** Titan fires `weapon: 'tracer'` (a machine gun) — give the 1,300-HP colossus a real signature weapon.
- **PERF philosophy for the death-gun:** aura stacking WAS multiplicative (30 ammo depots ≈ 800× fire rate). **✅ TAMED TO ADDITIVE in v3.90.0** exactly as this note predicted — designer called it after EXTINCTION report_6 (psi + ring-farms trivialized 30×). `auraAt` now does `m += (aura-1)` so two +8% zones = +16%, not +16.6% compounding. Still no hard cap; ring farms still strong, just no longer runaway-exponential. `auraHeal` stays Math.max (was never a stack).
- **AUDIT STILL TO DO:** strikes (`STRAT` table), enemy roster, achievements, raw UI strings — sweep for stale/redundant/weird, categorize KEEP/CUT/FIX/MERGE.
- **ON TRIAL** (suspected nothingburgers, watch in a real game before defending): Fire Control, Tractor Beam, Rail Hub.

## 🎨 BLOCKED ON THE DESIGNER
- **Capital Fabricator** super-ship art (designer is drawing the hull; transcribe to canvas once it exists).
- **Lore / story layer** (parents flagged the story as thin).
- **Music** — artist permissions for the default soundtrack.
- Possible spin-off: an **FPS in the same setting** (soldier's-eye view of the orbital siege; Helldivers-style co-op or extraction-under-bombardment).

## 🔒 DEFERRED DECISIONS (locked, apply later)
- Enemy-competence overhaul — **after 4.0** (make the enemy far smarter before touching the player further). Full design banked above in THE ENEMY OVERHAUL.
- Scenario menu rework — make the 4 intents feel like **equal choices**, not a difficulty ladder. Every cell of scenario×tier should be genuinely threatening (even Probe/Decap slam every capital hard in their short window).
- Difficulty as **×1 / ×2.5 / ×5 intensity tiers** decoupled from the 4 scenarios (the home for the brutal-vs-humane split). Escalation + planet-cracker only at top tiers of Invasion/Annihilation.
- Perf: it's **RENDERING**, not unit math, that's the bottleneck — optimize rendering (LOD/batching); web-workers/multicore can't touch Canvas2D (must stay on the main thread), so they're NOT the fix. Squad LOD shipped; general projectile/glow render cheapening still open (the old "9 FPS at 800 units" issue).
- **Strategic resources should grant BONUSES, not act as hard REQUIREMENTS** (the original design doc's intended fix — SRs ended up barely used vs. their intended role).
- **Graphical direction:** shape/outline reworks over tonal detail; the designer dislikes "jets become spaceships" at Space Age.
- **Redundancy consolidation audit (old list):** ground bombardment ×3, heavy tanks ×2, 1-item TECH tab; Comms-vs-Propaganda now RESOLVED (split roles); Scout CUT; Tractor Beam / Rail Hub on trial.
- **Musings banked:** optional ads + ~30 trivia pieces; a custom scenario editor already exists (`SCEN`).

## Side project (2026-10-14/15): Levy — a tModLoader mod (`Levy/`)

Not part of the game. The designer asked for a one-shot build of the "Levy" Terraria mod from Build Spec v0.1
(junk becomes a garrison; Regulars + Militia; boss rule + fear; wages). It lives in `Levy/` (the folder name must stay
`Levy` — tModLoader names the mod after it), with `PLAN.md`, `README.md`, `TESTING.md`; verification tooling in
`levy-tools/`. Target: tModLoader **1.4.4.9 (v2026.08.3.0 stable, .NET 8)**.

**Status: built, integrated, reviewed, not yet playtested.** Branch `claude/levy-mod`.
- All spec milestones M0–M8 and M10 implemented across ~90 source files, built by 14 parallel subagents against a shared
  contract, integrated, and compiled clean both with Roslyn against the real tModLoader assemblies and with tModLoader's
  headless `-build`, which packs **`Levy.tmod`** at the repo root.
- Two adversarial source-review passes (6 lenses, every finding re-verified against the decompiled game); 16 confirmed
  defects fixed, including the tile-entity-ID renumber on world load (home re-resolved from tile position), a
  militia-death double-count, HandAmmo accepting the wrong ammo, and fight-scoped veterancy for the Twins / Eater of
  Worlds. Remaining gaps are multiplayer-only (not a v1 goal); see `Levy/TESTING.md`.
- Now at `levy-mod/` (box: `Levy/` source, prebuilt `Levy.tmod`, `tools/`, `README.md`). **Being actively playtested by the designer — it works and is fun** (held a goblin army at a chokepoint; barks and fear firing).

**Playtest feedback — shipped (branch `claude/levy-mod`):**
- Readable scrollable Field Manual (was a chat dump); weapon name on nameplates; Re-arm order on the War Horn radial; armory-break spill feedback + OnKill safety spill.

**Playtest feedback — shipped, 2026-10-15 (combined drop, commit 72d24c5):**
- Real weapon fire: modded/global Shoot hooks run; vanilla special-arrow behavior reproduced (Hellwing, Daedalus rain, Molten Quiver, launcher/ammo pairings, Blood Rain). Config RealWeaponShoot (default on), safe fallback. Beam swords already fired beams. Found: Hellwing/Daedalus are hard-coded in Player.ItemCheck_Shoot/PickAmmo, not hooks — so ported, not hooked.
- More Armory issue slots (12 wpn / 12 armor / 6 ammo), wider panel.
- Auto-consume Life/Mana Crystals + Life Fruit from the Armory (AutoFeedCrystals, default on); Life Crystal sound on use/feed.
- Bosses call out the garrison (#12): auto-muster while any boss is alive (`LevyUtil.IsBossActive`), stand down on boss death.
- Manpower reserve ×3 (9/NPC); field still capped by RenderCap (#9 done).
- Class-aware weapon issue (#10): armor's actual class bonus (read via the stat pipeline, cached per set in `ArmorDoctrine`) biases weapon pick; armor issued first; falls back to best-available. Config ClassMatchIssue (default on).

**Watch after this drop (runtime, couldn't test headless):** class-aware scratch-Player runs UpdateEquips once per distinct armor set (same calls as soldier RefreshStats) — watch for odd side effects; class-aware tuning thresholds (ArmorDoctrine.MinBonus/MinLead) are untuned guesses; armor with only mana/other bonuses gets no preference.

**SHIPPED v0.3.0, 2026-10-15 pm (commit 0b55e12) — #1, #2, #14, part #4:**
- AI/movement overhaul: no-suicide standoff (ranged hold distance, never path into contact); ranges up (gun 40→72, bow 30→54, magic 25→40) + kite/acquire to match; fighting-retreat rout (routed soldiers back off AND keep firing, degraded aim, hold gun pose); dodge hostile projectiles; small-gap jumps; reachability — fire if target in range+LoS instead of walking a pit (King Slime fix), drop truly-unreachable targets instead of idling. All tuned via NAMED constants in LevyConstants.Brain/Weapons/Movement/Fear2 — EXPECT A TUNING PASS once playtested (standoff distance, range, retreat speed, aim penalties are first guesses). Known: long-range Levy-grade fire is loose (±100px @ 1000px); followers may lag to shoot at ~70 tiles (teleport covers worst case).
- Per-unit loadout menu: "Kit" button per roster row → loadout panel (weapon/sidearm/armor/accessory/ammo, tooltips); hand-equip; per-slot gold LOCK (bitmask on SoldierGear) skipped by auto-issue + Re-arm; net-synced; backward-compatible saves. Watch: panel layout sized by arithmetic (unseen); ammo slot accepts any ammo type; MP take-out rebuilds item from type+prefix.

**Playtest feedback — backlog (Wave 2). DONE in 0.2.0: #9, #10, #12. IN FLIGHT: #1, #2, #14, part #4. Still open: #3 (orders: Spread Out / Focus Target / Search & Destroy), #5 (idle melee swings), #6 (thorns), #7 (path jitter), #8 (dummy targeting), #11 (armor-coverage confirm), #13 (boss fear tuning):**
1. **Per-unit loadout menu** (TOP ask): click any soldier/militia in the Barracks menu, see kit with hover tooltips, hand-assign weapons/accessories, locked against auto-issue. Covers the accessory-slot ask.
2. **AI fix — soldiers must NOT run into enemies.** They currently close distance (toward commander/target) and get strung out, so one offscreen enemy kills them one by one. Keep a standoff / don't suicide into contact.
3. **Orders:** Spread Out (loose formation vs AoE like Eye of Cthulhu); Focus Target (click an enemy, concentrate fire); **Search & Destroy** (roam all directions hunting enemies instead of clustering). Note: current Rally is a morale steady-up, NOT spread-out.
4. **Movement:** use movement accessories (double jump, hover); handle 1-block gaps. Best-effort (NPC pathing).
5. **Flavor — idle melee swings:** melee soldiers occasionally swing even with no target (player often runs around attacking; they should too).
6. **Attack thorns in path** (needs clarity: destructible thorn/obstacle tiles vs thorn enemies — assume they should clear hazard tiles / strike thorny obstacles they pass).
7. **Path deviation:** per-soldier route jitter so they don't all take the identical path to a shared destination; occasional idle hops/repositioning for liveliness.
8. **Targeting — training dummies:** soldiers ignore the LuiAFK super dummy (and likely the vanilla Target Dummy). Add dummies to the allowed-target set so players can DPS-test their army.
9. **Manpower sustain:** triple the manpower RESERVE pool but keep the simultaneous field cap dictated by command points — militia currently run out and die before an invasion ends. (Fits tenet #1: more sustain, enemy stays the threat; no field-cap change.) Small constant change — fold into a merge, owner: RosterSystem/LevyConstants.Roster.
10. **Class-aware weapon issue (HIGH — reported bug):** auto-issue currently picks the single highest-DPS weapon in the Armory ignoring damage class (`WeaponClassifier.Score` = damage×shots÷useTime, no class term), so a soldier in Fossil/ranged armor grabs a magic staff if it out-scores the guns. Fix: in `IssueGear`, equip armor FIRST, read which DamageClass the soldier's equipped armor boosts most via the dummy stat pipeline (works for modded armor), then bias weapon selection toward that class. Turns "what armor you stock" into the soldier's doctrine. Per-unit menu overrides it. Files: `WeaponClassifier.cs` + `ArmoryTE.cs` (both locked by the in-flight weapon-fidelity + setup-friction builds — do this right after they merge).
11. **Armor coverage (likely NOT a bug):** equip path verified correct (armor drawn from issue rows + storage, applied to dummy for defense + sprite). "Most units bare" is most likely (a) supply < demand — each soldier eats a full set — or (b) armor stocked after muster; the new Re-arm order re-draws it. Confirm after class-aware fix; if still bare with plenty of sets + Re-arm, chase as a real bug.
12. **Bosses don't call out the garrison (CONFIRMED gap — include in the combined package):** `SettlementSystem.PostUpdateInvasions` auto-musters only when `LevyUtil.IsEventActive()` (invasions, blood/eclipse/pumpkin/frost moons, DD2). Bosses are excluded, so militia sit in the Barracks while a boss kills them (observed: King Slime). Fix: auto-mobilize when a boss NPC is alive (optionally near the settlement) and `AutoMuster` is on; stand down when the boss dies and no event is active. Files: `SettlementSystem.cs` + a `LevyUtil.IsBossActive()`/nearby helper (neither locked by the in-flight builds). Manual workaround for now: blow the War Horn to mobilize before a boss.
13. **Boss fear/rout tuning (watch):** soldiers DID rout vs King Slime even with hazard pay. Partly intended (bosses terrifying), but once they actually deploy + get the no-suicide/spread orders, re-evaluate boss fear magnitude so a boss is a hard fight, not an instant rout.
14. **Engagement failure vs mobile/gap-crossing bosses (HIGH — diagnosed from video):** militia WERE mobilized but stopped fighting King Slime. Cause: a vertical mineshaft pit sat between the troops and the slime; King Slime bounced across it repeatedly, ground units couldn't path across the gap, got stranded on the wrong side, and DPS cratered (~150 → ~20). Brain of Cthulhu (aerial, hovers) they shredded — so stationary/aerial bosses are fine, mobile ground bosses that cross terrain are not. This is the same root as "struggle around 1-block gaps." Needs (part of the Wave 2 AI pass): (a) engagement repositioning — move to a tile where the target is actually reachable/shootable instead of standing idle; (b) gap/pit traversal (jump across, or route around); (c) ranged units hold a firing line and keep shooting when LoS exists rather than chasing into the gap. Pairs with no-suicide standoff + spread-out + search-and-destroy. NOT the boss-mobilize gap (#12) — they were already out.
**SHIPPED v0.4.0 (commit 2475d43) — #15, #16, #17:**
15. Aerial engagement: soldiers with wings/extra-jumps/rocket-boots climb to an airborne target's altitude and hover to fire (jump→extra-jumps→wings on a recharging budget→boots), glide down when done; grounded soldiers unchanged. Hand-rolled flight (not vanilla wing physics) — feel needs tuning; can hover over lava and fall in (no landing-spot search yet).
16. Tactical positioning: `TryPerch` scores ledge/platform/elevation/LoS/travel and relocates ranged soldiers to high ground (PerchMinImprovement=4); dispersion via stable golden-ratio per-soldier slots (±6 tiles lateral, altitude spread) + a crowd-nudge so they fan out instead of stacking. Yields to standoff/retreat/aerial. Likely tuning knobs: PerchMinImprovement, DispersionCrowdPenalty.
17. Nameplate force-toggle: 8th War Horn radial button "Show names / Hide names" overrides the crowd auto-hide. Watch: radial now 8 buttons — check ellipse spacing.

**All v0.4.0 AI numbers are first-guess named constants — a tuning pass is expected once playtested.**

**SHIPPED v0.5.0 (commit e2abc66) — #3 combat orders:** Spread Out (squad toggle, ~2.5x formation + idle spread), Search & Destroy (SoldierOrder=4, roam on a 120-tile leash, hunt smart, standoff respected), Focus Target (radial → click enemy → squad focus-fires, commander-scoped, clears on death). Radial auto-sizes to 11 buttons. All first-guess constants (SpreadOutMult 2.5, SearchLeashTiles 120, SearchAcquireRangeMult 1.5, FocusRangeMult 1.25).

**SHIPPED v0.5.1 (commit 376d2a6):** Free Rein order (SoldierOrder.FreeRein=5) — fully autonomous, unleashed. Roams/fights across the area on its own AI, anchored to nearest settlement (never the commander) with a 600-tile outer safety circle; scouts toward enemies within 220 tiles; keeps all smart-fight rules. Radial now 12 buttons (auto-sized). Design note: leashing to the player was getting soldiers killed — this is the designer's preferred "let them handle it" mode. Open Q: Free Rein scouts toward ANY enemy incl. bosses within 220 tiles — ask designer if it should ignore bosses.

**SHIPPED v0.5.2 (commit 838fe36) — CRITICAL gear-loss fix (confirmed bug):** The Armory was silently destroying gear. Root cause: 120-slot storage (40 armor sets = 120 non-stacking pieces → always full) + militia stand-down `ReturnGear` spilling overflow onto the ground where it despawned. Fix: storage 120→1000 (scrollable grid), spill protected from the world-item recycler, and a stocked Armory can't be mined (chest rule — Loot All first). Note: previously-despawned gear is unrecoverable; only NEW loss is stopped. Downgrade caveat: a world saved at 1000 then loaded on an old build truncates to 120.

**SHIPPED v0.5.3 (e453c02):** safe spawn placement (don't materialize into contact) + 45-tick spawn grace + melee no-suicide (won't charge bosses/lethal-contact) + unarmed soldiers fall back instead of charging + **Armory link radius 20→120** (armories no longer must be glued to the Barracks; a common cause of unarmed musters).
**SHIPPED v0.5.4 (b5c1fd9):** boss call-out scoped to a boss within 250 tiles of a player — fixes permanent muster (ambient/modded "boss" NPC was keeping every town mustered forever → crowds of idle, often unarmed militia that never stood down). Diagnosed from a clip (Manpower 75/75, auto-muster on, daytime, no enemies).

**SHIPPED v0.5.5 (ce027ae):** MilitiaFieldCap config (default 10) — small squad fielded at once, manpower stays the deep reserve. Stops the whole 75-pool spawning/dying at once.
**SHIPPED v0.6.0 (d95c35b):** Armory Sort button; wide weapon compatibility (any ammo type incl. rockets/darts/gel/flamethrower/stakes/snowballs/modded, mana+shoot casters, swing/thrust melee of any class, plain shooters — all via the real shoot pipeline; rejects summon/yoyo/flail/boomerang/whip/channeled/tools/Coin Gun; dud-weapon safety so nothing crashes); squad weapon variety (top ~75% of viable types, stable per-soldier → mixed arsenal); tier-aware ranking (armor `rarity*350+def*100+log10(value)*40`, weapon `DPS*(1+0.06*tier+...)` → Crimtane over Platinum). WATCH (untested in play): flamethrower reach, rocket launchers vs bosses, no ammo-save odds for Megashark-likes, plain-shooters reuse Magic family at 0 mana.

**SHIPPED v0.7.0 (92c06bf) — EXPERIMENTAL summoner soldiers (Path B: per-soldier minions):** 3 new ModProjectiles (SoldierMinion/Whip/Boomerang) all owned by Main.myPlayer + anchored to their soldier via ai[0]. Minions orbit their soldier, dive on hostiles, draw the real summon weapon's sprite, count = dummy.maxMinions/slots (1-6); summon staff is passive (soldier fights with whip sidearm). Whips tag (240t); any soldier's minions +50% vs tagged + prefer them. Boomerangs return to the thrower. Doctrine: summoner armor → summon primary + whip sidearm. Classifier adds Summon/Whip/Boomerang (21/9/16 vanilla). Cleanup on death confirmed. Agent ran a headless sim (flocks held, tag bonus 1450 vs 516). KNOWN ROUGH (untested in a real client): minion/boomerang SPRITES unseen (may be flipped/odd framing), whip lash is plain geometry not the item sprite, all balance/ranges/orbit are first-guess, no summon mana cost, whip users inherit melee boss-avoidance, some boomerang-aiStyle non-boomerangs (Paladin's Hammer/Anchor) misclassified, existing soldiers with a blade sidearm aren't re-issued a whip. EXPECT A TUNING/BUGFIX ROUND.

**SHIPPED v0.8.0 (minion trail + ranged minions, weapon on-hit AUDIT, mana potions, harpoon fix):**
- **Minions TRAIL instead of orbit** — fanned-out slot formation behind/above the soldier (vanilla minionPos feel) + gentle bob, keep dive/recoil/leash. (SoldierMinion.cs, LevyConstants.Summon.cs)
- **RANGED minions now SHOOT, don't ram** — curated table keyed on the mimicked minion type (ai[1]): Hornet(stinger), Imp(fireball), Pygmy(spear), Tempest(sharkron), Xeno(UFO laser), Optic(retina laser) hover at standoff + fire the real projectile (tagged, +50% vs tagged), lead via SolveIntercept. Melee minions (spider/frog/raven/Spazmamini-dash) stay melee. IDs verified against decompiled AI_062/066/054. (SoldierMinion.cs)
- **Accessory-summoned projectiles** — partial: Volatile Gelatin + Spore Sac anchored to the soldier (they don't owner-home). Bone Helm is NOT a summon accessory (just a helmet). Sentry/pet-that-homes-to-player class left out (would need per-frame hacks). Safe-scaffold, extensible. (new SoldierNPC.AccessoryProc.cs + 1 call in Combat.cs)
- **Weapon on-hit AUDIT (the big one) — see levy-mod/Levy/WEAPON_AUDIT.md:**
  - Broadsword hits now run the REAL hook chain via the dummy (ModifyPlayerHitNPCWithItem → StatusToNPC → one StrikeNPC → OnPlayerHitNPCWithItem), damage applied EXACTLY once. So MODDED sword on-hit procs fire, AND dummy accessory melee effects (Magma Stone/Fire Gauntlet/Frostburn) + item debuffs (Fiery Greatsword fire, Blade of Grass poison, Bee Keeper confuse) now apply. (new MeleeEffects.cs, Combat.cs)
  - Vanilla hardcoded melee specials ported: Bee Keeper bees (1-3 +Hive Pack), Fiery Greatsword blast(978), Muramasa arc, Bladetongue ichor, Blood Butcherer/Tentacle Spike barbs (max 5), Bat Bat 1HP leech; once-per-swing via SwingSpecialSpent flag. Boomerang debuffs (Flamarang/Ice/Thorn Chakram) re-added in SoldierBoomerang.OnHitNPC. (MeleeEffects.cs, LevyConstants.MeleeFx.cs, SoldierBoomerang.cs)
  - **BONUS bug found: the whole ItemCheck_Shoot per-item ai[] chain was unported** — only Daedalus/Blood Rain existed, so Starfury/Light's Bane/Blade of Grass/Hellwing(bats)/Bee Gun/Crimson+Nimbus Rod(flew to world origin!)/Ice Rod all fired as broken/dead projectiles. Ported the prehardmode ones + HM bullet swaps (Sniper/Venus/Uzi). (new ItemShots.cs, WeaponUse.cs)
  - **Night's Edge family** (Night's Edge/Excalibur/True*/Terra Blade/Horseman's) were spawning a held-slash bound to the COMMANDER → reclassified as broadswords (hitbox does the damage). Flying wave / Terra beam NOT reproduced (category D — would need a soldier-anchored custom projectile; possible future).
- **HARPOON FIX** — Harpoon (and aiStyle-13 tethered-to-owner: Chain Knife/Golem Fist/Boxing Glove, +aiStyle-7 hooks) classified Unsupported. They tether to the commander + lock one-per-owner, so a soldier's harpoon fired from the player's chest and froze the player's weapon. Bug fix, not a nerf. (WeaponClassifier.cs)
- **MANA POTIONS** — the armory potion slot was health-only (healLife>0), so mana potions had no home. Slot 0 now accepts+dispenses mana restoratives too (label "healing / mana"); magic/summon soldiers sip one below 30% mana, need-aware. (ArmoryTE.cs, ArmoryPanel.cs, SoldierNPC.Damage.cs, LevyConstants.cs, UI2.hjson)
- **Clarified for the designer:** NO mana economy by design (tenet #1) — mana regens, Mana Crystals raise the pool, potions are optional top-up. Healing already worked but is SLOT-GATED (potion must sit in the issue slot). The "recovery speed 73/5.2" the designer saw is the PLAYER's HUD stat, not the soldiers'.
- **KNOWN ROUGH / TEST FIRST (untested in a real client):** minion trail spacing + ranged standoff ranges are first-guess; the melee on-hit routing + vanilla ports are code-verified only (hand-test Volcano/Fiery Greatsword, Bee Keeper, a modded sword, Starfury, Hellwing, Crimson Rod, Night's Edge swinging, Harpoon absent from armory, mana-potion sip). Ice Rod places real Ice Block tiles near enemies (vanilla behavior — may look odd). Night's Edge/Terra lost their wave/beam.

Swing hitbox still does melee damage, so beam is ON TOP (double-hit in melee range — intentional per tenet, dial via SwordBeams table damageMult). Base Night's Edge & Excalibur never had a free-flying ranged beam at all (pure melee). So they stay plain big-swing broadswords. Verified in /home/user/tml-src.

Awaiting the designer's call on whether + what look. Designer is art-sensitive (WRONG-list item #3), so DON'T ship a beam look unilaterally.

MINION targeting gap fixed: minions now snap to the soldier's focus/target every tick (was only a weak 0.71x distance bias). **DISCUSSED, awaiting designer calls (next wave):**
- **Militia reserve size** — keep 15/NPC (deep reserve, small field) or dial down? (asked)
- **Command Radio** — proposed: craftable (iron+wood+Mechanic wire) held item that opens the orders radial and applies to ALL units map-wide (no War Horn range). Awaiting green light on recipe/scope.

**SHIPPED v0.8.3 (CRITICAL gear-loss fix):**
- **Militia death deleted the dead soldier's entire kit.** `SettlementSystem.OnMilitiaBodyDied` removed the record + counted the loss but NEVER returned the gear; in a softcore world nothing dropped it either, so the kit (drawn from the armory) evaporated. Over a long fight waves of militia muster (pull stored sets into hand) and die, each death silently deleting a full set → armory drains to empty. (Designer lost dozens of Necro sets to a hardmode goblin army.) FIX: OnMilitiaBodyDied now calls ReturnGearHome(b, rec) on the genuine removal (idempotent: hardcore/mediumcore OnBodyDied already dropped+cleared the gear, so it no-ops there; softcore recovers the kit to the armory). Also recovers via HomePos when no barracks is found. This is distinct from the v0.5.2 overflow-spill fix — that path was fine; this was the death path. NOTE: already-lost gear is unrecoverable.

**SHIPPED v0.8.4 (Spoils — designer confirmed "toggleable setting" = the Spoils feature):**
- Config toggle `Spoils` (Economy section, DEFAULT ON) — "Soldiers loot coins": coins dropped near a living soldier body go into that soldier's home barracks Treasury (self-funds wages; mercenaries loot the battlefield). Server/single-player sweep from RosterSystem.PostUpdateNPCs every SpoilsIntervalTicks=20; SpoilsRadiusPx=80 (~5 tiles). Player-coin guard: skips any coin within an active player's GetItemGrabRange (incl. magnet/Gold Ring) and coins with noGrabDelay>0, so it never snatches the player's coins. Value via EconomySystem.CoinValue, removed with TurnToAir + SyncItem (MP), one throttled CoinPickup sound per sweep, no chat spam. Files: LevyConfig.cs, LevyConstants.Roster.cs, RosterSystem.cs, en-US.hjson.

**SHIPPED v0.8.5 (Command Radio + pylon armory-sharing; designer green-lit both, wants Magic Storage as its own part next):**
- **Command Radio** — craftable item (8 Iron/Lead Bar + 10 Wood + 5 Wire at a Work Bench, Green rarity). Carrying it flips the orders radial into MAP-WIDE mode: orders go out with atBarracks:true (bypasses OrderRadius → reaches all the commander's regulars anywhere), and left/right-click opens the radial anywhere (UISystem.ToggleRadial). War Horn + normal radial unchanged for players without a radio. No new packets (Order packet already carries atBarracks). Files: CommandRadio.cs/.png (new), OrdersRadial.cs, en-US.hjson.
- **Pylon armory-sharing (automatic, no toggle)** — armories within PylonShareRadius=60 tiles of a vanilla pylon (Main.PylonSystem.Pylons / TeleportPylonInfo.PositionInTiles) share stored gear ON THE ISSUE SIDE: a soldier mustering at base B can be armed from gear stored at base A. Home issue rows + storage first (unchanged priority), then spill into networked armories' Storage only. REVIEWED for loss/dupe (history: v0.8.3 deletion bug was here): single removal path (src.TakeSlot → src.Remove swaps real slot + marks that armory dirty → its Update syncs next tick), ReferenceEquals re-check before every armor-set take, each armory honors its OWN re-arm reservations, empty pool on MP client (server owns moves), identical to before when no pylons. Returns/deposits/potions/crystals NOT pooled (home only) — by design. Files: ArmoryTE.cs, SettlementSystem.cs (NetworkedArmories/NearPylon), LevyConstants.cs.
- Militia reserve LEFT at 15/NPC (designer didn't flag it).

**SHIPPED v0.8.6 (Magic Storage integration — the "special part"):**
- New `Common/Integrations/MagicStorageBridge.cs`: weak cross-mod (ModLoader.TryGetMod), ALL reflection, built against MS 1.4.4 v0.7.1 source (agent cloned it, not from memory). Armory within MagicStorageRadius=50 tiles of a Storage Heart/Access/Remote Access draws from that MS network when ISSUING — slots in LAST, after the armory's own + pylon-networked armories. Covers TakeBest (armor/accessory/sidearm/whip), TakeVaried (weapons), TakeArmorSet, TakeAmmo, AmmoIdsOnHand. Config toggle `MagicStorageSupply` (default true, no-op when MS absent). MS members: TEStorageHeart.GetStoredItems()/Withdraw(Item,bool)/TryWithdraw; TEStorageComponent.GetHeart(); withdraw matched defensively by shape. LOSS/DUPE-SAFE & FAIL-SAFE: equips ONLY what a withdraw actually returned (got set only when non-air), ammo surplus dropped not destroyed, favorites protected; any reflection miss/throw → logs once, disables MS supply for the session, Levy behaves as before; Enabled early-outs on cached static bools (zero hot-path cost when MS absent); server/single-player only. Files: MagicStorageBridge.cs (new), ArmoryTE.cs, LevyConfig.cs, en-US.hjson, LevyConstants.cs.
- ⚠️ **UNTESTED MS-PRESENT PATH:** MS isn't installed in the verify env, so only the MS-ABSENT load is confirmed (clean, bridge dormant). The actual "pull gear from the MS network" behavior NEEDS the designer to test in a world with Magic Storage.

**SHIPPED v0.8.8 (Mechanica summon-weapon compatibility — minions FIRE, verified live):**
- Designer clarified: Levy's summoner system is fine (flocks follow their own soldier); the gap was that Mechanica's shooter minions weren't in Levy's ranged-minion table, so the stand-in RAMMED instead of firing (the "mounted machine gun deals contact damage" bug). NOT a rework.
- FIX: made SoldierMinion.RangedMinions runtime-extensible (RegisterRangedMinion + raw-field overload + ClearRegisteredRangedMinions; vanilla entries untouched, writes locked, reads lock-free). New weak-ref `Common/Integrations/MechanicaMinionBridge.cs` (+ its own ModSystem) resolves Mechanica's shooter minions by name (mech.TryFind<ModProjectile>) at PostSetupContent and registers {minion -> bullet, cadence, speed, standoff, lead} read from the decompiled AIs. No compile-time Mechanica ref; no-op when absent.
- Registered ~18 (incl. Converted variants): MountedMachinegun, SmolBetsy, Laser/Missile/Mini-helicopter/Temple-heli/RoboDog/WarHeli drones, Curse/Ichor syringes (vanilla CursedFlame/IchorSplash). Skipped pure-melee (Piranha/JungleSnake/CoolShark/etc.) and sentries (no framework).
- LIVE VERIFIED: booted Levy+Mechanica, reflection diag confirmed RangedMinions=27 and MountedMachinegunMinion(1192)/MissileDrone/WarHeli/SmolBetsy all registered=True. Diagnostic removed; Levy-only boot clean.
- NOTE/earlier misreads (corrected): I first wrongly "fixed" summoner soldier POSITIONING (reverted, never shipped) before the designer clarified it's a minion-firing compat gap, not AI. Lesson re-logged: ask, don't assume.
- WORKFLOW NOTE (designer reminder): all feature coding goes to Sonnet subagents in worktrees; keep it that way.

**SHIPPED v0.8.7 (Mechanica / channeled modded weapon compatibility — VERIFIED LIVE against the real mod):**
- ROOT CAUSE (found by decompiling the uploaded Mechanica.tmod with ilspy): all 53 "converted" weapons set `item.channel = true`, and Levy's classifier had a blanket `if (item.channel) return Unsupported`. Also `BaseConvertedWeaponItem.Shoot` returns `player.controlUseItem`, which a soldier's dummy never set → would fire nothing.
- FIX (WeaponClassifier.cs + WeaponUse.cs): (1) removed the blanket channel reject; (2) held-beam guard — channel weapons whose shoot projectile is aiStyle 75 HeldProjectile / 84 ThickLaser stay Unsupported (so held beams don't fire from the commander); (3) modded-no-standard-class exclusion — a modded item (type>=ItemID.Count) that counts as none of Ranged/Magic/Melee/Summon/Throwing is Unsupported, which auto-excludes Mechanica's MechanicDamage (Generic-only) power weapons WITHOUT any Mechanica reference; vanilla items exempt via the carve-out; (4) TryRealShoot sets dummy.controlUseItem=true around CombinedHooks.Shoot so controlUseItem-gated weapons fire (restored in finally).
- **LIVE VERIFICATION** (booted a headless server with Levy + Mechanica v1.1.3, temporary MechDiag ModSystem classified every Mechanica weapon, then removed it): 92 supported / 23 unsupported. ALL ~70 Converted ranged/magic/melee weapons → SUPPORTED. The 23 excluded = LaserIris (original held-beam, guard caught it) + 22 Converted SENTRIES (Levy has no sentry framework — correctly excluded as "not added compatibility for"). Both mods load clean together; Levy-only boot clean after removing the diagnostic.
- NOTE: converted MELEE weapons that shoot a projectile classify into the Magic/caster firing bucket (they fire their projectile at range with their real melee-class damage) — fine, that's how the weapon attacks.
- STILL NOT DONE (by design / designer scope): Machinist/power weapons (excluded), sentries (no framework), maces/yoyos/flails (never added). MountedMachinegun (original summon weapon, not converted) "summon + bash" behavior — designer never answered the clarifying question on exact behavior; left as a possible separate movement task.

**(resolved) Mechanica mod compatibility — was: AWAITING UPLOAD / INVESTIGATION:**
- Designer is uploading `Mechanica.tmod` (Machinist-class mod, 机械之心, Steam workshop 3772635584) for inspection. Symptom: Mechanica weapons — EVEN converted-to-plain ranger/magic ones — REFUSE TO EQUIP on soldiers (classifier marks them Unsupported). Designer confirmed the converted forms use NO power resource, so it's not a Power gate. Candidate causes in WeaponClassifier.Classify: item.channel==true (line 81, continuous-fire guns), item.shoot==0 (custom-fire weapons), unsampleable modded projectile (plain-shooter path line 141 needs sample!=null), or a custom damage class that isn't vanilla Ranged/Magic. NEED the .tmod to see a real weapon def before fixing — each cause has a different fix; don't guess blind post-gear-loss. Likely fix benefits ALL modded weapons, not just Mechanica. Plan if a bridge is needed: same weak/reflection-guarded pattern as MagicStorageBridge.
- **BUG (investigate with the .tmod):** "the mounted machinegun — soldiers that use summon weapons will have their machine guns just bash enemies instead of shooting." A machine gun being BASHED = classified as a melee family (or an unarmed/contact fallback) instead of Gun. Could be the modded machine gun's definition (custom damage class / useStyle) OR a Levy summoner-sidearm logic bug (summon primary → sidearm should FIRE, not swing). Check the weapon def once uploaded; if it's a stock/vanilla-style gun being bashed, it's a Levy-side classification/sidearm bug to fix regardless of Mechanica.

**SOMEDAY / BANKED IDEAS (designer, not now):**
- **Thorium compatibility — Healer-class soldiers.** Designer wants to eventually play a Healer with soldier support (Thorium's Healer uses radiant weapons that heal allies). Musing only — hasn't played Thorium yet, said "maybe not yet." Cool fit for Levy (soldiers that heal the commander + each other). Bank it; don't build until the designer commits. Would need: classify Thorium radiant/healer weapons, soldiers target ALLIES for healing weapons (new targeting mode), weak-ref like the other mod bridges.

**SHIPPED — OVERNIGHT AI BATCH (all 10 items done autonomously while the designer slept; v0.9.0→v0.9.2; each wave compile+headless-boot verified). ALL code-verified only — NO live playtest (can't test combat feel / client UI headlessly) → designer must playtest.**
VERSION MAP: v0.9.0 Wave A (combat/weapons #2,#7,#4,#9 + targeting/aggro #6,#3-targeting + reflected-shot redirect). v0.9.1 Wave B (brain/movement #1,#5a,#8,#3-soldier-side). v0.9.2 Wave C (#5b Focus Attack order) + Wave D (#10 station buffs). v0.9.3 FOCUS ATTACK CONSOLIDATION (designer correction: "turning focus target into focus attack so you could have your units attack neutral units or just a position"). The two separate orders (Focus Target + Focus Attack) are now ONE order "Focus attack": one radial button (back to 12), one click-to-designate. Click a hostile → lock it (old focus-fire-until-dead). Click a critter/neutral or bare ground → attack that spot + anything in the focus radius (old position path). Both server mechanisms (_focusTargets NPC-lock, _focusPositions area) kept and mutually exclusive; only the client UI/designation merged. Critters are swept by the AREA (not NPC-tracked) — a static spot that shreds a herd/town, NOT lock-and-chase one moving critter (deferred unless designer asks). UI-only change, compile+headless-boot verified. v0.9.4 CLEAR-FOCUS TOGGLE (designer: "how do you stop them firing at a position?" — the merge dropped the old clear button). Radial button 7 flips to "Clear focus" whenever a focus stands (locked NPC or position); one click calls the squad off. Authoritative in SP via RosterSystem.HasFocus; client mirror UISystem.FocusActive for MP; ClearFocus() sends both clearing packets. (Before this, the ONLY way to drop a position was re-clicking the same spot within 2 tiles — no order cleared it.) v0.9.5 FLYING KNIFE FIX (designer video: soldier-fired Flying Knife hovered at the commander's dead cursor). Root cause: ProjectileID.FlyingKnife steers toward Main.player[owner]'s mouse/channel, and soldier shots are owned by the COMMANDER for kill-credit → it flew to the commander's cursor, not the enemy. Fix is entirely in SoldierProjectileTag.PreAI (new override, returns true/vanilla for player's own knives): soldier-owned Flying Knife gets custom flight — SEEK phase homes on the soldier's TargetIndex (fallback: nearest hostile within 50 tiles of the knife), orbits/jabs ~75 ticks, then RETURN phase flies back to the soldier and Kills within 20px; tileCollide off, spins, hard 180-tick lifetime cap, 20-tick orphan grace if the soldier dies. Constants in new LevyConstants.FlyingKnife.cs. Classification unchanged (plain Magic-path shooter, ~40-tile throw standoff — may tune to shorter if it feels sniper-y in play). NOTE: other owner-steered controllable weapons (if any surface) would need the same PreAI treatment; only Flying Knife handled. v0.9.6 CRITICAL DATA-LOSS FIX — militia gear deleted on teardown (designer: "made 20 necro sets, only 10 left... units eating inventory... does this happen when a player leaves the game?"). ROOT CAUSE: militia draw REAL gear from armory storage at muster (IssueGear), and on several teardown paths the kit was SILENTLY CLEARED (deleted) instead of returned/dropped. FIVE spots fixed, all routed through ReturnGearHome which now NEVER deletes: (1) ReturnGearHome no-armory case now DROPS the kit on the ground (body pos, else HomePos) instead of Clear()-to-nothing [the "removed the barracks+armory" case]; (2) new PreSaveAndQuit() returns every mustered/standing/pending militiaman's kit to armories before the quit-save [the "left the game" case]; (3) StandDownTick null-body; (4) StandDown null-body; (5) Housekeeping !HasBody — all now ReturnGearHome before removing the record. Only SettlementSystem.cs changed. RESIDUAL EDGE (not fixed, flagged to designer): a hard crash while militia are actively mustered between autosaves could still lose out-gear (gear isn't in the TE save; only PreSaveAndQuit + stand-down/death return it). Already-lost items are unrecoverable (deleted from save). FLYING KNIFE REWORKED in v0.9.8 (designer confirmed the vanilla-faithful interpretation). The soldier throws ONE knife; it hovers/jabs the soldier's current target (read from TargetIndex every tick) with NO timeout as long as the soldier is "engaging" (State Engage/Shaken/Routed && !HoldFire — same test as Combat); re-targets on switch (30-tick lose-target grace so it doesn't bail on a 1-frame gap); returns to the soldier and dies only when it disengages (hold fire, state leaves fighting, no target past grace, or body gone→orphan coast). ONE AT A TIME: FireMagic gates a second throw via SoldierProjectileTag.SoldierHasLiveKnife(whoAmI)→Dud while a knife is live. Single-loop AI (dropped the ai[0] seek/return phases). Constants: removed KnifeSeekTicks/KnifeMaxLifeTicks/KnifeFallbackSearchTiles; added KnifeLoseTargetGraceTicks=30, KnifeSafetyMaxTicks=10800 (3-min defensive backstop only). Files: SoldierProjectileTag.cs, WeaponUse.cs, LevyConstants.FlyingKnife.cs. NOTE v0.9.5→v0.9.8 history: v0.9.5 was the first (wrong) seek-then-return + throw-every-useTime version; I also twice misdiagnosed the root cause (claimed mouse-follow; actual vanilla idle = returns-to-owner=commander). POSSIBLE TUNE if designer dislikes the feel: one persistent blade grinding a target changes effective DPS vs a volley — adjust KnifeSeekAccel/MaxSpeed (orbit tightness) or damage, not a rebuild. v0.9.7 VANILLA HP PROGRESSION (designer: "why are they not using more life crystals?" — Levy capped at 12 crystals/340). Removed ALL grade-based HP gating (was Levy 12cr/400cap, MaA 18/500, Champion 20/600). Now flat vanilla for every grade: BaseHp 100, +20/Life Crystal up to MaxLifeCrystals=15 (→400), then +5/Life Fruit up to MaxLifeFruit=20 (→500), hard cap MaxSoldierHp=500; Life Fruit gated on crystals maxed first (LifeCrystals>=15), exactly like vanilla. Removed GradeHpCap[], MaxLifeCrystalsByGrade[] and the per-grade crystal consts. Touched LevyConstants.cs, LevyConstants.Roster.cs, SoldierRecord.MaxHp, RosterSystem ApplyCrystal+CanAbsorbCrystal. Existing saves need no migration (a 12-crystal/340 soldier just gains headroom to 15/400 then fruit). Grade still matters for CP cost / wages / gear-issue tier / unlocks — just not HP.
PLAYTEST / JUDGMENT CALLS TO CONFIRM: (a) melee soldiers avoid ALL mimics incl. awake ones (safe but melee-only squads won't kill mimics) — reversible; (b) Focus Attack fires at a bare spot at full ammo rate (no throttle, per tenet) — melee/thrust/whip/boomerang/minion still won't hit CRITTERS (only gun/bow/magic/swing do), bare-spot firing is ranged-only, bullets don't break blocks (only tile-breaking projectiles do); (c) station buffs: Honey/candles/gnome skipped (no soldier effect), War Table maxTurrets inert (no sentries), Sharpened approximated as +6 flat melee; (d) radial is back to 12 buttons (v0.9.3 merged Focus Target + Focus Attack into one "Focus attack" button) — client UI UNTESTED headlessly, check the wheel renders and the unified click-to-designate works (hostile=lock, critter/ground=area); (e) accuracy/engage-distance/standoff numbers are first-guess tuning. ORIGINAL ITEM LIST + judgment calls below.
**(the AI is "already incredibly smart" → polish not overhaul; was: CONSERVATIVE, don't regress, named constants.)**
1. Engage enemies behind COVER: ranged soldier with no line of sight REPOSITIONS for an angle (high ground/flank), does NOT run at the enemy, then keeps shooting. (brain/movement)
2. Projectile-melee weapons (Ice Sickle, Enchanted Sword, beam swords) SWING/FIRE their projectile at RANGE constantly, not only in melee; still melee when close. (classifier profile + combat)
3. Biome mimics: soldiers walk into disguised mimics & die on the ambush; reflected shots go to the PLAYER; mimics ignore units & chase the player. FIX: mimics target soldiers; soldiers treat mimics as lethal-contact (no suicide-walk). Reflection-to-player may be inherent (soldier shots owned by commander) → note if not fixable.
4. Long-range ACCURACY: slightly better past a distance threshold, close range ~unchanged. (Aiming/WeaponUse)
5a. More HIGH GROUND, don't crowd one doorway. (brain/movement)
5b. NEW ORDER "Focus Attack": designate a POSITION/block/critter; units attack toward/around it. (Enums/OrdersRadial/UISystem/Packets/Brain)
6. AGGRO accessories apply to soldiers: high-aggro soldiers draw enemies (Player.aggro weighting in targeting); low-aggro player → soldiers preferred; prefer ISSUING taunt accessories. (targeting + ArmoryTE)
7. Melee units more aggressive: swing as an enemy approaches, even slightly out of range. (combat)
8. Any PROJECTILE-weapon unit (incl. melee-that-shoots) engages from FURTHER. (profile RangeTiles + brain)
9. BANANARANG throws only 1, should throw up to 5. (SoldierBoomerang/WeaponUse per-weapon max-out)
10. STATION BUFFS: soldiers get the same proximity/placed-station buffs players do (campfire, heart lantern, ammo box, bewitching table, sharpening station, crystal ball, sunflower, water/peace candle, honey, star-in-bottle, garden gnome, slice of cake/war table, etc.). Designer: "use these automatically if they're beside an armory" → scan tiles near each ARMORY for buff stations (one cached scan per armory, cheap), apply the resulting FLAGS (campfire/heartLamp/ammoBox…) + BUFFS (Bewitched +1 minion, Clairvoyance, Well Fed…) to soldiers in range. On-use stations (bewitching table etc.) treated as proximity (auto-applied if near the armory) since soldiers can't right-click. Effects must actually land: set the dummy's station flags + buffs so the stat/regen/ammo/minion pipeline grants them (regen via Damage.cs, ammo conservation via WeaponUse, minion slot via Summon/maxMinions, stat buffs via dummy). Touches SoldierNPC.cs/Damage/WeaponUse → run AFTER Wave A so it doesn't conflict.

WAVES (Brain.cs is the serialization point): A (PARALLEL, no Brain) = A1 Weapons/Combat (#2,#7,#4,#9) + A2 Targeting/aggro (#6 + targeting half of #3). B (Brain/Movement) = #1,#5a,#8,#3 soldier-side. C = #5b Focus Attack. D = #10 station buffs (after A frees WeaponUse/SoldierNPC.cs). Each wave: compile + headless boot before merge; ship v0.9.x incrementally w/ changelog. Behavior changes code-verified only (no headless combat-feel test) → designer playtest needed. JUDGMENT CALLS (asleep): projectile-melee=hybrid; accuracy=gentle; Focus Attack=point+converge+fire, critters/tiles valid; aggro issue pref mild; mimic reflection likely deferred.

## LEVY — MOBILIZE THE TOWN / NPC OFFICERS (foundation shipped on `worktree-levy-officers`, unplayed)
- **Built:** the officer skeleton + ONE proof officer (the Nurse). A commission lives ON the town NPC (`OfficerGlobalNPC`: commander, slot, paying barracks, unpaid days; saved with the NPC, synced via extra-AI), so a dead NPC takes it with it and a respawn is a fresh civilian. `OfficerSystem` owns the rules (`CanCommission`, `Commission`, `Dismiss`, dawn payroll from the commissioning barracks, lapse after 2 unpaid dawns, `MaxSlots = 3`). `OfficerBrain` replaces the vanilla town AI while on duty (follow the commander, shoot with the stock town attack, retreat when hurt, catch-up teleport). Enemies target officers like soldiers via the new `ITargetBody` (SoldierNPC and `OfficerBody` both implement it). Menu = `OfficerPanel`, opened from a button on the Barracks panel. Packet `OfficerCommand`.
- **Add the next officer:** write `XOfficer.Create()` (an `OfficerDef`: NpcType, Key, optional `OfficerWeapon`, optional `OfficerKit` subclass for the ability) next to `NurseOfficer.cs`, register it in `OfficerRegistry.RegisterAll()`, add `Officers.Kit.{Key}.Name/Desc` to `en-US_Mods.Levy.Officers.hjson`. Numbers go in `LevyConstants.Officers.cs`.
- **Left out on purpose:** fire-support call-downs, summons-with-health, command-slot progression/tiers (raise `Officer.MaxSlots` or make it a function of boss kills), melee/magic officer weapons (extend `OfficerWeapon`), chasing enemies (officers only fight what comes into reach), per-officer config toggle.
- **Needs a play session:** see PLAYTEST.md section 20.
